===== FILE docs/v12-platform.md sha256=4f55ea9ad50ff240faec439cf743499ebb35d0334f6936ead6261aeb42bc8d66 =====
# MCP Device 1.0.12 platform guide

This source extends the existing MCP Device package. `start` remains the Device-only runtime; `device` is an alias for the existing Device command family. `server` starts the account/OAuth control plane and one mandatory local Device runtime using the same transport-neutral Device Core as remote Devices.

The acceptance record is [Spec 008](../specs/008-v12-platform/ACCEPTANCE.md). A built source tree, a locally tested tarball, npm publication and a production deployment are separate states. This increment does not publish or deploy the package.

## Runtime and state

Node 22.13 or newer is required, including built-in SQLite/FTS5. Acceptance uses the exact Node versions recorded in Spec 008. Native Device handlers retain their SDK v1 dependency; the retained control-plane surface uses SDK v2. The dependency lock is part of the candidate.

```text
mcp-device start
mcp-device device start
mcp-device device status
mcp-device server --runtime-dir <server-state> --host 127.0.0.1 --port 8104
# Trusted-LAN HTTP example with one shared server Skill root:
mcp-device server --runtime-dir <server-state> --host 0.0.0.0 --port 8101 --public-url http://abc:8101 --allow-http-lan --skill-root ~/.mcp-device/skills
mcp-device --help
```

Server state defaults to `~/.mcp-device/server`; legacy Device state defaults remain `~/.mcp-device`. An explicit `--runtime-dir` selects platform command state. `MCP_DEVICE_CONFIG_DIR` is also accepted by platform commands and the native execution configuration; it must be set before native modules load. Legacy Device identity and service configuration retain their existing path rules. Server starts the native modules only after selecting its state root. `--skill-root DIR` selects the single server-global Skill catalog shared by authenticated accounts; when omitted, an existing `~/.mcp-device/skills` directory is preferred for legacy/shared-team compatibility, otherwise the bundled distribution Skills are used. `MCP_SKILL_ROOT` is the service-friendly equivalent.

`--allow-http-lan` is an explicit trusted-LAN opt-in. It requires an explicit `--public-url http://...` and permits that non-loopback HTTP URL to be used as the OAuth issuer/resource base. Without the flag, non-loopback OAuth issuers remain HTTPS-only. This changes transport policy only; account authentication, Device ownership, pairing and cross-account isolation remain unchanged.

TODO: external MCP upstreams are currently server-global. Before relying on upstreams in multi-account deployments, add per-upstream ownership (`owner_account_id`) with private-by-default visibility and explicit sharing. Shared server Skills remain intentionally cross-account.

Server is a foreground process. Stop it with Ctrl-C or the supervising process manager. A Node parent using a private IPC channel may send `{ "type": "mcp-device:shutdown" }` for graceful shutdown on Windows. This is a parent/child process channel, not a network endpoint.

The state root contains identity, pairing/account state, the Gateway SQLite database, plugin registry/private data, a persisted knowledge-reference signing key and bounded activity records. Concurrent server, Device and maintenance processes cannot take the same state lease. Existing Device RuntimeOwner IPC is also checked. An unresponsive legacy control file requires inspection; maintenance does not silently stop another runtime or discard the control state.

The local execution owner does not connect to its own WebSocket or spawn another Device MCP server. A Palace worker process is plugin isolation, not a second Device runtime. Remote Devices continue using their authenticated GatewayDeviceChannel and reconnect/session-epoch contracts.

## Account, OAuth and pairing

Localhost follows the ordinary account, OAuth and Device pairing flows. Anonymous MCP calls are rejected. Create an initial administrator using the existing administrative command, supplying the password through `HCU_ADMIN_PASSWORD` rather than command-line arguments:

```text
mcp-device admin --runtime-dir <server-state> account create-admin <email>
mcp-device admin --runtime-dir <server-state> invite create 1
mcp-device server --runtime-dir <server-state> --host 127.0.0.1 --port 8104
```

The administrator manages invitations and the operator interface. Use an invitation to create an ordinary account at `/signup`; Device pairing and OAuth consent use that ordinary account, preserving the existing role separation.

The server prints one JSON event per line for `device_pairing_required` and `server_ready`. The pairing event contains the public user code and verification URL; it excludes the private device code and PKCE verifier. Open the verification URL, sign in with the ordinary account and claim the local Device. MCP clients then use the standard registration/authorization/consent/token flow. A restored identity and matching server state retain enrollment, accounts and refresh-token continuity.

The operator UI is under `/operator` on the same listener and requires administrator authentication. `/operator/state` uses the same policy. There is no automatic second operator port.

For remote Devices, provide the existing Gateway application TLS configuration through a server JSON config. `--config` accepts an object with an optional `env` object and an optional `configPath` for upstream configuration. `configPath` resolves relative to that JSON file. Configuration names and certificate requirements remain the retained Gateway contracts; incomplete TLS configuration fails startup and closes acquired resources. The isolated localhost test configuration does not provision production trust or bypass remote Device TLS requirements.

```text
mcp-device server --runtime-dir <server-state> --config <server-config.json> --host 127.0.0.1 --port 8104
```

Upstream configuration is selected consistently for both workspace and external MCP manager startup. Explicit options take precedence over the runtime-root configuration and bundled fallback. Existing upstream tools/resources/prompts, cache/reconciliation and collision rules remain in the control-plane adapter.

## Plugin lifecycle

Palace is supplied by the official distribution and initially installed and enabled. The Device Core has no Palace dependency. A disabled, uninstalled, crashed or incompatible provider loses its advertised capabilities while native file/process tools remain available. Uninstall leaves a tombstone, so later starts do not silently reinstall the plugin. Disable and uninstall preserve private data; purge is a separate explicit command for a disabled plugin.

```text
mcp-device plugins --runtime-dir <state> list
mcp-device plugins --runtime-dir <state> disable palace
mcp-device plugins --runtime-dir <state> enable palace
mcp-device plugins --runtime-dir <state> uninstall palace
mcp-device plugins --runtime-dir <state> install <manifest.json> --enable
mcp-device plugins --runtime-dir <state> configure <plugin-id> <settings.json>
mcp-device plugins --runtime-dir <state> upgrade <manifest.json>
mcp-device plugins --runtime-dir <state> upgrade palace --bundled
mcp-device plugins --runtime-dir <state> purge <plugin-id>
```

Configure requires a disabled plugin. Upgrade and purge require stopped runtime ownership so that migration/deletion cannot race a live provider. Upgrade works on a private copy and restores the previous manifest/data if validation or migration fails. An external manifest becomes a user-managed (`manual`) registration and is not reclaimed by the bundled distribution. `--bundled` explicitly restores the distribution source. A version-only distribution update with the same capabilities and data schema is re-pinned without a data migration; capability or schema changes remain gated behind explicit upgrade. Registry writes use revision checks, a tokenized exclusive lock and a stale-lock recovery lock; another runtime sees enabled-capability changes through refresh notifications.

The host is capability-oriented: providers, ordered augmenters, observers and fallback providers compose through versioned contracts. Dependencies resolve to healthy compatible providers and are pinned during an activation. A plugin can invoke its declared dependencies through scoped host services. Dependency loss invalidates dependent providers. Process isolation supports Node workers and external JSONL executables; explicitly trusted cooperative plugins may use the in-process path. See the [plugin contract](../specs/008-v12-platform/plugin-contract.md) for the API, cancellation, frame limits and examples.

Public MCP `capability.list` reports an owned Device's available capabilities and versions. `capability.call` invokes an advertised non-knowledge plugin contract through the normal authenticated Device dispatcher. It does not expose private knowledge import; knowledge exchange uses the three public contracts below.

## Knowledge federation

Knowledge exchange is a platform service. A requesting Device can use it without Palace or any local knowledge database.

| Public tool | Inputs | Effect |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `knowledge.search` | `device_id`, `query`; optional `project_id`, `limit`, `max_bytes`, `provider_device_id` | Select relevant available providers and return compact signed references. No import. |
| `knowledge.list` | `device_id`; optional scope/provider/budget fields | Return compact scoped references. No import. |
| `knowledge.read` | `device_id`, `ref`; optional `project_id`, `evidence`, `max_bytes` | Read one selected card. Lazily import only when the requesting Device supplies the compatible storage capability. |

Requests use authenticated account context supplied by the control plane. The caller cannot select another account through tool arguments. A Device has one current owner account, and the broker rejects ownerless or cross-account dispatch before invoking its Core. References bind the account, selected provider, origin, card version and project scope. Local-only cards do not cross Device boundaries. Provider results carry the authenticated serving Device identity rather than trusting a self-reported identity field. Palace SQLite is Device-local private state and may exist before pairing; a deliberate signed ownership transfer transfers that local state with the Device, while the old account immediately loses dispatch and federation access.

The router prefers local/project-relevant providers and uses a bounded provider count. It does not broadcast to every Device, replicate whole knowledge stores or start background pairwise synchronization. Search/list return previews. Read returns a complete card within the response budget or an explicit budget error; it never truncates a card while keeping a hash for the full content.

For a Device with a storage provider, repeated reads preserve origin, evidence, provenance, version and deduplication identity. For a Device without one, the platform uses a bounded session cache (256 entries, 8 MiB, five-minute TTL). It creates no Palace database on that Device.

Read performs an on-demand origin metadata check when available. An unavailable origin can leave an unchanged cached card explicitly stale. A known correction, withdrawal, scope revocation or version inconsistency must not silently fall back to an older usable card. Correction guards are bounded to the session; this is not a globally synchronized revocation database. Persisted card revisions and later online origin checks remain authoritative.

## Palace CLI, package and Skill lifecycle

The MCP Device package keeps the stable `palace` command required by the CLI-first contract. The independent `@hcu-lab.me/palace` package exposes the same command and canonical compiled implementation when installed in a standalone or separate npm prefix. Installing both root packages into the same global prefix is unsupported because npm cannot give one executable name two owners; npm must fail with `EEXIST`, and `--force` is unsupported. The standalone package includes `LICENSE`, `palace.plugin.json`, its Skill installer and a closed import graph. A manifest installed or upgraded from that package is recorded as a manual PluginHost source.

Palace Skill files are user-home package assets, not per-runtime plugin state. A per-user ownership ledger records every package owner and the installed baseline hashes. Installation uses plain copied files by default, stages replacements outside agent discovery roots, publishes by rename, and recovers recorded interrupted updates. A pristine managed copy can upgrade or relocate; a modified or foreign same-name Skill is preserved. The final pristine owner may remove the managed Skill, while another installed owner keeps it available. Ordinary `palace` reads are metadata-only and never reconcile Skill files. If lifecycle scripts were skipped, `palace status`/`palace skill status` reports the missing Skill and the exact recovery command `palace skill install`; starting the Device/server runtime also performs a non-blocking reconciliation. Explicit inspection and repair are available through `palace skill status|install|repair|remove|adopt|prune`.

Every agent workflow starts with `palace status`. The response identifies the package, version, install path, runtime root, plugin state and write policy. Disabled, uninstalled, faulted or upgrade-required runtimes return typed actionable limitations. The `palace` CLI is always read-only. When a Device runtime is stopped, mutation uses `mcp-device knowledge --runtime-dir <state> ...` under the exclusive server lease; a standalone Palace installation without `mcp-device` has no mutation path. The Skill never stops the runtime by itself.

## Palace construction and learning

Palace builds durable knowledge cards and procedural candidates from local source evidence. It is not a replacement chat-history table. Its bounded offline discovery includes recognized Pi, Claude, Codex and MCP/Palace locations plus explicitly configured projects, logs, Skills and Git evidence. Source discovery/indexing requires no model and no cloud connection. Original sources are read, not rewritten. Missing or changed sources are detected when exact evidence is read.

Default background indexing is bounded; settings can disable it with `autoIndex: false` or adjust `indexBudget` (`maxRecords`, `maxFiles`, `maxBytes`, `intervalMs`). The interval is clamped between one second and one hour, with a 30-second default. `--project-root` and `--source-root` add explicit roots. Existing P1 `logs` and gated Context commands keep their own behavior.

The platform activity journal records bounded execution metadata and real exit/status information. It does not store full commands, file contents or raw stdout and does not treat an activity row as proof of successful task completion. Rotated or missing source evidence is not fabricated.

Recurring explicit fixes from independent sources can produce an inactive procedure candidate automatically. Activating a learned Skill requires trusted local validation: actual execution receipts plus an independent postcondition. Caller-supplied `success` fields or an invented validation result are insufficient. Automatic activation is opt-in (`autoActivateSkills`) and needs a trusted local `validationModule` exporting the expected runner factory. No automated edit to `AGENTS.md` is performed.

The local knowledge CLI is useful with a stopped runtime, including before account pairing:

```text
mcp-device knowledge --runtime-dir <state> search "{\"query\":\"reconnect timeout\"}"
mcp-device knowledge --runtime-dir <state> list
mcp-device knowledge --runtime-dir <state> read @<request.json>
mcp-device knowledge --runtime-dir <state> ingest @<request.json>
mcp-device knowledge --runtime-dir <state> learn @<request.json>
mcp-device knowledge --runtime-dir <state> correct @<request.json>
mcp-device knowledge --runtime-dir <state> skills
```

Use a JSON request file when shell quoting is inconvenient. For an active runtime, use its authenticated MCP surface. See [Palace design and measured limitations](../specs/008-v12-platform/palace-design.md) for card/evidence contracts and benchmark interpretation. The initial retriever is lexical; semantic/vector/graph expansion is not enabled without evidence that it helps.

## Backup, migration and rollback

Operate on actual runtime state directories, not source checkouts. Stop the runtime before copying SQLite state. A nonempty WAL/journal is rejected until shutdown/checkpoint has completed. Backup does not reset identity, passwords, accounts, invitations or plugin data.

```text
mcp-device state backup --source <stopped-state> --backup <new-backup-dir>
mcp-device state verify --backup <backup-dir>
mcp-device state restore --backup <backup-dir> --target <new-state-dir>
mcp-device state migrate --device-root <old-device-state> --gateway-root <old-gateway-state> --target <new-state-dir> --backup <new-backup-dir>
mcp-device state migrate --device-root <old-device-state> --gateway-root <old-gateway-state> --target <new-state-dir> --backup <new-backup-dir> --apply
```

Migration defaults to a dry-run plan. Apply first creates and verifies separate backups, then copies into a new target and validates its file hashes. Existing targets, overlapping roots, symlinks/junctions, unsafe paths and collisions are rejected. The original roots remain available for rollback; restore publishes only a new verified target. Plugin private files and managed evidence are included. Externally referenced original source files remain external; a backup cannot manufacture missing source evidence.

See [state migration contract](../specs/008-v12-platform/state-migration.md) for the manifest, collision policy and recovery sequence. Package relocation and plugin upgrade are tested separately from byte-exact state restoration; preserve user-managed plugin code or supply its current manifest when moving hosts.

## Reproducing source acceptance

Use an isolated checkout and explicit test state. The repository provides executable inventories and independent baseline/candidate parity runners.

```text
npm ci
npm run build
npm run test:v12
npm run test:parity -- --list --group port-free
npm run test:parity -- --group port-free --output <isolated-evidence-dir>
npm run test:v12:server
```

Server acceptance owns port 8104 for its duration. The cold-package test additionally requires `MCP_V12_COLD_PACKAGE_ROOT` pointing at the package inside an isolated production-dependency npm installation. It verifies the installed entry point, default enabled Palace, normal pairing/OAuth, native execution, backup/restore, retained identity and refresh-token continuity.

On the current G6 test machine, port 8103 belongs to an existing audit process and is preserved. G8 ports 8102 (production) and 8101 (backup) are outside these tests. No production rollout is authorized by source acceptance.

===== FILE scripts/v12/run-device-parity.mjs sha256=17112663947dd8de35741b4b806f7f13e8822f47dfd940bdacd367e89ab7fbc2 =====
#!/usr/bin/env node
import fs from 'node:fs/promises';
import { createWriteStream } from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import os from 'node:os';
import { createHash } from 'node:crypto';
import { fileURLToPath, pathToFileURL } from 'node:url';
import { spawn, spawnSync } from 'node:child_process';

const repoRoot = path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '../..');
const manifest = JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(path.join(repoRoot, 'specs/008-v12-platform/device-parity.json'), 'utf8'));
const argv = process.argv.slice(2);
const argument = (name, fallback) => {
    const at = argv.indexOf(name);
    if (at < 0) return fallback;
    if (!argv[at + 1] || argv[at + 1].startsWith('--')) throw new Error('Missing value for ' + name);
    return argv[at + 1];
};
const group = argument('--group', 'port-free');
const baseline = path.resolve(argument('--baseline', manifest.baseline.path));
const candidate = path.resolve(argument('--candidate', repoRoot));
const dependencyRoots = {
    baseline: path.resolve(argument('--baseline-dependencies', path.join(baseline, 'node_modules'))),
    candidate: path.resolve(argument('--candidate-dependencies', path.join(candidate, 'node_modules')))
};
const outputRoot = path.resolve(argument('--output', path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'mcp-device-v12-parity-' + Date.now())));
const only = argument('--only', '');
if (!['', 'core', 'capabilities', 'plugins'].includes(only)) throw new Error('Invalid --only');
const targetMode = argument('--target', 'both');
if (!['baseline', 'candidate', 'both'].includes(targetMode)) throw new Error('Invalid --target');
const targets = [['baseline', baseline], ['candidate', candidate]].filter(([label]) => targetMode === 'both' || targetMode === label);
const requestedTest = argument('--test', '');
const selected = [...new Set(manifest.families.filter(item => item.group === group).flatMap(item => item.tests))]
    .filter(file => !requestedTest || file === requestedTest);
if (!selected.length && only !== 'core') throw new Error('Unknown or empty group: ' + group);
if (argv.includes('--list')) {
    console.log(JSON.stringify({ group, tests: selected, classification: 'SOURCE_ACCEPTED', freshStatus: 'NOT_RUN' }, null, 2));
    process.exit(0);
}
// Socket/lifecycle/manual groups are deliberately not runnable by accident.
// Their fixtures need a separately reviewed lane because 8103 is occupied and
// 8101/8102 are protected. This script never binds a server or changes identity.
const permittedGroups = new Set(['port-free', 'context', 'log-palace', 'contract-static']);
if (!permittedGroups.has(group)) throw new Error('Group requires a dedicated reviewed harness: ' + group);
for (const [, target] of targets) {
    await fs.access(path.join(target, 'dist/tool-dispatcher.js'));
    await fs.access(path.join(target, 'node_modules'));
}
await fs.mkdir(outputRoot, { recursive: true });
const forbiddenRoots = [baseline, candidate].map(item => item.toLowerCase() + path.sep);
if (forbiddenRoots.some(root => (outputRoot.toLowerCase() + path.sep).startsWith(root))) {
    throw new Error('Parity output must be outside source checkouts');
}
const receipts = {
    schemaVersion: 1, startedAt: new Date().toISOString(), node: process.version,
    machine: os.hostname(), platform: process.platform, group, targetMode, only, requestedTest,
    baseline: { path: baseline, expectedSha: manifest.baseline.sha, dependencies: dependencyRoots.baseline },
    candidate: { path: candidate, dependencies: dependencyRoots.candidate }, results: [],
    productionVerified: false, socketsAllowed: false
};
for (const [label, target] of [['baseline', baseline], ['candidate', candidate]]) {
    let head = spawnSync('git', ['-C', target, 'rev-parse', 'HEAD'], { encoding: 'utf8', windowsHide: true });
    if (head.status !== 0 && process.platform === 'linux') {
        // A Windows-created linked worktree stores an absolute drive path.
        // Resolve its existing metadata on WSL without editing either Git tree.
        const pointer = await fs.readFile(path.join(target, '.git'), 'utf8').catch(() => '');
        const match = pointer.trim().match(/^gitdir: ([A-Za-z]):[\\/](.+)$/);
        if (match) {
            const gitDir = '/mnt/' + match[1].toLowerCase() + '/' + match[2].replaceAll('\\', '/');
            head = spawnSync('git', ['--git-dir', gitDir, 'rev-parse', 'HEAD'], { encoding: 'utf8' });
            receipts[label].gitMetadata = gitDir;
        }
    }
    if (head.status !== 0) throw new Error('Cannot inspect ' + label + ' Git HEAD: ' + head.stderr);
    receipts[label].sha = head.stdout.trim();
    receipts[label].distFingerprint = {};
    for (const rel of ['version.js', 'tool-dispatcher.js', 'device/gateway-tool-adapter.js', 'context/device-adapter.js', 'log-palace/store.js']) {
        try { receipts[label].distFingerprint[rel] = createHash('sha256').update(await fs.readFile(path.join(target, 'dist', rel))).digest('hex'); }
        catch (error) { if (error.code !== 'ENOENT') throw error; receipts[label].distFingerprint[rel] = 'MISSING'; }
    }
}
if (receipts.baseline.sha !== manifest.baseline.sha) throw new Error('Baseline SHA differs from frozen manifest');
const receiptPath = path.join(outputRoot, 'device-parity-receipt.json');
async function saveReceipt() {
    await fs.writeFile(receiptPath, JSON.stringify(receipts, null, 2) + '\n');
}
await saveReceipt();

const guardFile = path.join(outputRoot, 'port-free-guard.mjs');
await fs.writeFile(guardFile, [
    "import net from 'node:net';",
    "import os from 'node:os';",
    "if (process.env.DEVICE_PARITY_HOME) os.homedir = () => process.env.DEVICE_PARITY_HOME;",
    "import { syncBuiltinESMExports } from 'node:module';",
    "const denied = () => { throw Object.assign(new Error('PARITY_PORT_FREE_GUARD: TCP socket operation is outside this fixture group'), { code: 'PARITY_PORT_FREE_GUARD' }); };",
    'const listen = net.Server.prototype.listen;',
    'const connect = net.Socket.prototype.connect;',
    "const tcp = value => typeof value === 'number' || (Array.isArray(value) && value.some(tcp)) || Boolean(value && typeof value === 'object' && ('port' in value || typeof value.host === 'string'));",
    'net.Server.prototype.listen = function(...args) { if (tcp(args[0])) return denied(); return listen.apply(this, args); };',
    'net.Socket.prototype.connect = function(...args) { if (tcp(args[0])) return denied(); return connect.apply(this, args); };',
    'syncBuiltinESMExports();',
    ''
].join('\n'));

async function linkDirectory(source, target) {
    try { await fs.symlink(source, target, process.platform === 'win32' ? 'junction' : 'dir'); }
    catch (error) {
        if (error.code !== 'EEXIST' || path.resolve(await fs.realpath(target)).toLowerCase() !== path.resolve(await fs.realpath(source)).toLowerCase()) throw error;
    }
}
const staged = new Map();
async function stage(target, label) {
    if (staged.has(label)) return staged.get(label);
    const root = path.join(outputRoot, 'workspaces', label);
    await fs.mkdir(root, { recursive: true });
    await fs.cp(path.join(target, 'test'), path.join(root, 'test'), {
        recursive: true,
        filter: source => !source.split(path.sep).some(piece => ['test_output', 'node_modules'].includes(piece))
    });
    for (const name of ['package.json', 'package-lock.json']) {
        try { await fs.copyFile(path.join(target, name), path.join(root, name)); }
        catch (error) { if (error.code !== 'ENOENT') throw error; }
    }
    // Resolve installed-relative fixtures against their isolated installation;
    // symlinking dist would resolve authorization's config path back to source.
    // Scripts are copied as well: direct-entry guards compare process.argv[1]
    // with import.meta.url and intentionally do not execute through a symlink.
    for (const name of ['dist', 'config', 'scripts', '.github']) {
        try { await fs.cp(path.join(target, name), path.join(root, name), { recursive: true }); }
        catch (error) { if (error.code !== 'ENOENT') throw error; }
    }
    for (const name of ['node_modules', 'src', 'specs']) {
        const source = name === 'node_modules' ? dependencyRoots[label] : path.join(target, name);
        try { await fs.access(source); await linkDirectory(source, path.join(root, name)); }
        catch (error) { if (error.code !== 'ENOENT') throw error; }
    }
    staged.set(label, root);
    return root;
}
function stopOwnChild(child) {
    if (process.platform === 'win32' && child.pid) {
        spawn('taskkill.exe', ['/PID', String(child.pid), '/T', '/F'], { windowsHide: true, stdio: 'ignore' });
    } else child.kill('SIGKILL');
}
async function run(label, root, file, extraEnv = {}, timeoutMs = 180000) {
    // Keep SQLite filenames below Windows' path limit after repository hashes.
    const id = label.slice(0, 1) + '-' + createHash('sha256').update(file).digest('hex').slice(0, 12);
    const sandbox = path.join(outputRoot, 'runs', id);
    await fs.mkdir(sandbox, { recursive: true });
    const stdoutPath = path.join(sandbox, 'stdout.log'), stderrPath = path.join(sandbox, 'stderr.log');
    const out = createWriteStream(stdoutPath), err = createWriteStream(stderrPath);
    let args = file.includes('/context/') || file.includes('/log-palace/') || file.includes('/v12/')
        ? ['--test', file] : [file];
    // Several accepted v11 suites compare a URL with a Windows filesystem path.
    // Calling the exported runner makes their assertions execute on every OS;
    // the original suite files remain unchanged, and the marker prevents a
    // zero-assertion exit from being counted as acceptance evidence.
    const source = await fs.readFile(path.join(root, file), 'utf8');
    const guardedEntrypoint = source.includes('if (import.meta.url === `file://${process.argv[1]}`)');
    const executionMarker = 'PARITY_SUITE_EXECUTED:' + file;
    if (guardedEntrypoint) {
        const wrapper = path.join(sandbox, 'invoke-exported-suite.mjs');
        await fs.writeFile(wrapper, [
            'const suite = await import(' + JSON.stringify(pathToFileURL(path.join(root, file)).href) + ');',
            "if (typeof suite.default !== 'function') throw new Error('PARITY_MISSING_EXPORTED_RUNNER');",
            'const outcome = await suite.default();',
            "if (outcome === false) throw new Error('PARITY_SUITE_RETURNED_FALSE');",
            'console.log(' + JSON.stringify(executionMarker) + ');',
            'process.exit(0);',
            ''
        ].join('\n'));
        args = [wrapper];
    }
    const startedAt = new Date().toISOString(), start = performance.now();
    const env = { ...process.env, ...extraEnv };
    for (const key of Object.keys(env)) if (key.toLowerCase() === 'path') delete env[key];
    env.PATH = path.dirname(process.execPath) + path.delimiter + (process.env.PATH ?? process.env.Path ?? '');
    const result = await new Promise(resolve => {
        const child = spawn(process.execPath, args, {
            cwd: root, windowsHide: true,
            env: {
                ...env,
                MCP_DEVICE_CONFIG_DIR: path.join(sandbox, 'config'),
                DEVICE_PARITY_HOME: root.startsWith(path.join(outputRoot, 'workspaces')) ? root : path.join(sandbox, 'home'),
                MCP_DEVICE_REMOTE: 'false', MCP_DEVICE_DISABLE_TELEMETRY: 'true',
                TMP: sandbox, TEMP: sandbox, TMPDIR: sandbox,
                NODE_OPTIONS: '--import ' + pathToFileURL(guardFile).href
            },
            stdio: ['ignore', 'pipe', 'pipe']
        });
        let timedOut = false;
        const timer = setTimeout(() => { timedOut = true; stopOwnChild(child); }, timeoutMs);
        child.stdout.pipe(out); child.stderr.pipe(err);
        child.on('error', error => {
            clearTimeout(timer); out.end(); err.end();
            resolve({ exitCode: null, status: 'FAIL', error: error.message, timedOut });
        });
        child.on('close', (code, signal) => {
            clearTimeout(timer);
            resolve({ exitCode: code, signal, status: code === 0 && !timedOut ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL', timedOut });
        });
    });
    await Promise.all([new Promise(resolve => out.closed ? resolve() : out.on('close', resolve)), new Promise(resolve => err.closed ? resolve() : err.on('close', resolve))]);
    if (guardedEntrypoint && result.status === 'PASS'
        && !(await fs.readFile(stdoutPath, 'utf8')).includes(executionMarker)) {
        result.status = 'FAIL';
        result.error = 'PARITY_NO_EXECUTION_RECEIPT';
    }
    const item = { label, test: file, entrypointMode: guardedEntrypoint ? 'EXPORTED_RUNNER' : 'DIRECT',
        command: [process.execPath, ...args], cwd: root, startedAt,
        durationMs: Math.round(performance.now() - start), ...result, stdoutPath, stderrPath };
    receipts.results.push(item);
    await saveReceipt();
    console.log(item.status + ' ' + label + ' ' + file + ' (' + item.durationMs + ' ms)');
    return item;
}

if (!only) {
    for (const [label, target] of targets) {
        const root = await stage(target, label);
        for (const file of selected) await run(label, root, file);
    }
}
if ((!only && !requestedTest && group === 'port-free' && targetMode === 'both') || only === 'core') {
    const baselineFixture = await stage(baseline, 'baseline');
    const candidateFixture = await stage(candidate, 'candidate');
    await run('comparator', candidateFixture, 'test/v12/device-core-parity.test.js', {
        DEVICE_PARITY_BASELINE_REPO: baselineFixture,
        DEVICE_PARITY_OUTPUT_ROOT: path.join(outputRoot, 'core-comparator')
    }, 240000);
}
if (only === 'capabilities') await run('candidate', await stage(candidate, 'candidate'), 'test/v12/device-capabilities.test.js');
if (only === 'plugins') {
    const root = await stage(candidate, 'candidate');
    for (const file of ['test/v12/plugin-host.test.js', 'test/v12/plugin-services.test.js', 'test/v12/plugin-bundles.test.js']) await run('candidate', root, file);
}
receipts.completedAt = new Date().toISOString();
receipts.summary = {
    passed: receipts.results.filter(item => item.status === 'PASS').length,
    failed: receipts.results.filter(item => item.status === 'FAIL').length,
    notRunFamilies: manifest.families.filter(item => !item.tests.some(file => receipts.results.some(result => result.test === file))).map(item => item.id),
    parityMeaning: 'Only the listed executed suites, targets and fingerprints are evidenced. Core behavioral comparison is present only when its explicit comparator result is listed. No production or unlisted-platform acceptance.'
};
await saveReceipt();
console.log('Receipt: ' + receiptPath);
if (receipts.summary.failed) process.exitCode = 1;
===== END scripts/v12/run-device-parity.mjs =====

===== FILE skills/palace-cli/SKILL.md sha256=3910c44e9cb76998397428a14ffd0b968b176626be938666669dd074bfeb219e =====
---
name: palace-cli
description: Use Palace's local CLI to retrieve evidence-backed project or device knowledge, check prior technical decisions, inspect procedure candidates, and avoid repeating previously observed errors. Apply before tasks requiring historical context or reusable fixes; use the installed Palace CLI and never call a dedicated Palace MCP server or NeuralMemory MCP tools.
---

# Palace CLI — evidence-first recall

Palace is an optional plugin, enabled by default with MCP Device or installed separately as `@hcu-lab.me/palace`. Its local **CLI**, not an MCP knowledge endpoint, is the operational interface.

## Availability first

Always begin with:

```bash
palace status
```

If `palace` is not on `PATH`, report that Palace is not installed. Do not run a source-checkout file as a substitute. If status reports `PALACE_DISABLED`, `PALACE_UNINSTALLED`, `PALACE_FAULTED`, `PALACE_UPGRADE_REQUIRED`, or another limitation, report its remediation and do not use Palace for the task.

## Retrieval commands

Run on the device hosting the Palace database:

```bash
palace search "specific problem or error" --limit 3 --max-bytes 8000
palace search "project issue" --project-id PROJECT_ID --limit 3
palace read CARD_ID --max-bytes 16000
palace skills                         # validated procedures only
palace skills --include-candidates    # candidates are not approved instructions
```

When needed, pass `--runtime-dir /path/to/server/runtime` or an explicitly reviewed `--data-root /path/to/palace/data`. For agents on another machine, use an **existing authorized Device shell tool** to run `palace` on the target machine. Do not introduce a Palace-specific MCP server.

## Workflow

1. Run `palace status` and obey the returned runtime/plugin state.
2. State the current task and terms that distinguish it from unrelated sessions. Query up to three results initially.
3. Inspect each result's `status`, `scope`, `evidenceCount`, `version`, and `summary` before loading a full card.
4. Read only the most relevant card. Do not dump the database into model context.
5. Treat `hypothesis` and `observed` as leads, not validated facts. A read-only snapshot does not reverify original source bytes; inspect primary evidence before relying on a claim.
6. Use `palace skills` without `--include-candidates` for reusable procedures. `activationPolicy: candidate_only` means this runtime cannot validate new candidates; it does not invalidate previously recorded validations. Check each returned card's status, evidence and current preconditions. Never treat a candidate as an approved procedure.
7. If evidence disagrees with the real environment, prefer direct observation and report the mismatch.
8. Report precise provenance and keep the final answer compact.

## Mutation and validation boundary

The Palace CLI is read-only while the runtime is active. Corrections and new procedures use the authorized `mcp-device knowledge correct|learn --runtime-dir DIR` workflow while that runtime is stopped under its exclusive lease. Never stop a runtime unless the user explicitly requests it. Never claim that a candidate is validated without a trusted execution receipt and independent postcondition.

## Boundaries

- Do not call NeuralMemory or legacy `knowledge.*` MCP tools for Palace operations.
- Never infer `validated` from similarity, repetition, model text, or a successful shell exit alone.
- Do not expose confidential card bodies or logs beyond the current request's scope.
- Verify token savings with completed-task correctness and actual provider token/billing telemetry against a no-Palace control under the same model and task conditions.

===== END skills/palace-cli/SKILL.md =====

===== FILE src/device/device.ts sha256=53b801fc4069797dab2d321658d2dd8e659cde649ac75668732abf0fbcfa9bfd =====
#!/usr/bin/env node

import { fileURLToPath } from 'url';
import os from 'os';
import path from 'path';

import { LocalExecutionEngine } from './execution-engine.js';
import { GatewayDeviceChannel } from './gateway-channel.js';
import { GatewayDeviceConfigStore, createGatewayProxyAgent } from './gateway-config.js';
import { GatewayDeviceIdentity } from './gateway-identity.js';
import { pairGatewayDevice } from './gateway-pairing.js';
import { bootstrapOfficialGatewayTrust } from './official-trust.js';
import { GatewayDeviceStatusStore } from './device-status.js';
import { createPlatformDeviceCore as createDeviceCore, type PlatformDeviceCore as DeviceCore } from '../platform/device-runtime.js';
import { RuntimeOwner, acquireRuntimeOwner, type RuntimeOwnerStatus } from './runtime-owner.js';
import { acquireServerLease } from '../platform/runtime-lease.js';
import {
    assertDeviceStartupAllowedDuringUpdate,
    launchDeviceUpdateHelper,
    prepareDevicePackageUpdate,
    reconcileDeviceUpdateAfterStart,
    retireDeviceUpdateState,
    signalDeviceUpdateHandoff
} from './self-update.js';
import { captureRemote } from '../utils/capture.js';
import { VERSION } from '../version.js';

export class MCPDevice {
    private isShuttingDown = false;
    private shutdownRequest?: Promise<void>;
    private core?: DeviceCore;
    private unsubscribeCapabilities?: () => void;
    private executionEngine = new LocalExecutionEngine();
    private gatewayChannel?: GatewayDeviceChannel;
    private gatewayProxyAgent?: { destroy: () => void };
    private runtimeOwner?: RuntimeOwner;
    private stateLeases: Array<() => Promise<void>> = [];

    constructor() {
        this.setupShutdownHandlers();
    }

    private setupShutdownHandlers() {
        const handleShutdown = async (signal: string) => {
            console.log(this.isShuttingDown
                ? `\n${signal} received, waiting for owned resources to stop...`
                : `\n${signal} received, initiating graceful shutdown...`);
            const shutdownWarning = setTimeout(() => {
                console.error('\n⚠️ Graceful shutdown is still waiting for owned resources; runtime ownership is retained.');
                process.exitCode = 1;
            }, 5000);

            try {
                await this.shutdown();
                process.exit(0);
            } catch (error) {
                console.error('Error during shutdown:', error);
                await captureRemote('remote_device_shutdown_handler_error', { error }).catch(() => {});
                // Exiting here would make the PID lease stale while a plugin
                // may still own its files. Keep the owner available for retry.
                process.exitCode = 1;
            } finally {
                clearTimeout(shutdownWarning);
            }
        };

        process.on('SIGINT', () => {
            handleShutdown('SIGINT').catch((error) => {
                console.error('Fatal error during shutdown:', error);
                captureRemote('remote_device_shutdown_handler_error', { error, signal: 'SIGINT' }).catch(() => {});
                process.exitCode = 1;
            });
        });

        process.on('SIGTERM', () => {
            handleShutdown('SIGTERM').catch((error) => {
                console.error('Fatal error during shutdown:', error);
                captureRemote('remote_device_shutdown_handler_error', { error, signal: 'SIGTERM' }).catch(() => {});
                process.exitCode = 1;
            });
        });
    }

    async start(options: { confirmTakeover?: (existing: RuntimeOwnerStatus) => Promise<boolean> | boolean } = {}) {
        try {
            console.log('🚀 Starting MCP Device...');
            if (process.env.DEBUG_MODE === 'true') console.log('  - 🐞 DEBUG_MODE');

            // A fresh external update helper owns the package lifecycle.
            // Refuse a competing runtime until it completes or becomes stale.
            await assertDeviceStartupAllowedDuringUpdate(VERSION);

            const managerArg = process.argv.find(value => value.startsWith('--manager='));
            const manager = managerArg?.slice('--manager='.length);
            const mode = process.argv.includes('--service')
                ? (process.platform === 'win32' ? 'windows' : manager === 'pm2' ? 'pm2' : 'systemd')
                : 'foreground';
            this.runtimeOwner = new RuntimeOwner({ mode, onStop: () => this.shutdown() });
            await acquireRuntimeOwner(this.runtimeOwner, { confirmTakeover: options.confirmTakeover });
            const { CONFIG_DIR } = await import('../config.js');
            const stateRoots = new Map<string, string>();
            for (const root of [this.runtimeOwner.paths.root, CONFIG_DIR]) {
                const absolute = path.resolve(root);
                stateRoots.set(process.platform === 'win32' ? absolute.toLowerCase() : absolute, absolute);
            }
            for (const [, root] of [...stateRoots].sort(([a], [b]) => a.localeCompare(b))) {
                this.stateLeases.push(await acquireServerLease(root, { allowDeviceOwnerPid: process.pid }));
            }
            await this.runtimeOwner.bootstrap();
            await bootstrapOfficialGatewayTrust(new GatewayDeviceConfigStore());
            // Remote mode uses direct in-process dispatch (tool-dispatcher.ts).
            // Do not eagerly initialize LocalExecutionEngine, avoiding child dist/index.js process spawn.

            const gatewayStatus = new GatewayDeviceStatusStore();
            const storedGatewayStatus = await gatewayStatus.load();
            const gatewayConfig = await new GatewayDeviceConfigStore().load();
            const gatewayUrl = String(gatewayConfig.gatewayUrl || storedGatewayStatus.gatewayUrl || '').trim();
            if (!gatewayUrl) throw new Error('Gateway configuration is missing. Run `mcp-device login` or `mcp-device install` first.');
            if (gatewayConfig.securityProtocolFloor >= 2 && !gatewayConfig.appCaPem) {
                throw new Error('Gateway security protocol floor requires a provisioned application CA.');
            }
            const proxy = createGatewayProxyAgent(gatewayConfig);
            this.gatewayProxyAgent = proxy.agent;

            process.env.MCP_DEVICE_REMOTE = 'true';
            console.log(`⏳ Connecting directly to MCP Device server ${gatewayUrl}`);

            const identity = new GatewayDeviceIdentity();
            const identityRecord = await identity.loadOrCreate();
            this.runtimeOwner.setDeviceId(identityRecord.deviceId);
            // Local providers start before pairing; local indexing needs no account session.
            this.core = await createDeviceCore({
                deviceId: identityRecord.deviceId,
                allowedRoots: gatewayConfig.allowedRoots
            });
            const enrollmentCredential = String(
                process.env.MCP_GATEWAY_ENROLLMENT_TOKEN || process.env.MCP_DEVICE_ENROLLMENT_TOKEN || ''
            ).trim() || undefined;
            let pairingGrant: string | undefined;

            if (!identityRecord.enrolled && !enrollmentCredential) {
                const pairing = await pairGatewayDevice({ gatewayUrl, identity, proxyAgent: proxy.agent, deviceName: os.hostname() });
                pairingGrant = pairing.enrollmentGrant;
                await gatewayStatus.update({
                    gatewayUrl,
                    deviceId: identityRecord.deviceId,
                    deviceName: os.hostname(),
                    identityPresent: true,
                    account: { connected: false, label: pairing.account.label },
                    connection: { ...storedGatewayStatus.connection, online: false }
                });
                console.log(`✓ Authorized account: ${pairing.account.label || 'connected'}`);
            }

            this.gatewayChannel = new GatewayDeviceChannel({
                gatewayUrl,
                enrollmentToken: enrollmentCredential,
                pairingGrant,
                identity,
                proxyAgent: proxy.agent,
                securityProtocolFloor: gatewayConfig.securityProtocolFloor,
                appCaPem: gatewayConfig.appCaPem || undefined,
                adapter: { call: (tool, args) => this.core!.invoke(tool, args) },
                capabilities: () => this.core?.capabilities ?? [],
                // In remote direct-dispatch mode, the daemon is ready once the gateway channel & dispatch are established.
                runtimeState: () => ({
                    runtime_ready: true,
                    runtime_reason: null,
                    execution_runtime_generation: this.core!.runtimeInstanceId
                }),
                agentVersion: VERSION || process.env.npm_package_version || '1.0.12',
                onUpdateRequest: async (targetVersion, { requestId }) => {
                    const record = await identity.loadOrCreate();
                    const prepared = await prepareDevicePackageUpdate({
                        targetVersion,
                        requestId,
                        deviceId: record.deviceId,
                        childPid: this.executionEngine.getChildPid(),
                        fromVersion: VERSION
                    });
                    try {
                        // Launch the helper while the service is healthy. The
                        // helper remains inert until the explicit handoff marker.
                        await launchDeviceUpdateHelper(prepared);
                    } catch (error) {
                        await retireDeviceUpdateState(prepared.state).catch(() => {});
                        throw error;
                    }
                    return {
                        mode: 'detached' as const,
                        handoff: async () => {
                            // Capture the last live child PID, then complete graceful
                            // shutdown and RuntimeOwner release before arming the helper.
                            // This preserves G5.6: no destructive update work may begin
                            // until the old runtime has finished releasing package handles.
                            const childPid = this.executionEngine.getChildPid();
                            await this.shutdown();
                            await signalDeviceUpdateHandoff({ childPid });
                            process.exit(0);
                        }
                    };
                },
                onStatus: async payload => {
                    const record = await identity.loadOrCreate();
                    await gatewayStatus.update({
                        gatewayUrl,
                        deviceId: record.deviceId,
                        deviceName: payload?.device?.name || os.hostname(),
                        identityPresent: true,
                        account: payload?.account || storedGatewayStatus.account,
                        connection: {
                            online: payload?.device?.online !== false,
                            connectionEpoch: payload?.device?.connectionEpoch ?? null,
                            lastConnectedAt: payload?.device?.connectedAt || Date.now()
                        },
                        usage: payload?.usage || null,
                        schema: payload?.schema || null,
                        usageFreshAt: Date.now()
                    });
                }
            });

            this.unsubscribeCapabilities = this.core.onCapabilitiesChanged?.(() => {
                this.gatewayChannel?.updateCapabilities();
            });
            await this.gatewayChannel.start();

            const updateReconciliation = await reconcileDeviceUpdateAfterStart(VERSION);
            if (updateReconciliation.action === 'success') {
                // Successful reconnect already advertised the target package_version
                // in auth_hello; that reconnect is the authoritative completion proof.
                // Do not send a late "installed" status: the gateway completes the
                // update from the authenticated reconnect itself.
                try {
                    await retireDeviceUpdateState(updateReconciliation.state);
                } catch (error: any) {
                    // A transient Windows EPERM/EBUSY while the detached helper is
                    // exiting must not turn a healthy target reconnect into startup failure.
                    console.warn('Update cleanup deferred:', error?.message || error);
                }
            } else if (updateReconciliation.action === 'report-failed') {
                try {
                    await this.gatewayChannel.reportUpdateFailure(
                        updateReconciliation.state.requestId,
                        updateReconciliation.state.targetVersion,
                        updateReconciliation.state.errorCode || 'DEVICE_UPDATE_FAILED',
                        updateReconciliation.state.message || 'MCP Device update rolled back.'
                    );
                    await retireDeviceUpdateState(updateReconciliation.state).catch((error: any) => {
                        console.warn('Failed update cleanup deferred:', error?.message || error);
                    });
                } catch (error: any) {
                    // Keep durable failed state for a later reporting attempt; do not
                    // make the restored runtime unavailable because reporting raced.
                    console.warn('Failed update report deferred:', error?.message || error);
                }
            }

            console.log('✓ Device ready through direct Gateway channel');
        } catch (error: any) {
            console.error(' - ❌ Device startup failed:', error.message);
            if (error.stack && process.env.DEBUG_MODE === 'true') console.error('Stack trace:', error.stack);
            await captureRemote('remote_device_startup_failed', { error });
            await this.shutdown();
            process.exit(1);
        }
    }

    shutdown(): Promise<void> {
        if (this.shutdownRequest) return this.shutdownRequest;
        this.isShuttingDown = true;
        const request = this.performShutdown();
        this.shutdownRequest = request;
        // Concurrent callers share the same outcome. A rejected cleanup keeps
        // ownership and resources available for a real retry; only success is cached.
        void request.catch(() => {
            if (this.shutdownRequest === request) this.shutdownRequest = undefined;
        });
        return request;
    }

    private async performShutdown(): Promise<void> {
        console.log('\n🛑 Shutting down device...');
        try {
            try {
                this.unsubscribeCapabilities?.();
                this.unsubscribeCapabilities = undefined;
                if (this.gatewayChannel) {
                    console.log('  → Closing direct Gateway channel...');
                    await this.gatewayChannel.stop();
                    this.gatewayChannel = undefined;
                }
                if (this.gatewayProxyAgent) {
                    this.gatewayProxyAgent.destroy();
                    this.gatewayProxyAgent = undefined;
                }
                await new GatewayDeviceStatusStore().markOffline().catch(() => {});
            } finally {
                // Release shared state only after the same Core and its plugins
                // have stopped, including failures partway through startup.
                await this.core?.close();
                this.core = undefined;
                await this.executionEngine.shutdown();
                while (this.stateLeases.length) {
                    await this.stateLeases[this.stateLeases.length - 1]();
                    this.stateLeases.pop();
                }
                if (this.runtimeOwner) {
                    await this.runtimeOwner.release();
                    this.runtimeOwner = undefined;
                }
            }
            console.log('✓ Device shutdown complete');
        } catch (error: any) {
            console.error('Shutdown error:', error.message);
            await captureRemote('remote_device_shutdown_error', { error }).catch(() => {});
            throw error;
        }
    }
}

// Start only when this module itself is the entrypoint. Package/bin execution goes
// through src/mcp-device.ts -> src/index.ts, which owns the single device lifecycle.
export function isModuleEntrypoint(moduleUrl: string, argvEntry?: string, pmExecPath?: string): boolean {
    const moduleEntryPath = path.resolve(fileURLToPath(moduleUrl));
    const candidates = [argvEntry, pmExecPath]
        .map(value => value ? path.resolve(value) : '')
        .filter(Boolean);
    return candidates.some(candidate => process.platform === 'win32'
        ? moduleEntryPath.toLowerCase() === candidate.toLowerCase()
        : moduleEntryPath === candidate);
}

const isMainModule = isModuleEntrypoint(import.meta.url, process.argv[1], process.env.pm_exec_path);
if (isMainModule) {
    const device = new MCPDevice();
    device.start();
}

#!/usr/bin/env node
import './bootstrap.js';

// Package installation, runtime startup, and explicit Skill commands own full
// reconciliation. Help, status, plugin listing and other read commands never
// create or update an agent home. Scripts-disabled installs get an explicit
// `palace skill install` remediation from the Palace metadata-only probe.
const requested=process.argv[2],nested=process.argv[3];
const startsRuntime=!process.argv.some(arg=>arg==='--help'||arg==='-h')&&(['start','restart','server'].includes(requested??'')||(['device','remote'].includes(requested??'')&&['start','restart'].includes(nested??'')));
if(startsRuntime){
  try { const { reconcilePalaceSkill } = await import('./plugins/palace/skill-manager.js'); await reconcilePalaceSkill({nonBlocking:true,repair:true}); }
  catch (error) { if (process.env.DEBUG_MODE === 'true') process.stderr.write('[palace-skill] '+String((error as Error).message??error)+'\n'); }
}

// Context and P1 commands remain isolated local operations.
if (process.argv[2] === 'context') {
  const { runContextCli } = await import('./context/cli.js');
  const result = await runContextCli(process.argv.slice(3));
  process.stdout.write(result.stdout);
  process.stderr.write(result.stderr);
  process.exitCode = result.exitCode;
} else if (process.argv[2] === 'logs') {
  const { runLogPalaceCli } = await import('./log-palace/cli.js');
  const result = runLogPalaceCli(process.argv.slice(3));
  process.stdout.write(result.stdout);
  process.stderr.write(result.stderr);
  process.exitCode = result.exitCode;
} else if (['server', 'plugins', 'knowledge', 'admin', 'state', 'help', '--help', '-h'].includes(process.argv[2])) {
  const { runPlatformCli } = await import('./platform/cli.js');
  await runPlatformCli(['--help', '-h'].includes(process.argv[2]) ? 'help' : process.argv[2], process.argv.slice(3));
} else {
  if (process.argv[2] === 'device') process.argv[2] = 'remote';
  else if (process.argv[2] !== 'remote') process.argv.splice(2, 0, 'remote');
  const { runRemote } = await import('./npm-scripts/remote.js');
  await runRemote();
}

const usage = `MCP Device 1.0.12

  mcp-device server [--runtime-dir DIR] [--host HOST] [--port PORT] [--loopback-port PORT] [--public-url URL] [--skill-root DIR] [--allow-http-lan] [--legacy-knowledge-mcp]

interface Parsed { values: Record<string, string[]>; flags: Set<string>; positional: string[]; }
function parse(argv: string[]): Parsed {
  const values: Record<string, string[]> = {}, flags = new Set<string>(), positional: string[] = [];
  const valued = new Set(['runtime-dir', 'host', 'port', 'loopback-port', 'public-url', 'skill-root', 'project-root', 'source-root', 'config', 'source', 'backup', 'target', 'device-root', 'gateway-root']);
  const switches = new Set(['help', 'enable', 'bundled', 'apply', 'json', 'allow-http-lan', 'legacy-knowledge-mcp']);
  for (let i = 0; i < argv.length; i++) {
    const arg = argv[i];
    if (arg === '--') { positional.push(...argv.slice(i + 1)); break; }
    if (arg === '-h') { flags.add('help'); continue; }
    if (!arg.startsWith('--')) { positional.push(arg); continue; }
    const equal = arg.indexOf('='), key = arg.slice(2, equal < 0 ? undefined : equal);
    if (switches.has(key) && equal < 0) { flags.add(key); continue; }
    if (!valued.has(key)) throw new CapabilityError('INVALID_ARGUMENT', 'Unknown option: --' + key);
    const value = equal < 0 ? argv[++i] : arg.slice(equal + 1);
    if (!value || value.startsWith('--')) throw new CapabilityError('INVALID_ARGUMENT', 'Missing value for --' + key);
    (values[key] ??= []).push(value);
  }
  return { values, flags, positional };
}
const one = (args: Parsed, key: string, fallback?: string): string | undefined => args.values[key]?.[args.values[key].length - 1] ?? fallback;
function stateRoot(args: Parsed, serverDefault = false): string {
  return path.resolve(one(args, 'runtime-dir', process.env.MCP_DEVICE_CONFIG_DIR || path.join(os.homedir(), '.mcp-device', ...(serverDefault ? ['server'] : [])))!);
}
function required(args: Parsed, key: string): string {
  const value = one(args, key); if (!value) throw new CapabilityError('INVALID_ARGUMENT', '--' + key + ' is required'); return path.resolve(value);
}
async function jsonFile(filename: string): Promise<any> {
  const text = await fs.readFile(path.resolve(filename), 'utf8');
  return JSON.parse(text.replace(/^\uFEFF/, ''));
}
const print = (value: unknown) => process.stdout.write(JSON.stringify(value, null, 2) + '\n');
const event = (value: unknown) => process.stdout.write(JSON.stringify(value) + '\n');

async function pluginCli(args: Parsed): Promise<void> {
  const { PluginHost } = await import('./plugin-host.js');
  const root = stateRoot(args, true), [action = 'list', target, extra] = args.positional;
  const host = new PluginHost({ stateRoot: root, deviceId: 'local-plugin-management' });
  let release: (() => Promise<void>) | undefined;
  await host.load();
  try {
    if (action === 'list') { print({ runtime_dir: root, plugins: host.list(), diagnostics: host.diagnostics() }); return; }
    if (action === 'repair-lock') { print({runtime_dir:root,...await host.repairRegistryLock()}); return; }
    if (!target) throw new CapabilityError('INVALID_ARGUMENT', 'Plugin id or manifest file is required');
    if (action === 'install' || action === 'upgrade') {
      let manifest: PluginManifest;
      if(args.flags.has('bundled')){
        const { bundledPluginManifests }=await import('../distribution.js');
        const bundled=bundledPluginManifests().find(item=>item.id===target);
        if(!bundled)throw new CapabilityError('PLUGIN_NOT_FOUND','Unknown bundled plugin: '+target);
        manifest=bundled;
      }else{
        manifest=await jsonFile(target) as PluginManifest;
        manifest.entry=path.resolve(path.dirname(path.resolve(target)),manifest.entry);
      }
      if (action === 'install') await host.install(manifest, { enabled: args.flags.has('enable'), installationSource: args.flags.has('bundled')?'distribution':'manual' });
      else {
        // Migration must not race a running provider in a different process.
        const { acquireServerLease } = await import('./runtime-lease.js');
        release = await acquireServerLease(root);
        await host.upgrade(manifest,{installationSource:args.flags.has('bundled')?'distribution':'manual'});
      }
    } else if (action === 'configure') {
      if (!extra) throw new CapabilityError('INVALID_ARGUMENT', 'configure requires a settings JSON file');
      const settings = await jsonFile(extra);
      if (!settings || Array.isArray(settings) || typeof settings !== 'object') throw new CapabilityError('INVALID_ARGUMENT', 'Plugin settings must be an object');
      const record = host.list().find(item => item.manifest.id === target);
      if (record?.enabled) throw new CapabilityError('PLUGIN_STILL_ENABLED', 'Disable the plugin before changing its settings');
      await host.configure(target, settings);
    } else if (['enable', 'disable', 'uninstall'].includes(action)) {
      await host[action as 'enable' | 'disable' | 'uninstall'](target);
    } else if (action === 'purge') {
      const { acquireServerLease } = await import('./runtime-lease.js');
      release = await acquireServerLease(root); await host.purge(target);
    } else throw new CapabilityError('INVALID_ARGUMENT', 'Unknown plugins action: ' + action);
  } finally { await host.close(); await release?.(); }
  print({ ok: true, action, plugins: host.list() });
}

async function knowledgeCli(args: Parsed): Promise<void> {
  const root = stateRoot(args, true), [action = 'search', payload = '{}'] = args.positional;
  if (!['search', 'list', 'read', 'ingest', 'learn', 'correct', 'skills'].includes(action)) throw new CapabilityError('INVALID_ARGUMENT', 'Unknown knowledge action');
  const input = payload.startsWith('@') ? await jsonFile(payload.slice(1)) : JSON.parse(payload);
  const { acquireServerLease } = await import('./runtime-lease.js');
  const release = await acquireServerLease(root);
  let host: import('./plugin-host.js').PluginHost | undefined;
  try {
    const { GatewayDeviceIdentity } = await import('../device/gateway-identity.js');
    const { PluginHost } = await import('./plugin-host.js');
    const { bundledPluginManifests } = await import('../distribution.js');
    const record = await new GatewayDeviceIdentity(path.join(root, 'gateway-identity.json')).loadOrCreate();
    host = new PluginHost({ stateRoot: root, deviceId: record.deviceId, projectRoots: args.values['project-root']?.map(item => path.resolve(item)), sourceRoots: args.values['source-root']?.map(item => path.resolve(item)) ?? [path.join(root, 'logs')] });
    await host.ensureBundled(bundledPluginManifests()); await host.start(); print(await host.invoke('knowledge.' + action, input));
  } finally { await host?.close(); await release(); }
}

async function adminCli(args: Parsed): Promise<void> {
  const root = stateRoot(args, true);
  await fs.mkdir(root, { recursive: true });
  const child = spawn(process.execPath, [path.join(packageRoot, 'control-plane', 'scripts', 'admin.mjs'), ...args.positional], {
    stdio: 'inherit', windowsHide: true, env: { ...process.env, MCP_RUNTIME_DIR: root, MCP_GATEWAY_DB_PATH: path.join(root, 'gateway.sqlite') }
  });
  await new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => { child.once('error', reject); child.once('exit', code => { process.exitCode = code ?? 1; resolve(); }); });
}

async function serverCli(args: Parsed): Promise<void> {
  if (args.positional.length && !(args.positional.length === 1 && args.positional[0] === 'start')) throw new CapabilityError('INVALID_ARGUMENT', 'server accepts only start; use Ctrl-C or the service manager to stop');
  const root = stateRoot(args, true), port = Number(one(args, 'port', '8104'));
  if (!Number.isInteger(port) || port < 1 || port > 65535) throw new CapabilityError('INVALID_ARGUMENT', 'Invalid server port');
  const localPortInput = one(args, 'loopback-port');
  const loopbackPort = localPortInput === undefined ? undefined : Number(localPortInput);
  if (loopbackPort !== undefined && (!Number.isInteger(loopbackPort) || loopbackPort < 1 || loopbackPort > 65535 || loopbackPort === port))
    throw new CapabilityError('INVALID_ARGUMENT', 'Loopback MCP port must be a distinct port in the range 1..65535');
  const { acquireServerLease } = await import('./runtime-lease.js');
  const release = await acquireServerLease(root);
  process.env.MCP_DEVICE_CONFIG_DIR = root;
  let core: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof import('./device-runtime.js').createPlatformDeviceCore>> | undefined;
  let runtime: any;
  try {
    const config = one(args, 'config') ? await jsonFile(one(args, 'config')!) : {};
    if (!config || typeof config !== 'object' || Array.isArray(config) || (config.env && (typeof config.env !== 'object' || Array.isArray(config.env)))) throw new CapabilityError('INVALID_ARGUMENT', 'Server config must be a JSON object with an optional env object');
    const publicUrl = one(args, 'public-url');
    const allowHttpLan = args.flags.has('allow-http-lan');
    if (allowHttpLan) {
      if (!publicUrl) throw new CapabilityError('INVALID_ARGUMENT', '--allow-http-lan requires --public-url http://<lan-host>:<port>');
      let url: URL;
      try { url = new URL(publicUrl); } catch { throw new CapabilityError('INVALID_ARGUMENT', '--public-url must be a valid absolute URL'); }
      if (url.protocol !== 'http:') throw new CapabilityError('INVALID_ARGUMENT', '--allow-http-lan is only for an explicit http:// LAN public URL');
    }
    const configuredSkillRoot = one(args, 'skill-root', process.env.MCP_SKILL_ROOT);
    const legacySharedSkillRoot = path.join(os.homedir(), '.mcp-device', 'skills');
    const skillRoot = configuredSkillRoot
      ? path.resolve(configuredSkillRoot)
      : await fs.stat(legacySharedSkillRoot).then(stat => stat.isDirectory() ? legacySharedSkillRoot : undefined).catch(() => undefined);
    const { GatewayDeviceIdentity } = await import('../device/gateway-identity.js');
    const identity = new GatewayDeviceIdentity(path.join(root, 'gateway-identity.json'));
    const record = await identity.loadOrCreate();
    const { createPlatformDeviceCore } = await import('./device-runtime.js');
    const legacyKnowledgeMcp = args.flags.has('legacy-knowledge-mcp');
    core = await createPlatformDeviceCore({ deviceId: record.deviceId, stateRoot: root, projectRoots: args.values['project-root']?.map(item => path.resolve(item)), sourceRoots: args.values['source-root']?.map(item => path.resolve(item)), exposeKnowledgeMcp: legacyKnowledgeMcp });
    const { createPlatformTools } = await import('./knowledge-mcp.js');
    const { startServerRuntime } = await import(pathToFileURL(path.join(packageRoot, 'control-plane', 'scripts', 'authenticated-mcp-wrapper.mjs')).href);
    const configPath = typeof config.configPath === 'string' ? path.resolve(path.dirname(path.resolve(one(args, 'config')!)), config.configPath) : undefined;
    runtime = await startServerRuntime({ core, identity, stateRoot: root, packageRoot, port, loopbackPort, host: one(args, 'host', '127.0.0.1'), publicUrl, allowHttpLan, skillRoot, env: config.env ?? {}, configPath, extraTools: legacyKnowledgeMcp ? await createPlatformTools(root) : [],
      deviceName: 'localhost',
      onPairing: (pairing: unknown) => event({ event: 'device_pairing_required', pairing }) });
    event({ event: 'server_ready', version: '1.0.12', url: runtime.baseUrl, ...(runtime.loopbackUrl ? { loopback_url: runtime.loopbackUrl } : {}), device_id: record.deviceId, runtime_dir: root, local_runtime: 'shared-device-core', authentication: 'account-oauth-and-pairing' });
    await new Promise<void>(resolve => {
      // A process manager may use Node's private parent/child IPC channel to stop
      // gracefully on Windows, where kill(SIGTERM) otherwise terminates abruptly.
      const message = (value: unknown) => { if ((value as { type?: string })?.type === 'mcp-device:shutdown') stop(); };
      const stop = () => { process.removeListener('SIGINT', stop); process.removeListener('SIGTERM', stop); process.removeListener('message', message); process.removeListener('disconnect', stop); resolve(); };
      process.once('SIGINT', stop); process.once('SIGTERM', stop);
      if (process.send) { process.on('message', message); process.once('disconnect', stop); }
      runtime.server.once('close', stop);
    });
  } finally { if (runtime) await runtime.close(); else await core?.close(); await release(); if (process.connected) process.disconnect(); }
}

async function stateCli(args: Parsed): Promise<void> {
  // The implementation is imported only for explicit state-management commands.
  const modulePath = new URL('./state-backup.js', import.meta.url).href;
  const state = await import(modulePath);
  const [action] = args.positional;
  if (action === 'backup') print(await state.createStateBackup({ sourceRoot: required(args, 'source'), backupRoot: required(args, 'backup') }));
  else if (action === 'verify') print(await state.verifyStateBackup({ backupRoot: required(args, 'backup') }));
  else if (action === 'restore') print(await state.restoreStateBackup({ backupRoot: required(args, 'backup'), targetRoot: required(args, 'target') }));
  else if (action === 'migrate') {
    const options = { deviceRoot: required(args, 'device-root'), gatewayRoot: required(args, 'gateway-root'), targetRoot: required(args, 'target'), backupRoot: required(args, 'backup') };
    const plan = await state.planLegacyMigration(options);
    print(args.flags.has('apply') ? await state.applyLegacyMigration({ plan }) : plan);
  } else throw new CapabilityError('INVALID_ARGUMENT', 'Use state backup, verify, restore or migrate');
}

export async function runPlatformCli(command: string, argv: string[]): Promise<void> {
  try {
    const args = parse(argv);
    if (command === 'help' || args.flags.has('help')) { process.stdout.write(usage); return; }
    if (command === 'server') await serverCli(args);
    else if (command === 'plugins') await pluginCli(args);
    else if (command === 'knowledge') await knowledgeCli(args);
    else if (command === 'admin') await adminCli(args);
    else if (command === 'state') await stateCli(args);
    else throw new CapabilityError('INVALID_ARGUMENT', 'Unknown platform command');
  } catch (error) { process.stderr.write(JSON.stringify({ ok: false, code: (error as CapabilityError).code ?? 'COMMAND_FAILED', error: (error as Error).message }) + '\n'); process.exitCode = 1; }
}
