===== FILE src/platform/plugin-driver.ts sha256=c665d203ef6d50c2d970b9a632283817e7b1428f9a5273674cec58758611821e =====
import { spawn, type ChildProcessWithoutNullStreams } from 'node:child_process';
import path from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath, pathToFileURL } from 'node:url';
import { randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';
import { CapabilityError, type PluginContext, type PluginInstance, type PluginManifest, type PluginModule } from './plugin-contract.js';
import { createPluginServices, type DependencyInvoker } from './plugin-services.js';

export interface PluginDriver {
  start(): Promise<void>;
  invoke(capability: string, input: unknown, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<unknown>;
  stop(): Promise<void>;
}
const boundedTimeout = (manifest: PluginManifest) => Math.min(120_000, Math.max(50, manifest.timeoutMs ?? 15_000));
const unavailable: DependencyInvoker = async () => { throw new CapabilityError('CAPABILITY_DEPENDENCY_UNAVAILABLE', 'Dependency services are unavailable'); };
const MAX_FRAME = 2 * 1024 * 1024;
interface PendingRequest {
  method: string;
  controller: AbortController;
  resolve(value: unknown): void;
  reject(error: Error): void;
  timer: NodeJS.Timeout;
  deadlineAt: number;
  cleanup(): void;
}
interface CancellingRequest { timer: NodeJS.Timeout; }
interface HostCall { parentId: string; controller: AbortController; cleanup(): void; }

class ProcessDriver implements PluginDriver {
  private child?: ChildProcessWithoutNullStreams;
  private pending = new Map<string, PendingRequest>();
  private cancelling = new Map<string, CancellingRequest>();
  private cancelAcks = new Map<string, string>();
  private hostCalls = new Map<string, HostCall>();
  private buffer = '';
  private stopping = false;
  private closing = false;
  private stopRequest?: Promise<void>;
  private closed?: Promise<void>;
  private termination?: Promise<void>;
  constructor(private manifest: PluginManifest, private context: PluginContext, private fault: (error: Error) => void,
    private invokeDependency: DependencyInvoker = unavailable) {}
  async start(): Promise<void> {
    const executable = this.manifest.executable ?? process.execPath;
    const args = this.manifest.executable ? (this.manifest.args ?? []) : [fileURLToPath(new URL('./plugin-child.js', import.meta.url))];
    this.child = spawn(executable, args, { cwd: this.context.dataRoot, stdio: ['pipe', 'pipe', 'pipe'], windowsHide: true, detached: process.platform !== 'win32' });
    this.closed = new Promise(resolve => this.child!.once('close', () => resolve()));
    this.child.stdout.setEncoding('utf8');
    this.child.stdout.on('data', (text: string) => {
      this.buffer += text;
      if (Buffer.byteLength(this.buffer) > MAX_FRAME) { this.fail(new CapabilityError('PLUGIN_OUTPUT_LIMIT', 'Plugin output exceeded frame budget')); return; }
      let end: number;
      while ((end = this.buffer.indexOf('\n')) !== -1) {
        const line = this.buffer.slice(0, end); this.buffer = this.buffer.slice(end + 1);
        if (!line.trim()) continue;
        try {
          const message = JSON.parse(line);
          if (message?.type === 'host_call') { this.receiveHostCall(message); continue; }
          if (message?.type === 'host_cancel') {
            const call = this.hostCalls.get(message.id);
            if (call && call.parentId === message.parent_id) call.controller.abort();
            continue;
          }
          if (message?.type !== undefined && message.type !== 'response') throw new Error('Unexpected plugin frame type');
          const pending = this.pending.get(message?.id);
          if (!pending) {
            const responseId=String(message?.id??'');
            const acknowledgedTarget=this.cancelAcks.get(responseId);
            if(acknowledgedTarget){
              // A cooperative cancel acknowledgement proves only that the child
              // received the signal. The original work remains supervised until
              // its own response arrives or the bounded cancellation grace ends.
              this.cancelAcks.delete(responseId);
              continue;
            }
            if(this.cancelling.has(responseId)){
              const cancelling=this.cancelling.get(responseId);
              if(cancelling)clearTimeout(cancelling.timer);
              this.cancelling.delete(responseId);
              for(const [cancelId,targetId] of this.cancelAcks)if(targetId===responseId)this.cancelAcks.delete(cancelId);
            }
            continue;
          }
          this.pending.delete(message.id); clearTimeout(pending.timer); pending.cleanup();
          if (message.error) {
            const error = new CapabilityError(String(message.error.code ?? 'PLUGIN_CALL_FAILED'), String(message.error.message));
            pending.reject(error);
            if (error.code.startsWith('PLUGIN_')) this.fail(error);
          } else pending.resolve(message.result);
        } catch { this.fail(new CapabilityError('PLUGIN_PROTOCOL_ERROR', 'Plugin emitted an invalid protocol frame')); return; }
      }
    });
    this.child.stderr.resume();
    this.child.on('error', error => this.fail(error));
    this.child.on('exit', (code, signal) => {
      if (!this.stopping) this.fail(new CapabilityError('PLUGIN_EXITED', `Plugin exited (${code ?? signal})`));
      else this.rejectAll(new CapabilityError('PLUGIN_STOPPED', 'Plugin stopped'));
    });
    const { services: _services, ...context } = this.context;
    await this.request('init', { entry: this.manifest.entry, context, schemaVersion: this.manifest.dataSchemaVersion, requires: this.manifest.requires ?? [] });
  }
  private sendHostResult(id: string, result?: unknown, error?: unknown): void {
    if (!this.child?.stdin.writable || this.stopping) return;
    const failure = error as (Error & { code?: string }) | undefined;
    let frame: string;
    try {
      frame = JSON.stringify(failure
        ? { type: 'host_result', id, error: { code: failure.code ?? 'CAPABILITY_CALL_FAILED', message: failure.message ?? String(failure) } }
        : { type: 'host_result', id, result }) + '\n';
      if (Buffer.byteLength(frame) > MAX_FRAME) throw new Error('Dependency result exceeded frame budget');
    } catch {
      frame = JSON.stringify({ type: 'host_result', id, error: { code: 'CAPABILITY_OUTPUT_LIMIT', message: 'Dependency result is not a bounded JSON value' } }) + '\n';
    }
    this.child.stdin.write(frame, error => { if (error) this.fail(error); });
  }
  private receiveHostCall(message: any): void {
    if (typeof message.id !== 'string' || !message.id || message.id.length > 128 || typeof message.parent_id !== 'string'
      || message.method !== 'capability.invoke' || typeof message.params?.name !== 'string' || this.hostCalls.has(message.id)) {
      this.fail(new CapabilityError('PLUGIN_PROTOCOL_ERROR', 'Invalid or duplicate dependency callback')); return;
    }
    const parent = this.pending.get(message.parent_id);
    if (!parent || parent.controller.signal.aborted || this.stopping) {
      this.sendHostResult(message.id, undefined, new CapabilityError('CAPABILITY_PARENT_EXPIRED', 'Dependency callback has no active parent request')); return;
    }
    if (this.hostCalls.size >= 64) {
      this.sendHostResult(message.id, undefined, new CapabilityError('CAPABILITY_CALL_LIMIT', 'Too many concurrent dependency callbacks')); return;
    }
    const controller = new AbortController();
    const abort = () => controller.abort();
    parent.controller.signal.addEventListener('abort', abort, { once: true });
    const call: HostCall = { parentId: message.parent_id, controller, cleanup: () => {
      parent.controller.signal.removeEventListener('abort', abort); controller.abort();
    } };
    this.hostCalls.set(message.id, call);
    const services = createPluginServices(this.manifest.requires ?? [], this.invokeDependency, controller.signal);
    void services.invoke(message.params.name, message.params.input).then(
      result => { if (this.pending.has(call.parentId) && !controller.signal.aborted) this.sendHostResult(message.id, result); },
      error => { if (this.pending.has(call.parentId)) this.sendHostResult(message.id, undefined, error); }
    ).finally(() => { if (this.hostCalls.get(message.id) === call) this.hostCalls.delete(message.id); call.cleanup(); });
  }
  private rejectAll(error: Error): void {
    const requests = [...this.pending.values()]; this.pending.clear();
    // Settle ordinary callers before aborting callbacks: graceful child shutdown
    // must not race a CANCELLED response against the host's PLUGIN_STOPPED result.
    for (const pending of requests) { clearTimeout(pending.timer); pending.reject(error); pending.cleanup(); }
    for(const cancelling of this.cancelling.values())clearTimeout(cancelling.timer);this.cancelling.clear();this.cancelAcks.clear();
    const callbacks = [...this.hostCalls.values()]; this.hostCalls.clear();
    for (const call of callbacks) call.cleanup();
  }
  private terminate(): Promise<void> {
    if (this.termination) return this.termination;
    const attempt = this.terminateProcess(); this.termination = attempt;
    void attempt.catch(() => { if (this.termination === attempt) this.termination = undefined; });
    return attempt;
  }
  private async terminateProcess(): Promise<void> {
    const child = this.child;
    if (!child) return;
    if (child.pid && child.exitCode === null) {
      if (process.platform === 'win32') {
        const taskkill = path.join(process.env.SystemRoot ?? process.env.SYSTEMROOT ?? 'C:\\Windows', 'System32', 'taskkill.exe');
        const fallback = () => { if (child.exitCode === null) { try { child.kill('SIGKILL'); } catch { /* close evidence remains mandatory */ } } };
        const killer = spawn(taskkill, ['/PID', String(child.pid), '/T', '/F'], { stdio: 'ignore', windowsHide: true });
        killer.once('error', fallback);
        killer.once('close', code => { if (code !== 0) fallback(); });
        killer.unref();
      } else {
        try { process.kill(-child.pid, 'SIGKILL'); } catch { child.kill('SIGKILL'); }
      }
    }
    let timer: NodeJS.Timeout | undefined;
    try { await Promise.race([this.closed, new Promise<void>((_, reject) => { timer = setTimeout(() => reject(new CapabilityError('PLUGIN_TERMINATION_FAILED', 'Plugin process did not release its resources')), 5000); })]); }
    finally { if (timer) clearTimeout(timer); }
  }
  private fail(error: Error): void {
    if (this.stopping) return;
    this.stopping = true; this.rejectAll(error); void this.terminate().catch(() => {}); this.fault(error);
  }
  private request(method: string, params: unknown, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<unknown> {
    if (signal?.aborted) return Promise.reject(new CapabilityError('CANCELLED', 'Plugin call cancelled'));
    if (this.closing && method !== 'stop') return Promise.reject(new CapabilityError('PLUGIN_STOPPED', 'Plugin is stopping'));
    if (!this.child?.stdin.writable || this.stopping) return Promise.reject(new CapabilityError('PLUGIN_UNAVAILABLE', 'Plugin process unavailable'));
    const id = randomUUID(), controller = new AbortController();
    const frame = JSON.stringify({ id, method, params }) + '\n';
    if (Buffer.byteLength(frame) > MAX_FRAME) return Promise.reject(new CapabilityError('PLUGIN_INPUT_LIMIT', 'Plugin request exceeds frame budget'));
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      const onAbort = () => {
        const active=this.pending.get(id);if(!active)return;
        this.pending.delete(id);clearTimeout(active.timer);
        active.reject(new CapabilityError('CANCELLED','Plugin call cancelled'));
        active.cleanup();
        if(!this.child?.stdin.writable||this.stopping)return;
        if(this.cancelling.size>=64){this.fail(new CapabilityError('PLUGIN_CALL_LIMIT','Too many incomplete cancelled requests'));return;}
        const graceMs=Math.max(1,Math.min(5000,active.deadlineAt-Date.now()));
        const grace=setTimeout(()=>{
          this.cancelling.delete(id);
          this.fail(new CapabilityError('PLUGIN_TIMEOUT','Plugin did not finish cancelled work before the grace deadline'));
        },graceMs);
        this.cancelling.set(id,{timer:grace});
        const cancelId=randomUUID();this.cancelAcks.set(cancelId,id);
        const cancelFrame=JSON.stringify({id:cancelId,method:'cancel',params:{id}})+'\n';
        this.child.stdin.write(cancelFrame,error=>{if(error)this.fail(error);});
      };
      const timeoutMs=boundedTimeout(this.manifest),deadlineAt=Date.now()+timeoutMs;
      const timer = setTimeout(() => this.fail(new CapabilityError('PLUGIN_TIMEOUT', 'Plugin exceeded its execution deadline')), timeoutMs);
      this.pending.set(id, { method, controller, resolve, reject, timer, deadlineAt, cleanup: () => {
        signal?.removeEventListener('abort', onAbort); controller.abort();
      } });
      signal?.addEventListener('abort', onAbort, { once: true });
      this.child!.stdin.write(frame, error => { if (error) this.fail(error); });
    });
  }
  invoke(capability: string, input: unknown, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<unknown> { return this.request('invoke', { capability, input }, signal); }
  stop(): Promise<void> {
    if (!this.stopRequest) {
      const attempt = this.stopProcess(); this.stopRequest = attempt;
      void attempt.catch(() => { if (this.stopRequest === attempt) this.stopRequest = undefined; });
    }
    return this.stopRequest;
  }
  private async stopProcess(): Promise<void> {
    if (this.stopping) { await this.terminate(); return; }
    this.closing = true;
    this.rejectAll(new CapabilityError('PLUGIN_STOPPED', 'Plugin stopped'));
    // This new lifecycle request retains its own deadline and failure path.
    try { await this.request('stop', {}); } catch { /* bounded stop, then terminate */ }
    this.stopping = true; this.rejectAll(new CapabilityError('PLUGIN_STOPPED', 'Plugin stopped')); await this.terminate();
  }
}

class CooperativeDriver implements PluginDriver {
  private instance?: PluginInstance;
  private stopping = false;
  private stopRequest?: Promise<void>;
  private controllers = new Set<AbortController>();
  constructor(private manifest: PluginManifest, private context: PluginContext, private fault: (error: Error) => void,
    private invokeDependency: DependencyInvoker = unavailable) {}
  private services(signal: AbortSignal) { return createPluginServices(this.manifest.requires ?? [], this.invokeDependency, signal); }
  private async bounded<T>(work: (signal: AbortSignal) => Promise<T> | T, outer?: AbortSignal): Promise<T> {
    if (outer?.aborted) throw new CapabilityError('CANCELLED', 'Plugin call cancelled');
    const controller = new AbortController(); this.controllers.add(controller);
    let timer: NodeJS.Timeout | undefined;
    let cancel: (() => void) | undefined;
    let onInnerAbort: (() => void) | undefined;
    try {
      return await Promise.race([
        Promise.resolve().then(() => {
          if (controller.signal.aborted) throw new CapabilityError('CANCELLED', 'Plugin call cancelled before execution');
          return work(controller.signal);
        }),
        new Promise<T>((_, reject) => {
          onInnerAbort = () => reject(new CapabilityError('CANCELLED', 'Plugin invocation scope ended'));
          controller.signal.addEventListener('abort', onInnerAbort, { once: true });
          timer = setTimeout(() => { reject(new CapabilityError('PLUGIN_TIMEOUT', 'Cooperative plugin deadline exceeded')); controller.abort(); }, boundedTimeout(this.manifest));
          cancel = () => controller.abort();
          outer?.addEventListener('abort', cancel, { once: true });
        })
      ]);
    } finally {
      if (timer) clearTimeout(timer);
      if (cancel) outer?.removeEventListener('abort', cancel);
      if (onInnerAbort) controller.signal.removeEventListener('abort', onInnerAbort);
      controller.abort(); this.controllers.delete(controller);
    }
  }
  async start(): Promise<void> {
    const mod = await import(pathToFileURL(this.manifest.entry).href + '?instance=' + randomUUID()) as PluginModule;
    if (this.context.previousSchemaVersion !== undefined && this.context.previousSchemaVersion !== this.manifest.dataSchemaVersion) {
      if (!mod.migrate) throw new CapabilityError('PLUGIN_MIGRATION_REQUIRED', 'Data schema changed without migration');
      await this.bounded(() => mod.migrate!({ dataRoot: this.context.dataRoot, fromVersion: this.context.previousSchemaVersion!, toVersion: this.manifest.dataSchemaVersion }));
    }
    this.instance = await this.bounded(signal => mod.createPlugin({ ...this.context, services: this.services(signal) }));
    await this.bounded(signal => this.instance!.start?.({ signal, services: this.services(signal) }));
  }
  async invoke(capability: string, input: unknown, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<unknown> {
    if (!this.instance || this.stopping) throw new CapabilityError('PLUGIN_UNAVAILABLE', 'Plugin not initialized or stopping');
    try { return await this.bounded(inner => this.instance!.invoke(capability, input, { signal: inner, services: this.services(inner) }), signal); }
    catch (error) { const code = (error as CapabilityError).code; if (!code || code.startsWith('PLUGIN_')) this.fault(error as Error); throw error; }
  }
  stop(): Promise<void> {
    if (!this.stopRequest) {
      const instance = this.instance; this.stopping = true;
      for (const controller of this.controllers) controller.abort();
      const attempt = (async () => {
        if (instance) await this.bounded(signal => instance.stop?.({ signal, services: this.services(signal) }));
        this.instance = undefined;
      })();
      this.stopRequest = attempt;
      void attempt.catch(() => { if (this.stopRequest === attempt) this.stopRequest = undefined; });
    }
    return this.stopRequest;
  }
}

export function createPluginDriver(manifest: PluginManifest, context: PluginContext, fault: (error: Error) => void,
  invokeDependency: DependencyInvoker = unavailable): PluginDriver {
  return manifest.isolation === 'process'
    ? new ProcessDriver(manifest, context, fault, invokeDependency)
    : new CooperativeDriver(manifest, context, fault, invokeDependency);
}

import fs from 'node:fs/promises';
import path from 'node:path';
import { randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';
import { CapabilityError, compatible, validateManifest, type Capability, type PluginManifest, type PluginRecord } from './plugin-contract.js';
import { createPluginDriver, type PluginDriver } from './plugin-driver.js';
import { coordinationOwnerAbandoned, processAppearsDead, repairCoordinationLock, withCoordinationLock } from './coordination-lock.js';
import { processBirth, processNamespace, type ProcessNamespace } from './process-identity.js';

type StoredPluginRecord = PluginRecord & { settings?: Record<string, unknown> };
interface Registry { version: 1; revision: number; plugins: Record<string, StoredPluginRecord>; }
type UpgradePhase = 'preparing' | 'candidate-ready' | 'swapping' | 'swapped' | 'rollback' | 'committed';
interface UpgradeIntent {
  version: 1; id: string; token: string; pid: number; startedAt: number; processBirth?:string; namespace?:ProcessNamespace; phase: UpgradePhase;
  candidate: string; backup: string; failed: string;
  previous: StoredPluginRecord; next: StoredPluginRecord;
}
export interface PluginHostOptions { stateRoot: string; deviceId: string; projectRoots?: string[]; sourceRoots?: string[]; }
type Candidate = { id: string; capability: Capability };
type DependencyBinding = { providerId: string; driver: PluginDriver; requiredVersion: string };

function canonical(value: unknown): unknown {
  if (Array.isArray(value)) return value.map(canonical);
  if (value && typeof value === 'object') return Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(value)
    .filter(([, item]) => item !== undefined).sort(([a], [b]) => a.localeCompare(b))
    .map(([key, item]) => [key, canonical(item)]));
  return value;
}
const sameValue = (a: unknown, b: unknown): boolean => JSON.stringify(canonical(a)) === JSON.stringify(canonical(b));
const activeUpgradeTokens = new Set<string>();
function bundleContract(manifest: PluginManifest, options: { ignoreVersion?: boolean } = {}): string {
  const { entry: _entry, version: _version, ...rest } = manifest;
  const contract = options.ignoreVersion ? rest : { ...rest, version: manifest.version };
  return JSON.stringify(canonical(contract));
}
async function pathExists(filename: string): Promise<boolean> {
  try { await fs.lstat(filename); return true; }
  catch (error) { if ((error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code === 'ENOENT') return false; throw error; }
}
async function renameWithRetry(source: string, target: string): Promise<void> {
  for (let attempt = 0; ; attempt++) {
    try { await fs.rename(source, target); return; }
    catch (error) {
      if (process.platform !== 'win32' || attempt >= 7 || !['EPERM', 'EBUSY', 'EACCES'].includes(String((error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code))) throw error;
      await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, Math.min(250, 20 * (2 ** attempt))));
    }
  }
}
async function writeJsonAtomic(filename: string, value: unknown): Promise<void> {
  await fs.mkdir(path.dirname(filename), { recursive: true });
  const temporary = filename + '.' + randomUUID() + '.tmp';
  const text = JSON.stringify(value, null, 2) + '\n';
  if (Buffer.byteLength(text) > 2 * 1024 * 1024) throw new CapabilityError('PLUGIN_UPGRADE_INTENT_INVALID', 'Plugin upgrade intent exceeds the bounded metadata limit');
  try {
    const handle = await fs.open(temporary, 'wx', 0o600);
    try { await handle.writeFile(text); await handle.sync(); } finally { await handle.close(); }
    await renameWithRetry(temporary, filename);
  } finally { await fs.rm(temporary, { force: true }); }
}

/** Private plugin state; neither accounts nor Device identity are owned here. */
export class PluginHost {
  private registry: Registry = { version: 1, revision: 0, plugins: {} };
  private drivers = new Map<string, PluginDriver>();
  // Faulted or disabled drivers remain owned until process/resource release is proved.
  private ownedDrivers = new Map<PluginDriver, string>();
  private dependencies = new Map<string, Map<string, DependencyBinding>>();
  private started = false;
  private refreshTimer?: NodeJS.Timeout;
  private operations: Promise<unknown> = Promise.resolve();
  private listeners = new Set<() => void>();
  readonly root: string;
  readonly registryPath: string;
  private notifyCapabilitiesChanged(): void { for (const listener of this.listeners) { try { listener(); } catch { /* observers do not own lifecycle */ } } }
  onCapabilitiesChanged(listener: () => void): () => void { this.listeners.add(listener); return () => this.listeners.delete(listener); }
  constructor(readonly options: PluginHostOptions) {
    this.root = path.resolve(options.stateRoot, 'plugins');
    this.registryPath = path.join(this.root, 'registry.json');
  }
  private pluginRoot(id: string): string { return path.join(this.root, id); }
  private upgradeIntentPath(id: string): string { return path.join(this.pluginRoot(id), 'upgrade-intent.json'); }
  private validateUpgradeIntent(value: unknown, id: string): UpgradeIntent {
    const intent=value as UpgradeIntent;
    const phases:UpgradePhase[]=['preparing','candidate-ready','swapping','swapped','rollback','committed'];
    if(!intent||intent.version!==1||intent.id!==id||!phases.includes(intent.phase)||
      !Number.isSafeInteger(intent.pid)||intent.pid<1||!Number.isFinite(intent.startedAt)||
      (intent.processBirth!==undefined&&(typeof intent.processBirth!=='string'||intent.processBirth.length>256))||
      (intent.namespace!==undefined&&(!intent.namespace||typeof intent.namespace.platform!=='string'||typeof intent.namespace.hostname!=='string'))||
      !/^[a-f0-9-]{36}$/i.test(intent.token)||intent.candidate!==`upgrade-candidate-${intent.token}`||
      intent.backup!==`upgrade-backup-${intent.token}`||intent.failed!==`upgrade-failed-${intent.token}`)
      throw new CapabilityError('PLUGIN_UPGRADE_RECOVERY_REQUIRED',`Invalid upgrade intent for ${id}`);
    validateManifest(intent.previous?.manifest);validateManifest(intent.next?.manifest);
    if(intent.previous.manifest.id!==id||intent.next.manifest.id!==id)
      throw new CapabilityError('PLUGIN_UPGRADE_RECOVERY_REQUIRED',`Upgrade intent identity mismatch for ${id}`);
    return intent;
  }
  private async writeUpgradeIntent(intent: UpgradeIntent): Promise<void> {
    await writeJsonAtomic(this.upgradeIntentPath(intent.id),intent);
  }
  private upgradePaths(intent: UpgradeIntent): { data:string;candidate:string;backup:string;failed:string;intentPath:string } {
    const root=this.pluginRoot(intent.id);
    return {data:path.join(root,'data'),candidate:path.join(root,intent.candidate),backup:path.join(root,intent.backup),failed:path.join(root,intent.failed),intentPath:this.upgradeIntentPath(intent.id)};
  }
  private recordGenerationMatches(actual: StoredPluginRecord|undefined, expected: StoredPluginRecord): boolean {
    // Activation may transiently change enabled/state/schema while validating a
    // new generation. Manifest plus provenance identifies the generation; the
    // complete prior record is restored on rollback.
    return !!actual&&sameValue(actual.manifest,expected.manifest)&&actual.installationSource===expected.installationSource;
  }
  private async rollbackUpgradeIntent(intent: UpgradeIntent): Promise<void> {
    const paths=this.upgradePaths(intent),current=this.registry.plugins[intent.id];
    if(!this.recordGenerationMatches(current,intent.previous)&&!this.recordGenerationMatches(current,intent.next))
      throw new CapabilityError('PLUGIN_UPGRADE_RECOVERY_REQUIRED',`Plugin ${intent.id} changed outside its pending upgrade`);
    intent.phase='rollback';await this.writeUpgradeIntent(intent);
    const backup=await pathExists(paths.backup),data=await pathExists(paths.data),failed=await pathExists(paths.failed);
    if(backup){
      if(data){if(failed)throw new CapabilityError('PLUGIN_UPGRADE_RECOVERY_REQUIRED',`Ambiguous rollback trees for ${intent.id}`);await renameWithRetry(paths.data,paths.failed);}
      await renameWithRetry(paths.backup,paths.data);
    }else if(!(data&&(failed||this.recordGenerationMatches(current,intent.previous)))){
      throw new CapabilityError('PLUGIN_UPGRADE_RECOVERY_REQUIRED',`Rollback backup is unavailable for ${intent.id}`);
    }
    this.registry.plugins[intent.id]=structuredClone(intent.previous);await this.save();
    await fs.rm(paths.failed,{recursive:true,force:true});await fs.rm(paths.candidate,{recursive:true,force:true});await fs.rm(paths.backup,{recursive:true,force:true});
    await fs.rm(paths.intentPath,{force:true});
  }
  private async recoverUpgradeIntent(intent: UpgradeIntent): Promise<void> {
    const paths=this.upgradePaths(intent);
    if(activeUpgradeTokens.has(intent.token))throw new CapabilityError('PLUGIN_UPGRADE_BUSY',`Plugin ${intent.id} is being upgraded in this process`);
    if(intent.pid!==process.pid){
      const legacy=!intent.processBirth||!intent.namespace;
      const abandoned=legacy?processAppearsDead(intent.pid):await coordinationOwnerAbandoned({pid:intent.pid,token:intent.token,processBirth:intent.processBirth,namespace:intent.namespace});
      if(!abandoned)throw new CapabilityError('PLUGIN_UPGRADE_BUSY',`Plugin ${intent.id} is being upgraded by a live or uninspectable owner PID ${intent.pid}`);
    }
    if(intent.phase==='committed'){
      if(!this.recordGenerationMatches(this.registry.plugins[intent.id],intent.next)||!await pathExists(paths.data))
        throw new CapabilityError('PLUGIN_UPGRADE_RECOVERY_REQUIRED',`Committed upgrade state is incomplete for ${intent.id}`);
      await fs.rm(paths.backup,{recursive:true,force:true});await fs.rm(paths.candidate,{recursive:true,force:true});await fs.rm(paths.failed,{recursive:true,force:true});await fs.rm(paths.intentPath,{force:true});return;
    }
    if(intent.phase==='preparing'||intent.phase==='candidate-ready'){
      if(await pathExists(paths.backup)||!await pathExists(paths.data)){await this.rollbackUpgradeIntent(intent);return;}
      if(!this.recordGenerationMatches(this.registry.plugins[intent.id],intent.previous))
        throw new CapabilityError('PLUGIN_UPGRADE_RECOVERY_REQUIRED',`Prepared upgrade registry changed for ${intent.id}`);
      await fs.rm(paths.candidate,{recursive:true,force:true});await fs.rm(paths.failed,{recursive:true,force:true});await fs.rm(paths.intentPath,{force:true});return;
    }
    if(intent.phase==='swapping'&&!await pathExists(paths.backup)&&await pathExists(paths.data)&&await pathExists(paths.candidate)){
      if(!this.recordGenerationMatches(this.registry.plugins[intent.id],intent.previous))
        throw new CapabilityError('PLUGIN_UPGRADE_RECOVERY_REQUIRED',`Upgrade registry changed before swap for ${intent.id}`);
      await fs.rm(paths.candidate,{recursive:true,force:true});await fs.rm(paths.intentPath,{force:true});return;
    }
    await this.rollbackUpgradeIntent(intent);
  }
  private async recoverUpgradeIntents(): Promise<void> {
    for(const id of Object.keys(this.registry.plugins)){
      const filename=this.upgradeIntentPath(id);if(!await pathExists(filename))continue;
      const stat=await fs.lstat(filename);if(!stat.isFile()||stat.isSymbolicLink()||stat.size>2*1024*1024)
        throw new CapabilityError('PLUGIN_UPGRADE_RECOVERY_REQUIRED',`Unsafe upgrade intent for ${id}`);
      const intent=this.validateUpgradeIntent(JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(filename,'utf8')),id);
      await this.recoverUpgradeIntent(intent);
    }
  }
  private serial<T>(operation: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {
    const next = this.operations.then(operation, operation); this.operations = next.catch(() => {}); return next;
  }
  async load(): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const parsed = JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(this.registryPath, 'utf8')) as Registry;
      if (parsed.version !== 1 || !Number.isSafeInteger(parsed.revision) || !parsed.plugins) throw new Error('Unsupported plugin registry');
      for (const [id, record] of Object.entries(parsed.plugins)) { validateManifest(record.manifest); if (id !== record.manifest.id) throw new Error('Plugin registry identity mismatch'); }
      this.registry = parsed;
      await this.recoverUpgradeIntents();
    } catch (error) { if ((error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code !== 'ENOENT') throw error; }
  }
  private registryLockOptions(){
    return {
      attempts:100,staleMs:5000,retainAbandoned:true,messagePrefix:'Plugin registry',
      codes:{busy:'PLUGIN_REGISTRY_BUSY',active:'PLUGIN_REGISTRY_LOCK_ACTIVE',recoveryRequired:'PLUGIN_REGISTRY_RECOVERY_REQUIRED'}
    } as const;
  }
  async repairRegistryLock():Promise<{recovered:boolean;reason?:string}>{
    await fs.mkdir(this.root,{recursive:true});
    return repairCoordinationLock(path.join(this.root,'registry.lock'),{...this.registryLockOptions(),breakLock:true});
  }
  private async save(): Promise<void> {
    await fs.mkdir(this.root,{recursive:true});
    const lockPath=path.join(this.root,'registry.lock');
    await withCoordinationLock(lockPath,async()=>{
      const temporary=this.registryPath+'.'+randomUUID()+'.tmp';
      try{
        let currentRevision=0;
        try{currentRevision=JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(this.registryPath,'utf8')).revision;}
        catch(error){if((error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code!=='ENOENT')throw error;}
        if(currentRevision!==this.registry.revision)throw new CapabilityError('PLUGIN_REGISTRY_CHANGED','Reload plugin registry before retrying the lifecycle change');
        const updated={...this.registry,revision:currentRevision+1};
        await fs.writeFile(temporary,JSON.stringify(updated,null,2)+'\n',{mode:0o600});
        await fs.rename(temporary,this.registryPath);this.registry.revision=updated.revision;this.notifyCapabilitiesChanged();
      }finally{await fs.rm(temporary,{force:true});}
    },this.registryLockOptions());
  }
  dataRoot(id: string): string { if (!/^[a-z][a-z0-9-]{0,63}$/.test(id)) throw new CapabilityError('PLUGIN_ID_INVALID', 'Invalid plugin id'); return path.join(this.root, id, 'data'); }
  list(): PluginRecord[] { return Object.values(this.registry.plugins).map(record => ({ ...record, manifest: structuredClone(record.manifest) })).sort((a, b) => a.manifest.id.localeCompare(b.manifest.id)); }
  async install(manifest: PluginManifest, options: { enabled?: boolean; settings?: Record<string, unknown>; installationSource?: 'distribution'|'manual' } = {}): Promise<void> {
    return this.serial(async () => {
      validateManifest(manifest); await this.load();
      const prior = this.registry.plugins[manifest.id];
      if (prior?.installed) {
        if (prior.manifest.version === manifest.version && prior.manifest.entry === manifest.entry) return;
        throw new CapabilityError('PLUGIN_UPGRADE_REQUIRED', 'Use upgrade to replace an installed plugin');
      }
      this.registry.plugins[manifest.id] = { manifest: structuredClone(manifest), installed: true, installationSource: options.installationSource ?? 'manual', enabled: options.enabled ?? false, schemaVersion: prior?.schemaVersion ?? manifest.dataSchemaVersion, state: options.enabled ? 'installed' : 'disabled', settings: options.settings ?? prior?.settings };
      await this.save();
      if (this.started && options.enabled) await this.activate(manifest.id, new Set());
    });
  }
  async ensureBundled(manifests: PluginManifest[]): Promise<void> {
    return this.serial(async () => {
      await this.load();
      for (const manifest of manifests) {
        validateManifest(manifest);
        const record = this.registry.plugins[manifest.id];
        if (!record) {
          this.registry.plugins[manifest.id] = {
            manifest: structuredClone(manifest), installed: true, enabled: true,
            installationSource: 'distribution', schemaVersion: manifest.dataSchemaVersion, state: 'installed'
          };
          await this.save();
          if (this.started) await this.activate(manifest.id, new Set()).catch(() => {});
          continue;
        }
        // A tombstone or user-managed installation is never claimed by the distribution.
        if (!record.installed || record.installationSource !== 'distribution') continue;
        const runtimeContractMatches=bundleContract(record.manifest,{ignoreVersion:true})===bundleContract(manifest,{ignoreVersion:true})
          && record.schemaVersion===manifest.dataSchemaVersion;
        if (!runtimeContractMatches) {
          const driver = this.drivers.get(manifest.id);
          this.drivers.delete(manifest.id); this.dependencies.delete(manifest.id); if(driver)this.notifyCapabilitiesChanged();
          await this.stopDriver(driver).catch(() => {});
          const gate = { version: manifest.version, dataSchemaVersion: manifest.dataSchemaVersion };
          const state = record.enabled ? 'faulted' : 'disabled';
          const error = 'PLUGIN_UPGRADE_REQUIRED: Bundled contract changed; run plugins upgrade '+manifest.id+' --bundled before loading this installation';
          const changed = JSON.stringify(record.bundleUpgradeRequired) !== JSON.stringify(gate)
            || record.state !== state || record.error !== error;
          record.bundleUpgradeRequired = gate; record.state = state; record.error = error;
          if (changed) await this.save();
          continue;
        }
        const needsRepin=bundleContract(record.manifest)!==bundleContract(manifest)
          || record.manifest.entry!==manifest.entry || Boolean(record.bundleUpgradeRequired);
        if (!needsRepin) continue;
        const driver = this.drivers.get(manifest.id);
        this.drivers.delete(manifest.id); this.dependencies.delete(manifest.id); if(driver)this.notifyCapabilitiesChanged();
        await this.stopDriver(driver).catch(() => {});
        // Version-only updates and package relocation keep the exact runtime/data contract.
        let oldEntryMissing = false;
        try { await fs.access(record.manifest.entry); }
        catch (error) { oldEntryMissing = (error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code === 'ENOENT'; }
        const recoveredMissingEntry = oldEntryMissing && record.state === 'faulted' && record.error?.startsWith('ENOENT:');
        const hadUpgradeGate=Boolean(record.bundleUpgradeRequired);
        record.manifest=structuredClone(manifest);delete record.bundleUpgradeRequired;
        if(driver||recoveredMissingEntry||hadUpgradeGate){
          record.state=record.enabled?'installed':'disabled';delete record.error;
        }
        await this.save();
        if (this.started && record.enabled)
          await this.activate(manifest.id, new Set()).catch(() => {});
      }
      for (const id of [...this.drivers.keys()]) if (!this.dependenciesAvailable(id))
        await this.markFault(id, new CapabilityError('CAPABILITY_DEPENDENCY_UNAVAILABLE', 'Bundled provider was replaced'));
    });
  }
  private candidates(name: string, version = '*', healthyOnly = true): Candidate[] {
    const result: Candidate[] = [];
    for (const [id, record] of Object.entries(this.registry.plugins)) {
      if (!record.installed || !record.enabled || record.bundleUpgradeRequired || (healthyOnly && (!this.drivers.has(id) || record.state !== 'enabled' || !this.dependenciesAvailable(id)))) continue;
      for (const capability of record.manifest.capabilities) if (capability.name === name && compatible(capability.version, version)) result.push({ id, capability });
    }
    return result.sort((a, b) => (b.capability.priority ?? 0) - (a.capability.priority ?? 0) || a.id.localeCompare(b.id));
  }
  capabilities(): string[] {
    const names = new Set<string>();
    for (const id of this.drivers.keys()) {
      const record = this.registry.plugins[id];
      if (record?.state !== 'enabled' || record.bundleUpgradeRequired || !this.dependenciesAvailable(id)) continue;
      for (const cap of record.manifest.capabilities) if (!['augmenter', 'observer'].includes(cap.role ?? 'provider')) names.add(cap.name);
    }
    return [...names].sort();
  }
  advertisements(): Capability[] {
    return this.capabilities().flatMap(name => this.candidates(name).filter(c => ['provider', 'fallback'].includes(c.capability.role ?? 'provider')).slice(0, 1).map(c => ({ ...c.capability })));
  }
  diagnostics(): { capability: string; code: string; providers: string[] }[] {
    const names = new Set(Object.values(this.registry.plugins).flatMap(r => r.manifest.capabilities.map(c => c.name)));
    return [...names].flatMap(name => {
      const providers = this.candidates(name).filter(c => (c.capability.role ?? 'provider') === 'provider');
      return providers.length > 1 && (providers[0].capability.priority ?? 0) === (providers[1].capability.priority ?? 0) ? [{ capability: name, code: 'PRIORITY_TIE_LEXICAL_ID', providers: providers.map(p => p.id) }] : [];
    });
  }
  private assertDependencyPath(id: string, visiting: Set<string>): void {
    if (visiting.has(id)) throw new CapabilityError('CAPABILITY_CYCLE', `Capability dependency cycle at ${id}`);
    const next = new Set(visiting).add(id);
    for (const dependency of this.dependencies.get(id)?.values() ?? []) this.assertDependencyPath(dependency.providerId, next);
  }
  private dependenciesAvailable(id: string, visiting = new Set<string>()): boolean {
    const record = this.registry.plugins[id];
    if (!record || record.bundleUpgradeRequired || visiting.has(id)) return false;
    const next = new Set(visiting).add(id);
    return (record.manifest.requires ?? []).every(requirement => {
      const binding = this.dependencies.get(id)?.get(requirement.name);
      return binding && binding.requiredVersion === requirement.version
        && this.drivers.get(binding.providerId) === binding.driver
        && this.registry.plugins[binding.providerId]?.state === 'enabled'
        && this.dependenciesAvailable(binding.providerId, next);
    });
  }
  private async activate(id: string, visiting: Set<string>): Promise<void> {
    const record = this.registry.plugins[id];
    if (record?.bundleUpgradeRequired) throw new CapabilityError('PLUGIN_UPGRADE_REQUIRED', 'Explicitly upgrade the changed bundled plugin before enabling it');
    if (this.drivers.has(id)) { this.assertDependencyPath(id, visiting); return; }
    if (!record?.installed || !record.enabled) throw new CapabilityError('PLUGIN_DISABLED', 'Plugin is not enabled');
    if (visiting.has(id)) throw new CapabilityError('CAPABILITY_CYCLE', `Capability dependency cycle at ${id}`);
    const next = new Set(visiting).add(id);
    const bindings = new Map<string, DependencyBinding>();
    try {
      for (const requirement of record.manifest.requires ?? []) {
        const providers = this.candidates(requirement.name, requirement.version, false).filter(c => ['provider', 'fallback'].includes(c.capability.role ?? 'provider'));
        if (!providers.length) throw new CapabilityError('CAPABILITY_DEPENDENCY_MISSING', `No compatible provider for ${requirement.name}`);
        const provider = providers[0]; await this.activate(provider.id, next);
        const providerDriver = this.drivers.get(provider.id);
        if (!providerDriver || !this.dependenciesAvailable(provider.id)) throw new CapabilityError('CAPABILITY_DEPENDENCY_UNAVAILABLE', 'Required provider is unavailable');
        bindings.set(requirement.name, { providerId: provider.id, driver: providerDriver, requiredVersion: requirement.version });
      }
      await this.stopOwnedDrivers(id);
      await fs.access(record.manifest.entry);
      await fs.mkdir(this.dataRoot(id), { recursive: true });
      const driver = createPluginDriver(record.manifest, {
        deviceId: this.options.deviceId, dataRoot: this.dataRoot(id), projectRoots: this.options.projectRoots ?? [], sourceRoots: this.options.sourceRoots ?? [], previousSchemaVersion: record.schemaVersion, settings: record.settings
      }, error => { void this.serial(() => this.drivers.get(id) === driver ? this.markFault(id, error) : Promise.resolve()); },
      async (name, input, options) => {
        const binding = this.dependencies.get(id)?.get(name);
        if (this.drivers.get(id) !== driver || !binding || binding.requiredVersion !== options.version
          || this.drivers.get(binding.providerId) !== binding.driver || !this.dependenciesAvailable(binding.providerId))
          throw new CapabilityError('CAPABILITY_DEPENDENCY_UNAVAILABLE', 'Bound dependency provider is unavailable');
        // Call the pinned provider directly; dependency services do not re-enter
        // augmenters/observers or select a new edge during an active invocation.
        return binding.driver.invoke(name, input, options.signal);
      });
      this.dependencies.set(id, bindings); this.drivers.set(id, driver); this.ownedDrivers.set(driver, id);
      await driver.start(); record.state = 'enabled'; record.schemaVersion = record.manifest.dataSchemaVersion; delete record.error; await this.save();
    } catch (error) { await this.markFault(id, error as Error); throw error; }
  }
  private async stopDriver(driver?: PluginDriver): Promise<void> {
    if (!driver) return;
    await driver.stop(); this.ownedDrivers.delete(driver);
  }
  private async stopOwnedDrivers(id: string): Promise<void> {
    await Promise.all([...this.ownedDrivers].filter(([, owner]) => owner === id).map(([driver]) => this.stopDriver(driver)));
  }
  private async markFault(id: string, error: Error): Promise<void> {
    const driver = this.drivers.get(id); this.drivers.delete(id); this.dependencies.delete(id);
    if (driver) this.notifyCapabilitiesChanged();
    const record = this.registry.plugins[id];
    if (record) { record.state = 'faulted'; record.error = `${(error as CapabilityError).code ?? 'PLUGIN_FAULT'}: ${error.message}`; await this.save().catch(() => {}); }
    await this.stopDriver(driver).catch(() => {});
    // Dependency loss degrades only dependents, not ordinary Device tools.
    for (const dependent of [...this.drivers.keys()]) {
      if (!this.dependenciesAvailable(dependent)) await this.markFault(dependent, new CapabilityError('CAPABILITY_DEPENDENCY_UNAVAILABLE', `A required capability became unavailable`));
    }
  }
  async start(): Promise<void> {
    await this.load(); this.started = true;
    for (const [id, record] of Object.entries(this.registry.plugins)) if (record.installed && record.enabled && record.state !== 'faulted') await this.activate(id, new Set()).catch(() => {});
    // Lifecycle changes from the CLI are atomically picked up without a second runtime.
    this.refreshTimer = setInterval(() => { void this.serial(() => this.refresh()).catch(() => {}); }, 1000); this.refreshTimer.unref();
  }
  private async refresh(): Promise<void> {
    const previous = this.registry; await this.load(); if (previous.revision === this.registry.revision) return;
    for (const [id, driver] of [...this.drivers]) {
      const current = this.registry.plugins[id];
      if (!current?.installed || !current.enabled || current.bundleUpgradeRequired || JSON.stringify(current.manifest) !== JSON.stringify(previous.plugins[id]?.manifest)) {
        this.drivers.delete(id); this.dependencies.delete(id); this.notifyCapabilitiesChanged(); await this.stopDriver(driver);
      } else current.state = 'enabled';
    }
    for (const [id, current] of Object.entries(this.registry.plugins)) if (current.installed && current.enabled && current.state !== 'faulted' && !this.drivers.has(id)) await this.activate(id, new Set()).catch(() => {});
    for (const id of [...this.drivers.keys()]) {
      if (!this.dependenciesAvailable(id)) await this.markFault(id, new CapabilityError('CAPABILITY_DEPENDENCY_UNAVAILABLE', 'A provider was disabled by another local process'));
    }
    this.notifyCapabilitiesChanged();
  }
  async enable(id: string): Promise<void> {
    return this.serial(async () => { await this.load(); const record = this.registry.plugins[id]; if (!record?.installed) throw new CapabilityError('PLUGIN_NOT_INSTALLED', 'Plugin is not installed'); if (record.bundleUpgradeRequired) throw new CapabilityError('PLUGIN_UPGRADE_REQUIRED', 'Explicitly upgrade the changed bundled plugin before enabling it'); record.enabled = true; record.state = 'installed'; delete record.error; await this.save(); if (this.started) await this.activate(id, new Set()); });
  }
  async disable(id: string): Promise<void> {
    return this.serial(async () => { await this.load(); const record = this.registry.plugins[id]; if (!record) throw new CapabilityError('PLUGIN_NOT_FOUND', 'Unknown plugin'); const driver=this.drivers.get(id);this.drivers.delete(id); this.dependencies.delete(id);if(driver)this.notifyCapabilitiesChanged(); await this.stopOwnedDrivers(id); record.enabled = false; record.state = 'disabled'; delete record.error; await this.save(); for (const dependent of [...this.drivers.keys()]) { if (!this.dependenciesAvailable(dependent)) await this.markFault(dependent, new CapabilityError('CAPABILITY_DEPENDENCY_UNAVAILABLE', 'Required provider disabled')); } });
  }
  async uninstall(id: string): Promise<void> {
    await this.disable(id);
    return this.serial(async () => { await this.load(); const record = this.registry.plugins[id]; record.installed = false; record.state = 'uninstalled'; await this.save(); });
  }
  async purge(id: string): Promise<void> {
    return this.serial(async () => { await this.load(); const record = this.registry.plugins[id]; if (record?.enabled || this.drivers.has(id)) throw new CapabilityError('PLUGIN_STILL_ENABLED', 'Disable plugin before explicitly purging its data'); await fs.rm(this.dataRoot(id), { recursive: true, force: true }); });
  }
  async configure(id: string, settings: Record<string, unknown>): Promise<void> {
    return this.serial(async () => { await this.load(); const record = this.registry.plugins[id]; if (!record) throw new CapabilityError('PLUGIN_NOT_FOUND', 'Unknown plugin'); if (this.drivers.has(id)) throw new CapabilityError('PLUGIN_STILL_ENABLED', 'Disable plugin before changing settings'); record.settings = structuredClone(settings); await this.save(); });
  }
  async upgrade(manifest: PluginManifest, options: { installationSource?: 'distribution' | 'manual' } = {}): Promise<void> {
    return this.serial(async () => {
      validateManifest(manifest); await this.load(); const old = this.registry.plugins[manifest.id];
      if (!old?.installed) throw new CapabilityError('PLUGIN_NOT_INSTALLED', 'Install the plugin first');
      const previous=structuredClone(old),next=structuredClone(old),token=randomUUID();
      const [birth,namespace]=await Promise.all([processBirth(process.pid),processNamespace()]);
      next.manifest=structuredClone(manifest);next.installationSource=options.installationSource??old.installationSource;
      next.state='installed';delete next.error;delete next.bundleUpgradeRequired;
      const intent:UpgradeIntent={version:1,id:manifest.id,token,pid:process.pid,startedAt:Date.now(),...(birth?{processBirth:birth}:{}),...(namespace?{namespace}:{}),phase:'preparing',
        candidate:`upgrade-candidate-${token}`,backup:`upgrade-backup-${token}`,failed:`upgrade-failed-${token}`,previous,next};
      const paths=this.upgradePaths(intent),driver=this.drivers.get(manifest.id);
      this.drivers.delete(manifest.id);this.dependencies.delete(manifest.id);if(driver)this.notifyCapabilitiesChanged();await this.stopOwnedDrivers(manifest.id);
      await fs.mkdir(paths.data,{recursive:true});activeUpgradeTokens.add(token);
      try {
        await this.writeUpgradeIntent(intent);
        await fs.cp(paths.data,paths.candidate,{recursive:true,force:false,errorOnExist:true});
        intent.phase='candidate-ready';await this.writeUpgradeIntent(intent);
        intent.phase='swapping';await this.writeUpgradeIntent(intent);
        await renameWithRetry(paths.data,paths.backup);await renameWithRetry(paths.candidate,paths.data);
        intent.phase='swapped';await this.writeUpgradeIntent(intent);
        this.registry.plugins[manifest.id]=structuredClone(next);await this.save();
        const current=this.registry.plugins[manifest.id],shouldEnable=previous.enabled;current.enabled=true;
        await this.activate(manifest.id,new Set());
        if(!this.started||!shouldEnable){
          const validationDriver=this.drivers.get(manifest.id);this.drivers.delete(manifest.id);this.dependencies.delete(manifest.id);
          if(validationDriver)this.notifyCapabilitiesChanged();await this.stopDriver(validationDriver);
          current.enabled=shouldEnable;current.state=shouldEnable?'installed':'disabled';await this.save();
        }
        intent.next=structuredClone(this.registry.plugins[manifest.id]);intent.phase='committed';await this.writeUpgradeIntent(intent);
        await fs.rm(paths.backup,{recursive:true,force:true});await fs.rm(paths.failed,{recursive:true,force:true});await fs.rm(paths.intentPath,{force:true});
      } catch(error) {
        this.drivers.delete(manifest.id);this.dependencies.delete(manifest.id);await this.stopOwnedDrivers(manifest.id).catch(()=>{});
        activeUpgradeTokens.delete(token);
        try {
          if(await pathExists(paths.intentPath)){
            const saved=this.validateUpgradeIntent(JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(paths.intentPath,'utf8')),manifest.id),committed=saved.phase==='committed';
            await this.recoverUpgradeIntent(saved);
            if(committed)return;
          }else this.registry.plugins[manifest.id]=previous;
          if(this.started&&previous.enabled)await this.activate(manifest.id,new Set()).catch(()=>{});
        }catch(recoveryError){
          throw new CapabilityError('PLUGIN_UPGRADE_RECOVERY_REQUIRED',`Upgrade failed and recovery requires inspection: ${String((recoveryError as Error).message??recoveryError)}`);
        }
        throw error;
      } finally {
        activeUpgradeTokens.delete(token);
        for (const dependent of [...this.drivers.keys()]) {
          if (!this.dependenciesAvailable(dependent)) await this.markFault(dependent,
            new CapabilityError('CAPABILITY_DEPENDENCY_UNAVAILABLE', 'Pinned provider changed during upgrade'));
        }
      }
    });
  }
  private unavailableCapability(name:string):CapabilityError {
    const records=Object.values(this.registry.plugins).filter(record=>record.manifest.capabilities.some(capability=>capability.name===name))
      .sort((a,b)=>(Math.max(...b.manifest.capabilities.filter(c=>c.name===name).map(c=>c.priority??0))-
        Math.max(...a.manifest.capabilities.filter(c=>c.name===name).map(c=>c.priority??0))));
    const record=records[0];
    if(!record)return new CapabilityError('CAPABILITY_UNAVAILABLE',`No compatible provider for ${name}`);
    if(!record.installed||record.state==='uninstalled')return new CapabilityError('PLUGIN_UNINSTALLED',`Plugin ${record.manifest.id} is uninstalled`);
    if(record.bundleUpgradeRequired)return new CapabilityError('PLUGIN_UPGRADE_REQUIRED',record.error??`Plugin ${record.manifest.id} requires an explicit bundled upgrade`);
    if(!record.enabled||record.state==='disabled')return new CapabilityError('PLUGIN_DISABLED',`Plugin ${record.manifest.id} is disabled`);
    if(record.state==='faulted')return new CapabilityError('PLUGIN_FAULTED',record.error??`Plugin ${record.manifest.id} is faulted`);
    return new CapabilityError('CAPABILITY_UNAVAILABLE',`No healthy compatible provider for ${name}`);
  }
  async invoke(name: string, input: unknown, options: { version?: string; signal?: AbortSignal } = {}): Promise<unknown> {
    const candidates = this.candidates(name, options.version ?? '*');
    const providers = candidates.filter(c => (c.capability.role ?? 'provider') === 'provider');
    const fallback = candidates.filter(c => c.capability.role === 'fallback');
    let result: unknown, succeeded = false, lastError: unknown;
    for (const candidate of [...providers, ...fallback]) {
      try { result = await this.drivers.get(candidate.id)!.invoke(name, input, options.signal); succeeded = true; break; }
      catch (error) { lastError = error; if (options.signal?.aborted) throw error; }
    }
    if (!succeeded) throw lastError ?? this.unavailableCapability(name);
    for (const candidate of candidates.filter(c => c.capability.role === 'augmenter')) {
      try { result = await this.drivers.get(candidate.id)?.invoke(name, { input, result }, options.signal) ?? result; } catch { /* provider result remains usable */ }
    }
    for (const candidate of candidates.filter(c => c.capability.role === 'observer')) {
      try { await this.drivers.get(candidate.id)?.invoke(name, { input, result }, options.signal); } catch { /* observations cannot invalidate a provider result */ }
    }
    return result;
  }
  async close(): Promise<void> {
    if (this.refreshTimer) clearInterval(this.refreshTimer); this.started = false;
    await this.operations;
    this.drivers.clear(); this.dependencies.clear();
    const released = await Promise.allSettled([...this.ownedDrivers.keys()].map(driver => this.stopDriver(driver)));
    const failures = released.filter((result): result is PromiseRejectedResult => result.status === 'rejected');
    if (failures.length) throw new CapabilityError('PLUGIN_TERMINATION_FAILED',
      `Cannot prove ${failures.length} plugin driver(s) released their resources; runtime ownership must remain held`);
  }
}

#!/usr/bin/env node
import { DatabaseSync } from 'node:sqlite';
import { existsSync, readFileSync, realpathSync } from 'node:fs';
import os from 'node:os';
import path from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { queryPalaceCards, searchPalaceCards } from './store.js';
import { palaceSkillStatus, palaceSkillProbe, reconcilePalaceSkill } from './skill-manager.js';
import { preview, boundedInt } from './util.js';
import type { KnowledgeCard } from './types.js';

// Standalone read-only CLI for the installable Palace plugin.
// Reads a live SQLite WAL snapshot safely, without invoking MCP or opening a second writer.
interface Params { command:string; positional:string[]; opts:Record<string,string|boolean> }
interface CliDetails { runtimeDir?:string|null;dataRoot?:string;pluginState?:string;runtimeActive?:boolean|null;remediation?:string }
class PalaceCliError extends Error {
  constructor(readonly code:string,message:string,readonly details:CliDetails={}){super(message);this.name='PalaceCliError';}
}
const packageRoot=fileURLToPath(new URL('../../../',import.meta.url));
function packageInfo():{packageName:string;packageVersion:string;installPath:string}{
  try {const pkg=JSON.parse(readFileSync(path.join(packageRoot,'package.json'),'utf8'));return {packageName:String(pkg.name??'unknown'),packageVersion:String(pkg.version??'unknown'),installPath:packageRoot};}
  catch{return {packageName:'unknown',packageVersion:'unknown',installPath:packageRoot};}
}
function parse(argv:string[]):Params {
  const positional:string[]=[]; const opts:Record<string,string|boolean>={};
  const valued=new Set(['runtime-dir','data-root','project-id','limit','max-bytes','target','forget-owner']);
  const flags=new Set(['include-candidates','include-inactive','full','help','break-lock','abandon-pending','all']);
  for(let i=0;i<argv.length;i++){
    const part=argv[i];
    if(!part.startsWith('--')){positional.push(part);continue;}
    const eq=part.indexOf('=');const key=part.slice(2,eq<0?undefined:eq);
    if(flags.has(key)&&eq<0){opts[key]=true;continue;}
    if(!valued.has(key))throw new PalaceCliError('UNKNOWN_OPTION','Unknown option: '+key);
    const value=eq<0?argv[++i]:part.slice(eq+1);
    if(!value||value.startsWith('--'))throw new PalaceCliError('MISSING_OPTION','Missing option: '+key);
    opts[key]=value;
  }
  return { command: positional.shift()??'status', positional, opts };
}
function checkedString(value:unknown,max=4096):string {
  if(typeof value!=='string'||Buffer.byteLength(value,'utf8')>max)throw new PalaceCliError('INVALID_ARGUMENT','Invalid string argument');
  return value;
}
function runtimeIsActive(runtime:string):boolean|null {
  const lock=path.join(runtime,'server-runtime.lock');
  if(!existsSync(lock))return existsSync(path.join(runtime,'runtime-control.json'))?null:false;
  try {
    const pid=Number(JSON.parse(readFileSync(lock,'utf8')).pid);
    if(!Number.isSafeInteger(pid)||pid<1)return null;
    try{process.kill(pid,0);return true;}catch(error){return (error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code==='ESRCH'?false:null;}
  }catch{return null;}
}
/** Recognize the generic host's existing private-data layout even through an
 * alias. An explicit directory is not permission to ignore a known disabled
 * runtime. Separately copied archives have no binding and remain read-only. */
function managedRuntime(dataRoot:string):string|null{
  let canonical:string;try{canonical=realpathSync(dataRoot);}catch{return null;}
  const palace=path.dirname(canonical),plugins=path.dirname(palace),runtime=path.dirname(plugins);
  const names=[path.basename(plugins),path.basename(palace),path.basename(canonical)].map(name=>process.platform==='win32'?name.toLowerCase():name);
  if(names.join('/')!=='plugins/palace/data')return null;
  return existsSync(path.join(runtime,'plugins','registry.json'))?runtime:null;
}
function lifecycle(runtime:string,explicitDataRoot:boolean,pkg:{packageName:string;installPath:string}):{pluginState:string;available:boolean;code?:string;remediation?:string}{
  if(explicitDataRoot)return {pluginState:'unknown',available:true};
  const registry=path.join(runtime,'plugins','registry.json');
  if(!existsSync(registry))return {pluginState:'unregistered',available:true};
  let record:any;
  try{record=JSON.parse(readFileSync(registry,'utf8'))?.plugins?.palace;}
  catch{throw new PalaceCliError('PALACE_REGISTRY_INVALID','Palace plugin registry is unreadable',{runtimeDir:runtime,remediation:'Inspect or restore plugins/registry.json before using Palace.'});}
  if(!record)return {pluginState:'not-installed',available:false,code:'PALACE_NOT_INSTALLED',remediation:`Install or restore Palace for runtime ${runtime}.`};
  if(!record.installed||record.state==='uninstalled'){
    const remediation=pkg.packageName==='@hcu-lab.me/mcp-device'
      ? `Run mcp-device plugins --runtime-dir "${runtime}" install palace --bundled --enable.`
      : `Register the standalone package with an existing Device host: mcp-device plugins --runtime-dir "${runtime}" install "${path.join(pkg.installPath,'palace.plugin.json')}" --enable. A standalone host without mcp-device has no mutation/re-registration path.`;
    return {pluginState:'uninstalled',available:false,code:'PALACE_UNINSTALLED',remediation};
  }
  if(record.bundleUpgradeRequired)return {pluginState:'upgrade-required',available:false,code:'PALACE_UPGRADE_REQUIRED',remediation:`Run mcp-device plugins --runtime-dir "${runtime}" upgrade palace --bundled.`};
  if(!record.enabled||record.state==='disabled')return {pluginState:'disabled',available:false,code:'PALACE_DISABLED',remediation:`Run mcp-device plugins --runtime-dir "${runtime}" enable palace.`};
  if(record.state==='faulted')return {pluginState:'faulted',available:false,code:'PALACE_FAULTED',remediation:`Inspect mcp-device plugins --runtime-dir "${runtime}" list and repair the recorded Palace fault.`};
  return {pluginState:String(record.state??'installed'),available:true};
}
function emit(value:Record<string,unknown>,maxBytes:number):void {
  const bytes=Buffer.from(JSON.stringify(value)+'\n');
  if(bytes.length>maxBytes)throw new PalaceCliError('BUDGET_TOO_SMALL',`${bytes.length} > ${maxBytes}`);
  process.stdout.write(bytes);
}
function unavailableBase(code:string,message:string,base:Record<string,unknown>,details:CliDetails):Record<string,unknown>{
  return {...base,ok:false,code,error:message,...details};
}
function schema(db:DatabaseSync,details:CliDetails):void {
  try{
    const hasMeta=Boolean((db.prepare("select 1 as present from sqlite_master where type='table' and name='meta'").get() as {present?:number}|undefined)?.present);
    if(!hasMeta)throw new PalaceCliError('PALACE_SCHEMA_UNSUPPORTED','Palace schema metadata is missing',details);
    const value=String((db.prepare('select value from meta where key=?').get('schema') as {value?:string}|undefined)?.value??'');
    if(value!=='1')throw new PalaceCliError('PALACE_SCHEMA_UNSUPPORTED','Unsupported Palace schema: '+(value||'missing'),details);
  }catch(error){if(error instanceof PalaceCliError)throw error;throw new PalaceCliError('PALACE_SCHEMA_UNSUPPORTED','Palace schema cannot be read',details);}
}
async function main():Promise<void> {
  const {command,positional,opts}=parse(process.argv.slice(2));
  if(opts.help){
    process.stdout.write('palace [--data-root DIR|--runtime-dir DIR] status|search QUERY|read ID|skills|skill status|install|repair|remove|adopt [--project-id ID] [--limit N] [--max-bytes N] [--include-candidates] [--full]\n');
    return;
  }
  if(!['status','search','read','skills','skill'].includes(command))throw new PalaceCliError('UNKNOWN_COMMAND','Unknown Palace command');
  if(opts['data-root']!==undefined&&opts['runtime-dir']!==undefined)throw new PalaceCliError('CONFLICTING_OPTIONS','Use either --data-root or --runtime-dir, not both');
  if(['status','skills'].includes(command)&&positional.length)throw new PalaceCliError('UNEXPECTED_ARGUMENT','This command accepts no positional arguments');
  if(command==='read'&&positional.length!==1)throw new PalaceCliError('CARD_ID_REQUIRED','Exactly one card id is required');
  if(command==='search'&&!positional.join(' ').trim())throw new PalaceCliError('QUERY_REQUIRED','A query is required');
  if(command==='skill'&&positional.length>1)throw new PalaceCliError('UNEXPECTED_ARGUMENT','Use one Skill lifecycle action');
  const skillAction=command==='skill'?(positional[0]??'status'):null;
  if((opts['break-lock']||opts['abandon-pending'])&&skillAction!=='repair')throw new PalaceCliError('UNKNOWN_OPTION','Recovery options are only valid for skill repair');
  if(opts.all&&skillAction!=='remove')throw new PalaceCliError('UNKNOWN_OPTION','--all is only valid for skill remove');
  if(opts.target!==undefined&&skillAction!=='adopt')throw new PalaceCliError('UNKNOWN_OPTION','--target is only valid for skill adopt');
  if(opts['forget-owner']!==undefined&&skillAction!=='repair')throw new PalaceCliError('UNKNOWN_OPTION','--forget-owner is only valid for skill repair');
  const maxBytes=boundedInt(opts['max-bytes']===undefined?undefined:Number(opts['max-bytes']),16384,512,65536);
  if(command==='skill'){
    const action=positional[0]??'status';let result:Record<string,unknown>;
    if(action==='status'||action==='doctor')result=await palaceSkillStatus({packageRoot});
    else if(action==='install')result=await reconcilePalaceSkill({packageRoot,force:true,install:true,repair:true});
    else if(action==='repair')result=await reconcilePalaceSkill({packageRoot,force:true,repair:true,breakLock:opts['break-lock']===true,abandonPending:opts['abandon-pending']===true,forgetOwner:typeof opts['forget-owner']==='string'?opts['forget-owner']:undefined});
    else if(action==='prune')result=await reconcilePalaceSkill({packageRoot,force:true,prune:true});
    else if(action==='remove')result=await reconcilePalaceSkill({packageRoot,force:true,removeCurrentOwner:true,removeAll:opts.all===true});
    else if(action==='adopt'){
      if(opts.target!=='agents'&&opts.target!=='pi')throw new PalaceCliError('SKILL_TARGET_REQUIRED','Explicitly select --target agents or --target pi. The prior target is retained, not deleted.');
      result=await reconcilePalaceSkill({packageRoot,force:true,install:true,repair:true,adoptTarget:opts.target});
    }else throw new PalaceCliError('SKILL_ACTION_UNSUPPORTED','Use palace skill status, install, repair, remove or adopt');
    emit(result,maxBytes);if(result.ok===false)process.exitCode=1;return;
  }
  const skill=await palaceSkillProbe({packageRoot});
  const skillState=skill.state;
  const selectedRuntime=path.resolve(String(opts['runtime-dir']??process.env.MCP_DEVICE_CONFIG_DIR??path.join(os.homedir(),'.mcp-device','server')));
  const explicitDataRoot=opts['data-root']!==undefined||(opts['runtime-dir']===undefined&&process.env.PALACE_DATA_ROOT!==undefined);
  const dataRoot=path.resolve(String(explicitDataRoot?(opts['data-root']??process.env.PALACE_DATA_ROOT):path.join(selectedRuntime,'plugins','palace','data')));
  const inferred=explicitDataRoot?managedRuntime(dataRoot):null,runtime=inferred??selectedRuntime;
  const archive=explicitDataRoot&&inferred===null,runtimeBinding=archive?'unmanaged-archive':inferred?'managed-path':'explicit-runtime';
  const filename=path.join(dataRoot,'palace.sqlite'),pkg=packageInfo();
  const active=archive?null:runtimeIsActive(runtime),life=lifecycle(runtime,archive,pkg);
  const writePath=archive||active!==false?null:`mcp-device knowledge --runtime-dir "${runtime}"`;
  const mutationPolicy=archive?'runtime-binding-unknown':active===true?'stopped-runtime-only':active===null?'owner-state-unknown':'exclusive-lease-required';
  const base={plugin:'palace',mode:'read-only',runtimeDir:archive?null:runtime,runtimeBinding,dataRoot,pluginState:life.pluginState,runtimeActive:active,writePath,mutationPolicy,skillState,skill,...pkg};
  const details={runtimeDir:archive?null:runtime,dataRoot,pluginState:life.pluginState,runtimeActive:active,remediation:life.remediation};
  if(!life.available){
    const failure=unavailableBase(life.code??'PALACE_UNAVAILABLE','Palace is not callable for this runtime.',base,details);
    if(command==='status'){emit(failure,maxBytes);process.exitCode=1;return;}
    throw new PalaceCliError(life.code??'PALACE_UNAVAILABLE','Palace is not callable for this runtime.',details);
  }
  if(!existsSync(filename)){
    const failure=unavailableBase('PALACE_NOT_INSTALLED','Palace database is not installed.',base,{...details,remediation:life.remediation??`Start or install Palace for runtime ${runtime}.`});
    if(command==='status'){emit(failure,maxBytes);process.exitCode=1;return;}
    throw new PalaceCliError('PALACE_NOT_INSTALLED','Palace database is not installed',{...details,remediation:String(failure.remediation)});
  }
  const db=new DatabaseSync(filename,{readOnly:true});
  try {
    // Establish one WAL snapshot for schema, counts and selected rows in this invocation.
    db.exec('BEGIN');
    schema(db,details);
    const limit=boundedInt(opts.limit===undefined?undefined:Number(opts.limit),10,1,50);
    const projectId=opts['project-id']===undefined?undefined:checkedString(opts['project-id'],512);
    const includeInactive=opts['include-inactive']===true;
    const includeCandidates=opts['include-candidates']===true;
    const full=opts.full===true;
    let response:Record<string,unknown>;
    if(command==='status'){
      const counts=(db.prepare('select kind,status,count(*) as count from cards group by kind,status').all() as Record<string,unknown>[]);
      const totals=(db.prepare('select (select count(*) from cards) cards,(select count(*) from evidence) evidence,(select count(*) from versions) versions').get() as Record<string,unknown>);
      response={...base,ok:true,totals,counts};
    }else if(command==='search'){
      const query=checkedString(positional.join(' '));if(!query.trim())throw new PalaceCliError('QUERY_REQUIRED','A search query is required',details);
      const selected=searchPalaceCards(db,'',{query,projectId,includeInactive,limit:0,resultLimit:limit});
      const result=selected.items.map(x=>full?x:preview(x));
      response={...base,ok:true,query,items:result,returned:result.length,candidates:selected.candidates,note:'Snapshot results; evidence bytes not reverified by read-only CLI'};
    }else if(command==='skills'){
      const rows=queryPalaceCards(db,'',{projectId,includeInactive:includeCandidates||includeInactive,kind:'procedure',status:includeCandidates?undefined:'validated',limit:limit+1});
      const result=rows.slice(0,limit).map(x=>full?x:preview(x));
      const activationPolicy=String((db.prepare('select value from meta where key=?').get('activationPolicy') as {value?:string}|undefined)?.value??'candidate_only');
      response={...base,ok:true,items:result,returned:result.length,candidates:rows.length,activationPolicy,note:'Validated procedures only unless --include-candidates is explicit'};
    }else if(command==='read'){
      const id=checkedString(positional[0],512);if(!id||positional.length!==1)throw new PalaceCliError('CARD_ID_REQUIRED','Exactly one card id is required',details);
      const row=db.prepare('select json,kind from cards where id=?').get(id) as {json:string;kind:string}|undefined;
      if(!row)throw new PalaceCliError('KNOWLEDGE_NOT_FOUND','Knowledge card was not found',details);
      const card=JSON.parse(row.json) as KnowledgeCard;
      if(card.scope.visibility==='project'&&(!projectId||projectId!==card.scope.projectId))throw new PalaceCliError('SCOPE_REQUIRED','Matching --project-id is required',details);
      if(!includeInactive&&['stale','superseded','withdrawn'].includes(card.status))throw new PalaceCliError('KNOWLEDGE_NOT_CURRENT','Knowledge card is inactive',details);
      response={...base,ok:true,kind:row.kind,card,evidenceVerifiedNow:false,note:'Source integrity must be verified separately before treating this card as an established fact.'};
    }else throw new PalaceCliError('UNKNOWN_COMMAND','Unknown Palace command: '+command,details);
    emit(response,maxBytes);
  } finally {db.close();}
}
try{await main();}catch(error){
  const failure=error as Error&{code?:string;details?:CliDetails};
  const code=failure.code??(failure instanceof PalaceCliError?failure.code:String(failure.message??failure).split(':')[0]);
  process.stderr.write(JSON.stringify({ok:false,code,error:String(failure.message??failure),...(failure instanceof PalaceCliError?failure.details:{})})+'\n');
  process.exitCode=1;
}
