import { mkdirSync, readFileSync, existsSync, writeFileSync, renameSync } from 'node:fs';
import { basename, join, resolve, relative, sep, isAbsolute } from 'node:path';
import { LogPalace } from '../../log-palace/store.js';
import type { KnowledgeCard, KnowledgeEvidence, KnowledgePreview, PalaceOptions, PalaceProvider, InvokeContext, KnowledgeScope } from './types.js';
import { PalaceStore, type EvidenceLocation } from './store.js';
import { SourceCatalog, extractDocument, extractObservation, gitSnapshot, readDocument } from './sources.js';
import { abort, assertLive, boundedInt, canonical, cardHash, clip, hash, object, preview, scopeOf, text } from './util.js';
export type * from './types.js';
export { cardHash } from './util.js';

export const PALACE_CAPABILITIES = ['knowledge.search','knowledge.list','knowledge.read','knowledge.stat','knowledge.import',
  'knowledge.ingest','knowledge.correct','knowledge.learn','knowledge.skills'] as const;
type Input = Record<string,unknown>;
type SourceObservation = Record<string,unknown>;

class Provider implements PalaceProvider {
  private store?:PalaceStore;
  private logs?:LogPalace;
  private timer?:ReturnType<typeof setInterval>;
  private tail:Promise<unknown>=Promise.resolve();
  private catalog:SourceCatalog;
  private stopping=false;
  private readonly indexBudget:Input;
  private readonly indexInterval:number;
  constructor(private readonly options:PalaceOptions) {
    if(!options.deviceId||options.deviceId.length>256)throw new Error('DEVICE_ID_REQUIRED');
    this.catalog=new SourceCatalog(options);
    this.indexBudget=object(options.indexBudget??{});this.indexInterval=boundedInt(this.indexBudget.intervalMs,30000,1000,3600000);
  }
  async start():Promise<void> {
    if(this.store)return;
    this.stopping=false;mkdirSync(this.options.dataRoot,{recursive:true});
    this.store=new PalaceStore(this.options.dataRoot,this.options.deviceId);
    this.store.setMeta('activationPolicy',this.options.validationRunner?'configured':'candidate_only');
    try {this.logs=new LogPalace(join(this.options.dataRoot,'source-index'));}
    catch(error){this.store.close();this.store=undefined;throw error;}
    for(const path of this.catalog.logRoots()) {
      if(/\.(md|markdown)$/i.test(path))continue;
      try {this.logs.add(path);}catch(error){this.store.setMeta('lastDiscoveryError',String(error).slice(0,512));}
    }
    if(this.options.autoIndex!==false) {
      try {await this.invoke('knowledge.ingest',this.indexBudget);}catch(error){this.store.setMeta('lastIndexError',String(error).slice(0,512));}
      this.timer=setInterval(()=>{void this.invoke('knowledge.ingest',this.indexBudget).catch(error=>{
        if(this.store)this.store.setMeta('lastIndexError',String(error).slice(0,512));
      });},this.indexInterval);
      this.timer.unref();
    }
  }
  async stop():Promise<void> {
    this.stopping=true;if(this.timer)clearInterval(this.timer);this.timer=undefined;
    await this.tail.catch(()=>{});
    this.logs?.close();this.logs=undefined;this.store?.close();this.store=undefined;
  }
  invoke(capability:string,input:unknown,context:InvokeContext={}):Promise<unknown> {
    if(this.stopping||!this.store||!this.logs)return Promise.reject(new Error('PLUGIN_NOT_STARTED'));
    const args=object(input);
    if(Buffer.byteLength(JSON.stringify(args),'utf8')>256*1024)return Promise.reject(new Error('INPUT_TOO_LARGE'));
    const job=this.tail.then(async()=>{abort(context.signal);return this.dispatch(capability,args,context);});
    this.tail=job.catch(()=>{});return job;
  }
  private get db():PalaceStore {if(!this.store)throw new Error('PLUGIN_NOT_STARTED');return this.store;}
  private get p1():LogPalace {if(!this.logs)throw new Error('PLUGIN_NOT_STARTED');return this.logs;}
  private id(input:Input):string {
    const ref=text(input.ref??input.id,512);if(!ref)throw new Error('KNOWLEDGE_REF_REQUIRED');return ref;
  }
  private card(id:string):KnowledgeCard {const card=this.db.get(id);if(!card)throw new Error('KNOWLEDGE_NOT_FOUND');return card;}
  private newCard(id:string,summary:string,body:string,status:KnowledgeCard['status'],scope:KnowledgeScope,
    evidence:KnowledgeEvidence[],derivedFrom:string[]=[]):KnowledgeCard {
    const now=Date.now(),card:KnowledgeCard={id,version:1,contentHash:'',summary:clip(summary,256),body:clip(body,16384),status,
      originDeviceId:this.options.deviceId,servingDeviceId:this.options.deviceId,scope,evidence,
      derivedFrom:[...new Set(derivedFrom)],provenance:[this.options.deviceId],updatedAt:now,lastVerifiedAt:now};
    card.contentHash=cardHash(card);return card;
  }
  private managedEvidence(value:unknown,kind:KnowledgeEvidence['kind']):KnowledgeEvidence {
    const bytes=Buffer.from(canonical(value)+'\n'),recordHash=hash(bytes);
    if(bytes.length>128*1024)throw new Error('EVIDENCE_TOO_LARGE');
    const relativePath=join('evidence',recordHash+'.json'),path=join(this.options.dataRoot,relativePath);
    mkdirSync(join(this.options.dataRoot,'evidence'),{recursive:true});
    if(!existsSync(path)){const tmp=path+'.tmp';writeFileSync(tmp,bytes);renameSync(tmp,path);}
    const evidence:KnowledgeEvidence={id:'ev:'+hash(this.options.deviceId+':managed:'+recordHash),
      originDeviceId:this.options.deviceId,sourceId:'managed:'+recordHash,sourceRef:'managed:'+recordHash,recordHash,
      kind,sourceAvailable:true,observedAt:Date.now()};
    this.db.saveEvidence({evidence,type:'managed',path:relativePath,bytes:bytes.length});return evidence;
  }
  private evidenceRead(location:EvidenceLocation,maxBytes=4096):Record<string,unknown> {
    if(location.type==='remote')return {id:location.evidence.id,available:false,reason:'REMOTE_EVIDENCE_REFERENCE',
      originDeviceId:location.evidence.originDeviceId,sourceRef:location.evidence.sourceRef};
    if(location.type==='p1'){
      const read=this.p1.read(location.sourceRef??location.evidence.sourceRef,Math.min(65536,Math.max(128,maxBytes)));
      return {id:location.evidence.id,available:true,verifiedBytes:true,recordHash:location.evidence.recordHash,...read};
    }
    const path=location.type==='managed'?resolve(this.options.dataRoot,location.path??''):location.path??'';
    if(location.type==='managed'){
      const rel=relative(resolve(this.options.dataRoot),path);
      if(rel.startsWith('..'+sep)||rel==='..')throw new Error('EVIDENCE_PATH_INVALID');
    }
    const bytes=readFileSync(path);if(bytes.length>256*1024)throw new Error('EVIDENCE_TOO_LARGE');
    if(hash(bytes)!==location.evidence.recordHash)throw new Error('SOURCE_CHANGED');
    const decoded=new TextDecoder('utf-8',{fatal:true}).decode(bytes);
    return {id:location.evidence.id,available:true,verifiedBytes:true,recordHash:location.evidence.recordHash,
      text:clip(decoded,maxBytes),truncated:bytes.length>maxBytes};
  }
  private refresh(card:KnowledgeCard):KnowledgeCard {
    for(const evidence of card.evidence){
      const location=this.db.evidence(evidence.id);if(!location||location.type==='remote')continue;
      try {this.evidenceRead(location,128);}
      catch {this.db.markUnavailable(evidence.id);}
    }
    return this.card(card.id);
  }
  private checkScope(card:KnowledgeCard,input:Input):void {
    const projectId=text(input.projectId,512);
    if(projectId && card.scope.visibility==='project' && card.scope.projectId!==projectId)throw new Error('KNOWLEDGE_SCOPE_MISMATCH');
  }
  private async dispatch(capability:string,input:Input,context:InvokeContext):Promise<unknown> {
    switch(capability){
      case 'knowledge.search':return this.search(input,false);
      case 'knowledge.list':return this.search(input,true);
      case 'knowledge.stat':{let card=this.card(this.id(input));this.checkScope(card,input);card=this.refresh(card);return this.db.stat(card.id);}
      case 'knowledge.read':return this.read(input);
      case 'knowledge.import':return this.import(input);
      case 'knowledge.ingest':return this.ingest(input,context.signal);
      case 'knowledge.correct':return this.correct(input);
      case 'knowledge.learn':return this.learn(input,context.signal);
      case 'knowledge.skills':return this.skills(input,context.signal);
      default:throw new Error('CAPABILITY_UNAVAILABLE');
    }
  }
  private search(input:Input,list:boolean):unknown {
    const query=list?'':text(input.query,2048);
    if(!list&&!query.trim())throw new Error('QUERY_REQUIRED');
    const limit=boundedInt(input.limit,8,1,50),maxBytes=boundedInt(input.maxBytes,8192,512,65536);
    const common={query,projectId:text(input.projectId,512)||undefined,
      includeInactive:input.includeInactive===true,kind:text(input.kind,64)||undefined,status:text(input.status,64)||undefined,
      cursor:list?text(input.cursor,512)||undefined:undefined};
    const rows=list?this.db.query({...common,limit:limit+1}):this.db.search({...common,limit:0,query,resultLimit:limit,lookahead:true}).items;
    const items:KnowledgePreview[]=[];
    let truncated=rows.length>limit;
    for(const card of rows.slice(0,limit)){
      const item=preview(card),proposal={items:[...items,item],total:rows.length,truncated,nextCursor:list?card.id:null};
      if(Buffer.byteLength(JSON.stringify(proposal),'utf8')>maxBytes){truncated=true;break;}
      items.push(item);
    }
    const response={items,total:rows.length,truncated,nextCursor:list&&truncated&&items.length?items[items.length-1].id:null};
    if(Buffer.byteLength(JSON.stringify(response),'utf8')>maxBytes)throw new Error('BUDGET_TOO_SMALL');
    return response;
  }
  private read(input:Input):unknown {
    const maxBytes=boundedInt(input.maxBytes,65536,1024,256*1024);
    let card=this.card(this.id(input));this.checkScope(card,input);card=this.refresh(card);
    const response:{card:KnowledgeCard;evidence?:Record<string,unknown>[]}={card};
    if(Buffer.byteLength(JSON.stringify(response),'utf8')>maxBytes)throw new Error('BUDGET_TOO_SMALL');
    if(input.evidence===true){
      response.evidence=[];
      for(const evidence of card.evidence.slice(0,32)){
        const location=this.db.evidence(evidence.id);
        let item:Record<string,unknown>;
        try {item=location?this.evidenceRead(location,Math.min(4096,maxBytes)): {id:evidence.id,available:false,reason:'REMOTE_EVIDENCE_REFERENCE'};}
        catch(error){item={id:evidence.id,available:false,reason:String(error).slice(0,160)};}
        const candidate={...response,evidence:[...response.evidence,item]};
        if(Buffer.byteLength(JSON.stringify(candidate),'utf8')>maxBytes)break;
        response.evidence.push(item);
      }
    }
    return response;
  }
  private import(input:Input):unknown {
    const raw=object(input.card);
    if(Buffer.byteLength(JSON.stringify(raw),'utf8')>65536)throw new Error('CARD_TOO_LARGE');
    const id=text(raw.id,256),origin=text(raw.originDeviceId,256);
    if(!id||!origin||!Number.isSafeInteger(raw.version)||Number(raw.version)<1)throw new Error('INVALID_CARD');
    const evidence=raw.evidence;
    if(!Array.isArray(evidence)||!evidence.length||evidence.length>32)throw new Error('INVALID_EVIDENCE');
    const ids=new Set<string>();
    for(const value of evidence){
      const e=object(value);
      if(!text(e.id,256)||!text(e.originDeviceId,256)||!text(e.sourceId,512)||!text(e.sourceRef,512)||
        !/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/.test(text(e.recordHash,64))||!['source_trace','tool_receipt','postcondition'].includes(String(e.kind)))
        throw new Error('INVALID_EVIDENCE');
      if(typeof e.sourceAvailable!=='boolean'||!Number.isFinite(e.observedAt))throw new Error('INVALID_EVIDENCE');
      if(ids.has(String(e.id)))throw new Error('DUPLICATE_EVIDENCE');ids.add(String(e.id));
    }
    if(!Array.isArray(raw.derivedFrom)||raw.derivedFrom.length>32||raw.derivedFrom.some(x=>typeof x!=='string'||x.length>256))
      throw new Error('INVALID_DERIVATION');
    if(!Array.isArray(raw.provenance)||raw.provenance.length>64||raw.provenance.some(x=>typeof x!=='string'||x.length>256))
      throw new Error('INVALID_PROVENANCE');
    if(!['hypothesis','observed','validated','stale','superseded','withdrawn'].includes(String(raw.status)))throw new Error('INVALID_STATUS');
    if(!Number.isFinite(raw.updatedAt)||Number(raw.updatedAt)<0||
      !(raw.lastVerifiedAt===null||Number.isFinite(raw.lastVerifiedAt)))throw new Error('INVALID_CARD_TIME');
    const card={...raw,id,originDeviceId:origin,summary:text(raw.summary,256),body:text(raw.body,32768),
      scope:scopeOf(raw.scope),servingDeviceId:this.options.deviceId} as unknown as KnowledgeCard;
    if(!card.summary||cardHash(card)!==card.contentHash)throw new Error('CARD_HASH_MISMATCH');
    if(card.scope.visibility==='local'&&origin!==this.options.deviceId)throw new Error('LOCAL_ONLY_KNOWLEDGE');
    const existing=this.db.get(id);
    if(origin===this.options.deviceId&&(!existing||card.version>existing.version))throw new Error('ORIGIN_WRITE_FORBIDDEN');
    return this.db.importCard(card);
  }
  private async ingest(input:Input,signal?:AbortSignal):Promise<unknown> {
    const maxRecords=boundedInt(input.maxRecords,64,1,256),maxBytes=boundedInt(input.maxBytes,2*1024*1024,1024,16*1024*1024);
    const maxFiles=boundedInt(input.maxFiles,32,1,128);abort(signal);
    for(const path of this.catalog.logRoots())if(!/\.(md|markdown)$/i.test(path))try{this.p1.add(path);}catch{}
    const index=this.p1.index({maxFiles,maxBytes});
    let remaining=maxBytes,created=0,skipped=0;
    const page=this.p1.observations({cursor:this.db.getMeta('observationCursor')||undefined,limit:maxRecords,maxBytes:1024*1024});
    for(const row of page.items as SourceObservation[]){
      abort(signal);
      const ref=String(row.ref),recordBytes=Number(row.bytes),sourcePath=String(row.source_path);
      const evidenceId='ev:'+hash(this.options.deviceId+':p1:'+ref+':'+String(row.hash));
      if(!row.source_available){this.db.markUnavailable(evidenceId);skipped++;continue;}
      if(recordBytes>remaining){skipped++;continue;}
      remaining-=recordBytes;
      const id='k:'+hash(this.options.deviceId+':observation:'+evidenceId);
      const existing=this.db.get(id);if(existing&&existing.status!=='stale'){skipped++;continue;}
      try{
        const read=this.p1.read(ref,8192);
        const extracted=extractObservation(read.text,String(row.kind),String(row.tool??''));
        if(!extracted){skipped++;continue;}
        const evidence:KnowledgeEvidence={id:evidenceId,originDeviceId:this.options.deviceId,sourceId:'source:'+hash(sourcePath),
          sourceRef:ref,recordHash:String(row.hash),kind:'source_trace',sourceAvailable:true,observedAt:Date.now()};
        const scope=this.catalog.scope(typeof row.file_path==='string'&&isAbsolute(row.file_path)?row.file_path:sourcePath);
        const normalized=(body:string)=>body.normalize('NFKC').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim().toLowerCase();
        const similarityKey=hash(canonical({scope,status:extracted.status,body:normalized(extracted.body)}));
        const similar=this.db.similar(similarityKey);
        if(similar&&(similar.status==='withdrawn'||similar.status==='superseded'||normalized(similar.body)!==normalized(extracted.body))){skipped++;continue;}
        if(similar&&similar.evidence.some(e=>e.id===evidence.id&&e.sourceAvailable)&&similar.status!=='stale'){skipped++;continue;}
        if(similar&&similar.evidence.length>=32){skipped++;continue;}
        const saved=this.db.atomic(()=>{
          this.db.saveEvidence({evidence,type:'p1',sourceRef:ref,bytes:recordBytes});
          const base=similar??existing;
          const allEvidence=[...(base?.evidence??[]).filter(e=>e.sourceAvailable&&e.id!==evidence.id),evidence];
          const fresh=this.newCard(base?.id??id,extracted.summary,extracted.body,extracted.status,scope,allEvidence);
          const card=base?this.db.nextVersion(base,{status:fresh.status,evidence:allEvidence,lastVerifiedAt:Date.now()}):fresh;
          this.db.put(card);this.db.rememberSimilarity(similarityKey,card.id);return card;
        });created++;
        if(saved.evidence.length>=2){
          const fix=saved.body.match(/(?:fix|solution|resolved by)\s*:\s*([^\n]{8,1024})/i)?.[1];
          if(fix)await this.learn({key:'automatic:'+saved.id,summary:clip('Procedure candidate: '+fix,256),steps:[fix],evidenceRefs:[saved.id],autoActivate:false},signal);
        }
      }catch{skipped++;}
    }
    this.db.setMeta('observationCursor',page.nextCursor??'');
    for(const source of this.catalog.documents(Math.min(32,maxFiles))){
      abort(signal);
      try{
        const data=readDocument(source.path);if(data.bytes.length>remaining)continue;remaining-=data.bytes.length;
        const old=this.db.document(source.path);if(old?.hash===data.hash)continue;
        const extracted=extractDocument(data.text),id=old?.cardId??'k:'+hash(this.options.deviceId+':document:'+source.path);
        const evidence:KnowledgeEvidence={id:'ev:'+hash(this.options.deviceId+':file:'+source.path+':'+data.hash),
          originDeviceId:this.options.deviceId,sourceId:'source:'+hash(source.path),sourceRef:'file:'+hash(source.path)+':'+data.hash,
          recordHash:data.hash,kind:'source_trace',sourceAvailable:true,observedAt:Date.now()};
        this.db.atomic(()=>{
          this.db.saveEvidence({evidence,type:'file',path:source.path,bytes:data.bytes.length});
          const current=this.db.get(id),fresh=this.newCard(id,extracted.summary,extracted.body,'observed',source.scope,[evidence]);
          const card=current?this.db.nextVersion(current,{summary:fresh.summary,body:fresh.body,evidence:[evidence],status:'observed',lastVerifiedAt:Date.now()}):fresh;
          this.db.put(card,source.kind);
          if(current)this.db.invalidateDependents(id);
          this.db.setDocument(source.path,data.hash,id);
        });created++;
      }catch{skipped++;}
    }
    for(const root of this.catalog.projects.slice(0,4)){
      abort(signal);const snapshot=await gitSnapshot(root);if(!snapshot||Buffer.byteLength(snapshot)>remaining)continue;
      remaining-=Buffer.byteLength(snapshot);
      const key='git:'+hash(root),revision=hash(snapshot);
      if(this.db.getMeta(key)===revision)continue;
      const evidence=this.managedEvidence({type:'git-history',project:root,output:snapshot},'source_trace');
      const id='k:'+hash(this.options.deviceId+':'+key),old=this.db.get(id);
      const fresh=this.newCard(id,'Recent Git history: '+basename(root),snapshot,'observed',this.catalog.scope(root),[evidence]);
      this.db.atomic(()=>{
        this.db.put(old?this.db.nextVersion(old,{summary:fresh.summary,body:fresh.body,evidence:[evidence],status:'observed'}):fresh,'project');
        if(old)this.db.invalidateDependents(id);
        this.db.setMeta(key,revision);
      });created++;
    }
    const verify=this.db.locations(this.db.getMeta('verificationCursor'),Math.min(64,maxRecords));
    let invalidated=0;
    for(const location of verify.items){
      abort(signal);if(location.type==='remote'||!location.evidence.sourceAvailable)continue;
      try {this.evidenceRead(location,128);}catch{invalidated+=this.db.markUnavailable(location.evidence.id).length;}
    }
    this.db.setMeta('verificationCursor',verify.cursor??'');
    return {indexed:index,created,skipped,invalidated,cards:this.db.count(),cursor:page.nextCursor,complete:page.complete,
      projects:this.catalog.projects.map(path=>({path,scope:this.catalog.scope(path)})),modelCalls:0};
  }
  private correct(input:Input):unknown {
    const old=this.card(this.id(input));
    if(old.originDeviceId!==this.options.deviceId)throw new Error('ORIGIN_CORRECTION_REQUIRED');
    const body=text(input.body??input.correctedText,16384),summary=text(input.summary,256)||clip(body,256),reason=text(input.reason,1024);
    if(!body.trim()||!reason.trim())throw new Error('CORRECTION_AND_REASON_REQUIRED');
    const evidence=this.managedEvidence({type:'correction',ref:old.id,version:old.version,reason,body,at:Date.now()},'source_trace');
    return this.db.atomic(()=>{
      const card=this.db.nextVersion(old,{summary,body,status:'hypothesis',evidence:[evidence],lastVerifiedAt:Date.now()});
      this.db.put(card,this.db.kind(old.id));
      const invalidated=this.db.invalidateDependents(old.id);
      return {card,invalidated};
    });
  }
  private parents(input:Input):{cards:KnowledgeCard[];evidence:KnowledgeEvidence[]} {
    const refs=input.evidenceRefs??input.evidence;
    if(!Array.isArray(refs)||!refs.length||refs.length>32||refs.some(x=>typeof x!=='string'))throw new Error('EVIDENCE_REQUIRED');
    const cards=new Map<string,KnowledgeCard>(),evidence=new Map<string,KnowledgeEvidence>();
    for(const ref of refs as string[]){
      let card=this.db.get(ref);
      if(card){card=this.refresh(card);assertLive(card);cards.set(card.id,card);for(const e of card.evidence)evidence.set(e.id,e);continue;}
      const loc=this.db.evidence(ref);if(!loc)throw new Error('EVIDENCE_NOT_FOUND');
      if(!loc.evidence.sourceAvailable)throw new Error('EVIDENCE_NOT_CURRENT');
      this.evidenceRead(loc,128);
      for(const cardId of this.db.evidenceCards(ref)){
        const parent=this.refresh(this.card(cardId));assertLive(parent);cards.set(cardId,parent);
      }
      evidence.set(ref,loc.evidence);
    }
    if(!cards.size||evidence.size>32)throw new Error('EVIDENCE_REQUIRED');
    return {cards:[...cards.values()],evidence:[...evidence.values()]};
  }
  private async activate(card:KnowledgeCard,signal?:AbortSignal):Promise<{card:KnowledgeCard;activated:boolean;reason:string}> {
    if(!this.options.validationRunner)return {card,activated:false,reason:'VALIDATION_POLICY_NOT_CONFIGURED'};
    abort(signal);
    const receipt=await this.options.validationRunner(JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(card)) as KnowledgeCard,signal);
    abort(signal);
    if(!receipt||!text(receipt.executionId,256)||!text(receipt.validatorVersion,256)||!Number.isSafeInteger(receipt.exitCode)||
      typeof receipt.postconditionPassed!=='boolean')throw new Error('INVALID_VALIDATOR_RECEIPT');
    const evidence=this.managedEvidence({type:'validation',cardId:card.id,revision:card.contentHash,...receipt,at:Date.now()},'postcondition');
    if(receipt.exitCode!==0||!receipt.postconditionPassed)return {card,activated:false,reason:'VALIDATION_FAILED'};
    for(const parent of card.derivedFrom)assertLive(this.refresh(this.card(parent)));
    const next=this.db.nextVersion(card,{status:'validated',evidence:[...card.evidence,evidence],lastVerifiedAt:Date.now()});
    if(next.evidence.length>32)throw new Error('EVIDENCE_BUDGET_EXCEEDED');
    this.db.atomic(()=>this.db.put(next,'procedure'));
    return {card:next,activated:true,reason:'EXECUTED_VALIDATION_PASSED'};
  }
  private async learn(input:Input,signal?:AbortSignal):Promise<unknown> {
    const parents=this.parents(input),scope=input.scope===undefined?parents.cards[0].scope:scopeOf(input.scope);
    for(const parent of parents.cards)if(parent.scope.visibility==='project'&&
      (scope.visibility!=='project'||scope.projectId!==parent.scope.projectId))throw new Error('SCOPE_WIDENING_REQUIRES_SEPARATE_EVIDENCE');
    const steps=input.steps;
    if(!Array.isArray(steps)||!steps.length||steps.length>20||steps.some(x=>typeof x!=='string'||Buffer.byteLength(x)>1024))
      throw new Error('PROCEDURE_STEPS_REQUIRED');
    const summary=text(input.summary??input.title,256);if(!summary)throw new Error('SUMMARY_REQUIRED');
    const body=(steps as string[]).map((step,i)=>(i+1)+'. '+step).join('\n');
    const key=text(input.key,256)||hash(canonical({body,scope})),id='k:'+hash(this.options.deviceId+':procedure:'+key);
    const old=this.db.get(id);
    let card=this.newCard(id,summary,body,'hypothesis',scope,parents.evidence,parents.cards.map(c=>c.id));
    if(old) {
      const equivalent=old.body===body&&canonical(old.scope)===canonical(scope)&&
        canonical(old.evidence.filter(e=>e.kind!=='postcondition').map(e=>e.id).sort())===canonical(parents.evidence.map(e=>e.id).sort());
      if(equivalent&&old.status==='validated')return {card:old,activated:true,deduplicated:true,reason:'EXISTING_VALIDATION'};
      card=this.db.nextVersion(old,{summary,body,status:'hypothesis',scope,evidence:parents.evidence,derivedFrom:parents.cards.map(c=>c.id)});
    }
    this.db.atomic(()=>{this.db.put(card,'procedure');if(old)this.db.invalidateDependents(id);});
    if(input.autoActivate===false)return {card,activated:false,reason:'CANDIDATE_ONLY'};
    return this.activate(card,signal);
  }
  private async skills(input:Input,signal?:AbortSignal):Promise<unknown> {
    const action=text(input.action,64)||'list';
    if(action==='read'){
      const card=this.refresh(this.card(this.id(input)));this.checkScope(card,input);
      if(this.db.kind(card.id)!=='procedure')throw new Error('SKILL_NOT_FOUND');
      if(card.status!=='validated'&&input.includeCandidates!==true)throw new Error('SKILL_NOT_ACTIVE');
      const response={card,body:card.body,active:card.status==='validated'};
      if(Buffer.byteLength(JSON.stringify(response),'utf8')>boundedInt(input.maxBytes,65536,1024,65536))throw new Error('BUDGET_TOO_SMALL');
      return response;
    }
    if(action==='rollback'){
      const id=this.id(input),old=this.db.get(id,boundedInt(input.version,0,1,Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER));
      if(!old||this.db.kind(id)!=='procedure'||old.status!=='validated')throw new Error('VALIDATED_REVISION_REQUIRED');
      const currentEvidence=new Set<string>();
      for(const parent of old.derivedFrom){const current=this.refresh(this.card(parent));assertLive(current);for(const e of current.evidence)currentEvidence.add(e.id);}
      if(old.evidence.some(e=>e.kind!=='postcondition'&&!currentEvidence.has(e.id)))throw new Error('CORRECTED_EVIDENCE_CANNOT_ROLLBACK');
      if(!this.options.validationRunner)throw new Error('VALIDATION_POLICY_NOT_CONFIGURED');
      const current=this.card(id),candidate=this.db.nextVersion(current,{...old,status:'hypothesis',version:current.version+1,
        evidence:old.evidence.filter(e=>e.kind!=='postcondition')});
      this.db.atomic(()=>this.db.put(candidate,'procedure'));
      return this.activate(candidate,signal);
    }
    if(action!=='list')throw new Error('SKILL_ACTION_UNSUPPORTED');
    const maxBytes=boundedInt(input.maxBytes,8192,512,65536);
    const response={...(this.search({...input,kind:'procedure',status:input.includeCandidates===true?undefined:'validated',
      includeInactive:input.includeCandidates===true},true) as {items:KnowledgePreview[];truncated:boolean;nextCursor:string|null}),
      activationPolicy:this.options.validationRunner?'configured':'candidate_only'};
    while(Buffer.byteLength(JSON.stringify(response),'utf8')>maxBytes&&response.items.length){
      response.items.pop();response.truncated=true;response.nextCursor=response.items.length?response.items[response.items.length-1].id:null;
    }
    if(Buffer.byteLength(JSON.stringify(response),'utf8')>maxBytes)throw new Error('BUDGET_TOO_SMALL');
    return response;
  }
}
export function createPalaceProvider(options:PalaceOptions):PalaceProvider {return new Provider(options);}

import { DatabaseSync } from 'node:sqlite';
import { copyFileSync, existsSync, mkdirSync, mkdtempSync, rmSync, statSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
import { pathToFileURL } from 'node:url';
import type { KnowledgeCard, KnowledgeEvidence, KnowledgeStatus } from './types.js';
import { cardHash, canonical, preview } from './util.js';
import { queryTerms, rankCandidates } from './retrieval.js';

type Row = Record<string,unknown>;
export interface EvidenceLocation {
  evidence: KnowledgeEvidence;
  type: 'p1' | 'file' | 'managed' | 'remote';
  path?: string;
  sourceRef?: string;
  bytes?: number;
}
export interface PalaceQueryInput {
  query?:string;projectId?:string;includeInactive?:boolean;kind?:string;status?:string;
  limit:number;cursor?:string;visibility?:string;
}
export function queryPalaceCards(db:DatabaseSync,deviceId:string,input:PalaceQueryInput):KnowledgeCard[] {
  const tokens=queryTerms(input.query??'');
  if(tokens.length>24)throw new Error('QUERY_TOO_LARGE');
  const args:(string|number)[]=[];
  let sql='SELECT c.json FROM cards c ';
  if(tokens.length) {
    sql+='JOIN card_fts ON card_fts.id=c.id WHERE card_fts MATCH ?';
    args.push(tokens.map(x=>'"'+x.replace(/"/g,'""')+'"').join(' OR '));
  } else sql+='WHERE 1=1';
  if(!input.includeInactive)sql+=" AND c.status NOT IN ('stale','superseded','withdrawn')";
  if(input.projectId) {sql+=" AND (c.project_id=? OR c.visibility='account')";args.push(input.projectId);}
  else sql+=" AND c.visibility<>'project'";
  if(input.visibility) {sql+=' AND c.visibility=?';args.push(input.visibility);}
  if(input.kind){sql+=' AND c.kind=?';args.push(input.kind);}
  if(input.status){sql+=' AND c.status=?';args.push(input.status);}
  if(input.cursor){sql+=' AND c.id>?';args.push(input.cursor);}
  sql+=tokens.length?" ORDER BY CASE c.status WHEN 'validated' THEN 0 WHEN 'observed' THEN 1 ELSE 2 END, bm25(card_fts),c.id":" ORDER BY c.id";
  sql+=' LIMIT ?';args.push(input.limit);
  return (db.prepare(sql).all(...args) as Row[]).map(row=>{
    const card=JSON.parse(String(row.json)) as KnowledgeCard;if(deviceId)card.servingDeviceId=deviceId;return card;
  });
}
export function searchPalaceCards(db:DatabaseSync,deviceId:string,input:PalaceQueryInput&{query:string;resultLimit:number;lookahead?:boolean}):{items:KnowledgeCard[];candidates:number} {
  // Pagination lookahead must not change the candidate corpus or its scoring frequencies.
  const candidateLimit=Math.min(200,Math.max(40,input.resultLimit*8));
  const candidates=queryPalaceCards(db,deviceId,{...input,limit:candidateLimit});
  return {items:rankCandidates(candidates,input.query,input.resultLimit+(input.lookahead?1:0)),candidates:candidates.length};
}
export class PalaceStore {
  readonly db: DatabaseSync;
  constructor(readonly dataRoot: string,readonly deviceId: string) {
    mkdirSync(dataRoot,{recursive:true});
    const filename=join(dataRoot,'palace.sqlite');
    // Compatibility is established before a writer can run WAL recovery,
    // checkpoint, PRAGMA changes, or DDL. A clean database can be inspected
    // through immutable=1 without SQLite touching shared-memory bytes. A
    // non-empty WAL is exceptional; probe an external stable snapshot instead.
    if(existsSync(filename)&&statSync(filename).size>0){
      const check=(probe:DatabaseSync)=>{
        const objects=probe.prepare("SELECT name,type FROM sqlite_master WHERE name NOT LIKE 'sqlite_%' LIMIT 2").all() as Row[];
        const hasMeta=objects.some(row=>String(row.name)==='meta'&&String(row.type)==='table')
          ||Boolean((probe.prepare("SELECT 1 AS present FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' AND name='meta'").get() as Row|undefined)?.present);
        if(!hasMeta)throw new Error('PALACE_SCHEMA_UNSUPPORTED');
        const schema=String((probe.prepare('SELECT value FROM meta WHERE key=?').get('schema') as Row|undefined)?.value??'');
        if(schema!=='1')throw new Error('PALACE_SCHEMA_UNSUPPORTED');
      };
      const wal=filename+'-wal',shm=filename+'-shm',walBytes=existsSync(wal)?statSync(wal).size:0;
      if(walBytes===0){
        const uri=pathToFileURL(filename).href+'?immutable=1';
        const probe=new DatabaseSync(uri,{readOnly:true});try{check(probe);}finally{probe.close();}
      }else{
        const snapshot=mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(),'palace-schema-probe-')),copy=join(snapshot,'palace.sqlite');
        const files=[filename,wal,...(existsSync(shm)?[shm]:[])];
        const fingerprint=()=>files.map(file=>{const stat=statSync(file,{bigint:true});return [file,stat.size.toString(),stat.mtimeNs.toString()].join('|');}).join('\\n');
        const before=fingerprint();
        try{
          copyFileSync(filename,copy);copyFileSync(wal,copy+'-wal');if(existsSync(shm))copyFileSync(shm,copy+'-shm');
          if(fingerprint()!==before)throw new Error('PALACE_SCHEMA_BUSY');
          const probe=new DatabaseSync(copy,{readOnly:true});try{check(probe);}finally{probe.close();}
          if(fingerprint()!==before)throw new Error('PALACE_SCHEMA_BUSY');
        }finally{rmSync(snapshot,{recursive:true,force:true});}
      }
    }
    this.db=new DatabaseSync(filename);
    try {
      this.db.exec("PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL; PRAGMA synchronous=FULL; PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON; PRAGMA busy_timeout=3000");
      this.db.exec('BEGIN IMMEDIATE');
      try {
        this.db.exec(
          'CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS meta(key TEXT PRIMARY KEY,value TEXT NOT NULL);'+
          'CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS cards(id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,version INTEGER NOT NULL,status TEXT NOT NULL,kind TEXT NOT NULL,'+
          'project_id TEXT,visibility TEXT NOT NULL,summary TEXT NOT NULL,body TEXT NOT NULL,json TEXT NOT NULL);'+
          'CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS versions(id TEXT NOT NULL,version INTEGER NOT NULL,json TEXT NOT NULL,PRIMARY KEY(id,version));'+
          'CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS evidence(id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,json TEXT NOT NULL);'+
          'CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS card_evidence(card_id TEXT NOT NULL,evidence_id TEXT NOT NULL,PRIMARY KEY(card_id,evidence_id));'+
          'CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS evidence_cards ON card_evidence(evidence_id,card_id);'+
          'CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS derivations(parent TEXT NOT NULL,child TEXT NOT NULL,PRIMARY KEY(parent,child));'+
          'CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS documents(path TEXT PRIMARY KEY,hash TEXT NOT NULL,card_id TEXT NOT NULL);'+
          'CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS observation_keys(key TEXT PRIMARY KEY,card_id TEXT NOT NULL);'+
          'CREATE VIRTUAL TABLE IF NOT EXISTS card_fts USING fts5(id UNINDEXED,summary,body);'
        );
        this.setMeta('schema','1');
        this.db.exec('COMMIT');
      } catch(error) {this.db.exec('ROLLBACK');throw error;}
    } catch(error) {this.db.close();throw error;}
  }
  close(): void { this.db.exec('PRAGMA wal_checkpoint(TRUNCATE)');this.db.close(); }
  atomic<T>(fn:()=>T):T {
    this.db.exec('BEGIN IMMEDIATE');
    try {const value=fn();this.db.exec('COMMIT');return value;}
    catch(error){this.db.exec('ROLLBACK');throw error;}
  }
  getMeta(key:string):string {return String((this.db.prepare('SELECT value FROM meta WHERE key=?').get(key) as Row|undefined)?.value??'');}
  setMeta(key:string,value:string):void {this.db.prepare('INSERT INTO meta(key,value) VALUES(?,?) ON CONFLICT(key) DO UPDATE SET value=excluded.value').run(key,value);}
  get(id:string,version?:number):KnowledgeCard|undefined {
    const row=(version===undefined?this.db.prepare('SELECT json FROM cards WHERE id=?').get(id):
      this.db.prepare('SELECT json FROM versions WHERE id=? AND version=?').get(id,version)) as Row|undefined;
    if(!row)return undefined;
    const card=JSON.parse(String(row.json)) as KnowledgeCard;
    card.servingDeviceId=this.deviceId;
    return card;
  }
  kind(id:string):string {return String((this.db.prepare('SELECT kind FROM cards WHERE id=?').get(id) as Row|undefined)?.kind??'');}
  put(card:KnowledgeCard,kind='observation'):void {
    this.db.prepare('INSERT INTO versions(id,version,json) VALUES(?,?,?) ON CONFLICT(id,version) DO NOTHING')
      .run(card.id,card.version,JSON.stringify(card));
    this.db.prepare('INSERT INTO cards(id,version,status,kind,project_id,visibility,summary,body,json) VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?) '+
      'ON CONFLICT(id) DO UPDATE SET version=excluded.version,status=excluded.status,kind=excluded.kind,project_id=excluded.project_id,'+
      'visibility=excluded.visibility,summary=excluded.summary,body=excluded.body,json=excluded.json')
      .run(card.id,card.version,card.status,kind,card.scope.projectId??null,card.scope.visibility,card.summary,card.body,JSON.stringify(card));
    this.db.prepare('DELETE FROM card_fts WHERE id=?').run(card.id);
    this.db.prepare('INSERT INTO card_fts(id,summary,body) VALUES(?,?,?)').run(card.id,card.summary,card.body);
    this.db.prepare('DELETE FROM card_evidence WHERE card_id=?').run(card.id);
    for(const evidence of card.evidence)this.db.prepare('INSERT OR IGNORE INTO card_evidence(card_id,evidence_id) VALUES(?,?)').run(card.id,evidence.id);
    this.db.prepare('DELETE FROM derivations WHERE child=?').run(card.id);
    for(const parent of card.derivedFrom) {
      if(parent===card.id)throw new Error('DERIVATION_CYCLE');
      this.db.prepare('INSERT OR IGNORE INTO derivations(parent,child) VALUES(?,?)').run(parent,card.id);
    }
  }
  saveEvidence(location:EvidenceLocation):void {
    const old=this.evidence(location.evidence.id);
    if(old && old.evidence.recordHash!==location.evidence.recordHash)throw new Error('EVIDENCE_ID_CONFLICT');
    if(old && old.type!=='remote' && location.type==='remote')return;
    this.db.prepare('INSERT INTO evidence(id,json) VALUES(?,?) ON CONFLICT(id) DO UPDATE SET json=excluded.json')
      .run(location.evidence.id,JSON.stringify(location));
  }
  evidence(id:string):EvidenceLocation|undefined {
    const row=this.db.prepare('SELECT json FROM evidence WHERE id=?').get(id) as Row|undefined;
    return row?JSON.parse(String(row.json)) as EvidenceLocation:undefined;
  }
  evidenceCards(id:string):string[] {return (this.db.prepare('SELECT card_id FROM card_evidence WHERE evidence_id=?').all(id) as Row[]).map(x=>String(x.card_id));}
  locations(cursor='',limit=64):{items:EvidenceLocation[];cursor:string|null} {
    const rows=this.db.prepare('SELECT id,json FROM evidence WHERE id>? ORDER BY id LIMIT ?').all(cursor,limit+1) as Row[];
    return {items:rows.slice(0,limit).map(x=>JSON.parse(String(x.json))),cursor:rows.length>limit?String(rows[limit-1].id):null};
  }
  similar(key:string):KnowledgeCard|undefined {
    const row=this.db.prepare('SELECT card_id FROM observation_keys WHERE key=?').get(key) as Row|undefined;
    return row?this.get(String(row.card_id)):undefined;
  }
  rememberSimilarity(key:string,cardId:string):void {
    this.db.prepare('INSERT INTO observation_keys(key,card_id) VALUES(?,?) ON CONFLICT(key) DO UPDATE SET card_id=excluded.card_id').run(key,cardId);
  }
  document(path:string):{hash:string;cardId:string}|undefined {
    const row=this.db.prepare('SELECT hash,card_id FROM documents WHERE path=?').get(path) as Row|undefined;
    return row?{hash:String(row.hash),cardId:String(row.card_id)}:undefined;
  }
  setDocument(path:string,hash:string,cardId:string):void {
    this.db.prepare('INSERT INTO documents(path,hash,card_id) VALUES(?,?,?) ON CONFLICT(path) DO UPDATE SET hash=excluded.hash,card_id=excluded.card_id')
      .run(path,hash,cardId);
  }
  nextVersion(old:KnowledgeCard,changes:Partial<KnowledgeCard>):KnowledgeCard {
    const card={...old,...changes,version:old.version+1,updatedAt:Date.now(),servingDeviceId:this.deviceId};
    card.contentHash=cardHash(card);return card;
  }
  /** Caller wraps this plus the correction itself in a single transaction. */
  invalidateDependents(id:string,status:KnowledgeStatus='withdrawn'):string[] {
    const queue=[id],seen=new Set<string>([id]),affected:string[]=[];
    for(let at=0;at<queue.length;at++){
      if(queue.length>10000)throw new Error('DERIVATION_BUDGET_EXCEEDED');
      const rows=this.db.prepare('SELECT child FROM derivations WHERE parent=?').all(queue[at]) as Row[];
      for(const row of rows){
        const child=String(row.child);if(seen.has(child))continue;
        seen.add(child);queue.push(child);
        const card=this.get(child);if(!card)continue;
        if(card.status!==status)this.put(this.nextVersion(card,{status,lastVerifiedAt:null}),this.kind(child));
        affected.push(child);
      }
    }
    return affected;
  }
  markUnavailable(evidenceId:string):string[] {
    return this.atomic(()=>{
      const loc=this.evidence(evidenceId);if(!loc||!loc.evidence.sourceAvailable)return [];
      loc.evidence.sourceAvailable=false;this.saveEvidence(loc);const ids=this.evidenceCards(evidenceId),affected:string[]=[];
      for(const id of ids) {
        const card=this.get(id);if(!card||card.originDeviceId!==this.deviceId)continue;
        if(!['stale','superseded','withdrawn'].includes(card.status)){
          const evidence=card.evidence.map(e=>e.id===evidenceId?{...e,sourceAvailable:false}:e);
          this.put(this.nextVersion(card,{status:'stale',evidence,lastVerifiedAt:null}),this.kind(id));
          affected.push(id,...this.invalidateDependents(id));
        }
      }
      return [...new Set(affected)];
    });
  }
  query(input:PalaceQueryInput):KnowledgeCard[] {return queryPalaceCards(this.db,this.deviceId,input);}
  search(input:PalaceQueryInput&{query:string;resultLimit:number;lookahead?:boolean}):{items:KnowledgeCard[];candidates:number}{return searchPalaceCards(this.db,this.deviceId,input);}
  count():number {return Number((this.db.prepare('SELECT count(*) n FROM cards').get() as Row).n);}
  importCard(incoming:KnowledgeCard):{imported:boolean;deduplicated:boolean;card:KnowledgeCard} {
    const existing=this.get(incoming.id);
    if(existing && existing.originDeviceId!==incoming.originDeviceId)throw new Error('KNOWLEDGE_ORIGIN_CONFLICT');
    if(existing && incoming.version<existing.version)return {imported:false,deduplicated:true,card:existing};
    if(existing && incoming.version===existing.version) {
      if(existing.contentHash!==incoming.contentHash)throw new Error('KNOWLEDGE_VERSION_CONFLICT');
      return {imported:false,deduplicated:true,card:existing};
    }
    return this.atomic(()=>{
      const card={...incoming,servingDeviceId:this.deviceId,provenance:[...new Set([...incoming.provenance,this.deviceId])]};
      for(const evidence of card.evidence)this.saveEvidence({evidence,type:'remote'});
      this.put(card,existing?this.kind(existing.id):'imported');
      if(existing && existing.contentHash!==card.contentHash)this.invalidateDependents(card.id);
      return {imported:true,deduplicated:false,card};
    });
  }
  stat(id:string):Record<string,unknown> {
    const card=this.get(id);if(!card)throw new Error('KNOWLEDGE_NOT_FOUND');
    return {...preview(card),derivedCount:(this.db.prepare('SELECT count(*) n FROM derivations WHERE parent=?').get(id) as Row).n};
  }
}

===== END src/plugins/palace/store.ts =====

function walk(dir) {
  return fs.readdirSync(dir, { withFileTypes: true }).flatMap(entry => {
    const fullPath = path.join(dir, entry.name);
    return entry.isDirectory() ? walk(fullPath) : [fullPath];
  });
}


// Public/runtime product identity must be vendor-neutral apart from the immutable
// package/domain identity and the v2 wire labels retained for protocol continuity.
const allowedIdentityLiterals = [
  '@hcu-lab.me/mcp-device',
  'https://device.hcu-lab.me',
  'hcu-lab.me',
  'hcu-device-v2-inner-tls',

];

const brandSurfaceFiles = [
  'README.md',
  'package.json',
  ...walk(path.join(root, 'src')).filter(file => /\.(?:ts|json|html|css|md)$/.test(file)),
  ...walk(path.join(root, 'scripts')).filter(file => /\.(?:js|cjs|mjs|ts|json|md)$/.test(file)),
  ...(fs.existsSync(path.join(root, 'skills')) ? walk(path.join(root, 'skills')).filter(file => /\.(?:md|json)$/.test(file)) : [])
].map(file => path.isAbsolute(file) ? path.relative(root, file).replaceAll('\\', '/') : file);
for (const rel of brandSurfaceFiles) {
  let content = read(rel);
  for (const literal of allowedIdentityLiterals) content = content.split(literal).join(' '.repeat(literal.length));

  assert.deepEqual(matches, [], `${rel} exposes legacy/vendor branding: ${matches.join(', ')}`);
}

===== FILE test/test-green-hardening.js sha256=27aa8385badad5bf7cca8f5a3bfddabad7dfb99b6dd5edfa58b3c7590f0924ca =====
import assert from 'assert';
import fs from 'fs/promises';
import os from 'os';
import path from 'path';
import { once } from 'node:events';

// Self-sandbox even when this file is run directly instead of through
// test/run-all-tests.js. No test may touch the user's real ~/.mcp-device.
const sandbox = await fs.mkdtemp(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'mcp-device-green-hardening-'));
process.env.MCP_DEVICE_CONFIG_DIR = sandbox;
process.env.MCP_DEVICE_CONFIG_DIR = sandbox;

const { CONFIG_DIR, CONFIG_FILE, TOOL_CALL_FILE } = await import('../dist/config.js');
const { configManager } = await import('../dist/config-manager.js');
const { TextFileHandler } = await import('../dist/utils/files/text.js');
const { TerminalManager } = await import('../dist/terminal-manager.js');
const { trackToolCall } = await import('../dist/utils/trackTools.js');
const { handleReadFile } = await import('../dist/handlers/filesystem-handlers.js');

try {
  // 1) Corrupt config must be preserved, not silently replaced.
  await fs.mkdir(CONFIG_DIR, { recursive: true });
  await fs.writeFile(CONFIG_FILE, '{"defaultShell":', 'utf8');
  const config = await configManager.getConfig();
  assert(config.defaultShell, 'safe in-memory defaults should remain available');
  await assert.rejects(() => fs.stat(CONFIG_FILE), /ENOENT/);
  const corruptCopies = (await fs.readdir(CONFIG_DIR)).filter(name => /^config\.corrupt-.*\.json$/.test(name));
  assert.equal(corruptCopies.length, 1);
  assert.equal(await fs.readFile(path.join(CONFIG_DIR, corruptCopies[0]), 'utf8'), '{"defaultShell":');

  // A later explicit user-driven save recreates canonical JSON atomically.
  await configManager.setValue('defaultShell', 'test-shell');
  const saved = JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(CONFIG_FILE, 'utf8'));
  assert.equal(saved.defaultShell, 'test-shell');

  // 2) UTF-16LE BOM reads preserve line semantics and hide the BOM.
  const filesDir = path.join(CONFIG_DIR, 'green-hardening-files');
  await fs.mkdir(filesDir, { recursive: true });
  const utf16Path = path.join(filesDir, 'utf16.txt');
  const utf16Text = 'alpha\r\nbeta\r\ngamma\r\n';
  await fs.writeFile(
    utf16Path,
    Buffer.concat([Buffer.from([0xff, 0xfe]), Buffer.from(utf16Text, 'utf16le')])
  );
  const handler = new TextFileHandler();
  const utf16Full = await handler.read(utf16Path, { offset: 0, length: 10 });
  assert(String(utf16Full.content).includes('alpha'));
  assert(String(utf16Full.content).includes('gamma'));
  assert(!String(utf16Full.content).includes('\ufeff'));
  const utf16Slice = await handler.read(utf16Path, { offset: 1, length: 1 });
  assert(String(utf16Slice.content).includes('beta'));
  const utf16Tail = await handler.read(utf16Path, { offset: -1, length: 1 });
  assert(String(utf16Tail.content).includes('gamma'));
  const utf16Info = await handler.getInfo(utf16Path);
  assert.equal(utf16Info.metadata?.lineCount, 3);

  // 3) Early-break reads must release the underlying file handle immediately.
  const earlyPath = path.join(filesDir, 'early-break.txt');
  const renamedPath = path.join(filesDir, 'early-break-renamed.txt');
  await fs.writeFile(earlyPath, Array.from({ length: 500 }, (_, i) => 'line-' + i).join('\n'), 'utf8');
  await handler.read(earlyPath, { offset: 0, length: 1 });
  await fs.rename(earlyPath, renamedPath);
  await fs.rm(renamedPath);

  // 4) Invalid shell must return a tool error without an unhandled ChildProcess error.
  const terminals = new TerminalManager();
  const invalidShell = process.platform === 'win32'
    ? 'C:\\definitely-not-real\\missing-shell.exe'
    : '/definitely-not-real/missing-shell';
  const shellResult = await terminals.executeCommand('echo never-runs', 500, invalidShell, false, filesDir);
  assert(shellResult.pid === -1 || /Process error|Failed to get process ID|ENOENT/i.test(shellResult.output));
  await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 50));

  // A late ChildProcess error after a background session is returned must not
  // silently retire that still-live session.
  const realShell = process.platform === 'win32'
    ? path.join(process.env.SystemRoot || 'C:\\Windows', 'System32', 'WindowsPowerShell', 'v1.0', 'powershell.exe')
    : (process.env.SHELL || '/bin/sh');
  const background = await terminals.executeCommand(
    process.platform === 'win32' ? 'Start-Sleep -Seconds 30' : 'sleep 30',
    100,
    realShell,
    false,
    filesDir
  );
  assert(background.pid > 0 && background.isBlocked === true);
  const liveSession = terminals.sessions.get(background.pid);
  assert(liveSession, 'background session must remain registered');
  liveSession.process.emit('error', new Error('synthetic late error'));
  assert(terminals.sessions.has(background.pid), 'late error must not retire a live session');
  // forceTerminate signals asynchronously; Windows still owns the fixture cwd until close.
  const backgroundClosed = once(liveSession.process, 'close', { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(5000) });
  assert.equal(terminals.forceTerminate(background.pid), true);
  await backgroundClosed;

  // 5) Remote inherited read_file URL support must fail closed before any fetch.
  process.env.MCP_DEVICE_REMOTE = 'true';
  const urlResult = await handleReadFile({ path: 'https://example.invalid/private', isUrl: true });
  assert(/REMOTE_URL_READ_DISABLED/.test(String(urlResult.content?.[0]?.text || '')));

  // 6) Remote tool-call logs must never persist raw secrets/contents.
  const secret = 'SECRET_TEST_VALUE';
  await trackToolCall('write_file', {
    path: path.join(filesDir, 'secret.txt'),
    content: secret,
    Authorization: 'Bearer ' + secret
  });
  const toolLog = await fs.readFile(TOOL_CALL_FILE, 'utf8');
  assert(!toolLog.includes(secret));
  assert(/Args: count=/.test(toolLog));

  console.log('Green hardening regressions passed');
} finally {
  await fs.rm(sandbox, { recursive: true, force: true });
}

process.exit(0);

===== END test/test-green-hardening.js =====

      HOME: helpHome,
      USERPROFILE: helpHome,
      MCP_GATEWAY_URL: ''
    }
  });
  assert.equal(result.error, undefined, result.error?.message);
  assert.equal(result.status, 0, `help exited ${result.status}: ${result.stderr || result.stdout}`);
  const output = `${result.stdout}\n${result.stderr}`;
  assert.match(output, /Usage:\s+mcp-device/);
  assert.match(output, /Commands:\s+start, login, logout, status, install, stop, uninstall/);
  assert.doesNotMatch(output, /\brun\b|autostart|Starting MCP Device/i);
  const platformHelp = spawnSync(process.execPath, ['dist/mcp-device.js', '--help'], {
    cwd: new URL('..', import.meta.url),
    encoding: 'utf8',
    timeout: process.platform === 'win32' ? 20000 : 5000,
    env: { ...process.env, HOME: helpHome, USERPROFILE: helpHome, MCP_GATEWAY_URL: '' }
  });
  assert.equal(platformHelp.error, undefined, platformHelp.error?.message);
  assert.equal(platformHelp.status, 0, platformHelp.stderr);
  assert.equal(platformHelp.stdout.split(/\r?\n/)[0], 'MCP Device ' + pkg.version);
  for (const command of ['start', 'device', 'server', 'admin', 'plugins', 'state']) {
    assert.match(platformHelp.stdout, new RegExp('mcp-device ' + command + '\\b'));
  }
  assert.doesNotMatch(platformHelp.stdout, /Starting MCP Device/);
} finally {
  fs.rmSync(helpHome, { recursive: true, force: true });
}

console.log('MCP Device package identity test passed');

===== FILE test/v12/palace-task-replay.test.js sha256=03d082674955872a165079287a06a995991dd2cf891b17eb98944ace1437da43 =====
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { mkdtempSync, mkdirSync, writeFileSync, readFileSync, existsSync, rmSync } from 'node:fs';
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { spawnSync } from 'node:child_process';
import { performance } from 'node:perf_hooks';
import { createPalaceProvider } from '../../dist/plugins/palace/index.js';

test('deterministic executable task replay compares no-Palace retries with a validated reusable procedure',async t=>{
  const root=mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(),'mcp-v12-palace-replay-')),logs=join(root,'logs');
  mkdirSync(logs);
  const executable=join(root,'settlement.cjs'),ledger=join(root,'ledger.json');
  writeFileSync(executable,[
    "const fs=require('node:fs');",
    "const [ledger,key,timeout]=process.argv.slice(2);",
    "if(Number(timeout)<250){console.log(JSON.stringify({ok:false,error:'SQLITE_BUSY',operation:'settlement',retryable:true}));process.exit(75);}",
    "const entries=fs.existsSync(ledger)?JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(ledger,'utf8')):{};",
    "entries[key]=entries[key]||{chargeCount:1,key};",
    "fs.writeFileSync(ledger,JSON.stringify(entries));",
    "console.log(JSON.stringify({ok:true,key,chargeCount:entries[key].chargeCount}));"
  ].join('\n'));
  const execute=(key,timeout)=>{
    const result=spawnSync(process.execPath,[executable,ledger,key,String(timeout)],{encoding:'utf8',timeout:5000});
    const state=existsSync(ledger)?JSON.parse(readFileSync(ledger,'utf8')):{};
    return {exitCode:result.status??99,stdout:result.stdout,stderr:result.stderr,
      postcondition:result.status===0&&state[key]?.chargeCount===1&&state[key]?.key===key};
  };
  // Prior source claims are separate from held-out task IDs. A trusted runner must execute
  // the proposed recipe and inspect the resulting state before it becomes an active Skill.
  const observation='Error SQLITE_BUSY during settlement. Fix: Set busyTimeoutMs = 250 and preserve the original idempotency key before retry.';
  writeFileSync(join(logs,'prior.log'),observation+'\n'+observation+'\n');
  let validationCalls=0;
  const provider=createPalaceProvider({deviceId:'replay',dataRoot:join(root,'state'),sourceRoots:[logs],autoIndex:true,
    validationRunner:async card=>{
      validationCalls++;
      const timeout=Number(card.body.match(/busyTimeoutMs\s*=\s*(\d+)/)?.[1]??0);
      const result=execute('training-validation-'+validationCalls,timeout);
      return {executionId:'replay-validation-'+validationCalls,exitCode:result.exitCode,postconditionPassed:result.postcondition,
        output:result.stdout+result.stderr,validatorVersion:'settlement-fixture/1'};
    }});
  t.after(async()=>{await provider.stop();rmSync(root,{recursive:true,force:true});});
  await provider.start();
  // Ingestion may propose a candidate, but log text never reaches the execution validator automatically.
  const candidates=await provider.invoke('knowledge.skills',{includeCandidates:true});
  assert.ok(candidates.items.some(item=>item.status==='hypothesis'));assert.equal(validationCalls,0);
  const observations=await provider.invoke('knowledge.search',{query:'SQLITE_BUSY busyTimeoutMs',limit:3,maxBytes:4096});
  const procedureIds=new Set(candidates.items.map(item=>item.id));
  const evidence=observations.items.find(item=>!procedureIds.has(item.id));assert.ok(evidence);
  const promoted=await provider.invoke('knowledge.learn',{key:'explicit-settlement-retry',summary:'Validated settlement retry procedure',
    steps:['Set busyTimeoutMs = 250 and preserve the original idempotency key before retry.'],evidenceRefs:[evidence.id]});
  assert.equal(promoted.activated,true);assert.equal(validationCalls,1);
  const active=await provider.invoke('knowledge.skills',{});
  assert.equal(active.items.length,1);
  const tasks=['held-out-a','held-out-b','held-out-c'];
  const baseline={taskSuccess:0,repeatedErrors:0,executionCalls:0,retrievalCalls:0,agentVisibleBytes:0,elapsedMs:0};
  let began=performance.now();
  for(const task of tasks){
    let previousError=false;
    // Fixed baseline policy: retry default once, then use its diagnostic fallback.
    for(const timeout of [0,0,250]){
      const result=execute('baseline-'+task,timeout);baseline.executionCalls++;
      baseline.agentVisibleBytes+=Buffer.byteLength(result.stdout+result.stderr);
      if(result.exitCode!==0){if(previousError)baseline.repeatedErrors++;previousError=true;}
      if(result.postcondition){baseline.taskSuccess++;break;}
    }
  }
  baseline.elapsedMs=performance.now()-began;
  const palace={taskSuccess:0,repeatedErrors:0,executionCalls:0,retrievalCalls:0,agentVisibleBytes:0,elapsedMs:0};
  began=performance.now();
  const found=await provider.invoke('knowledge.search',{query:'retry idempotency',limit:3,maxBytes:4096});palace.retrievalCalls++;
  palace.agentVisibleBytes+=Buffer.byteLength(JSON.stringify(found));
  const selected=found.items.find(item=>item.status==='validated');assert.ok(selected);
  const read=await provider.invoke('knowledge.skills',{action:'read',ref:selected.id,maxBytes:8192});palace.retrievalCalls++;
  palace.agentVisibleBytes+=Buffer.byteLength(JSON.stringify(read));
  const remembered=Number(read.body.match(/busyTimeoutMs\s*=\s*(\d+)/)?.[1]??0);
  for(const task of tasks){
    const result=execute('palace-'+task,remembered);palace.executionCalls++;
    palace.agentVisibleBytes+=Buffer.byteLength(result.stdout+result.stderr);
    if(result.postcondition)palace.taskSuccess++;
  }
  palace.elapsedMs=performance.now()-began;
  const result={benchmark:'palace-executable-replay-v1',node:process.version,tasks:tasks.length,validationCalls,
    taskPrompt:'Complete each new settlement after a reconnect while preserving its supplied idempotency key.',
    baseline,palace,
    limits:['Deterministic fixture policies; no LLM productivity or general task-success claim.',
      'Baseline retries once before its fixed diagnostic fallback; Palace reuses one selected validated recipe across three new task IDs.',
      'All tasks execute a real child process and inspect ledger postconditions.',
      'Agent-visible bytes include retrieval metadata/evidence and may increase despite fewer repeated errors.',
      'Training/validation cost is reported separately and is not hidden in the replay improvement.']};
  console.log('PALACE_TASK_REPLAY '+JSON.stringify(result));
  if(process.env.MCP_V12_PALACE_REPLAY_OUTPUT)writeFileSync(process.env.MCP_V12_PALACE_REPLAY_OUTPUT,JSON.stringify(result,null,2)+'\n');
  assert.equal(baseline.taskSuccess,3);assert.equal(palace.taskSuccess,3);
  assert.equal(baseline.repeatedErrors,3);assert.equal(palace.repeatedErrors,0);
  assert.equal(palace.executionCalls,3);
});

===== END test/v12/palace-task-replay.test.js =====

===== FILE test/v12/plugin-host.test.js sha256=fc302e76aae31de88cdce79b5bc0c3b9b181f8807959bc7a0e9b4df6166382fd =====
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import fs from 'node:fs/promises';
import os from 'node:os';
import path from 'node:path';
import { randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';
import { PluginHost } from '../../dist/platform/plugin-host.js';
import { compatible } from '../../dist/platform/plugin-contract.js';

async function fixture(t) {
  const root = await fs.mkdtemp(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'mcp-v12-plugin-'));
  const host = new PluginHost({ stateRoot: root, deviceId: 'fixture-device' });
  t.after(async () => { await host.close(); await fs.rm(root, { recursive: true, force: true }); });
  const write = async (name, body) => { const entry = path.join(root, name + '.mjs'); await fs.writeFile(entry, body); return entry; };
  const manifest = (id, entry, caps = [{ name: 'text.echo', version: '1.0.0' }], extra = {}) => ({ id, version: '1.0.0', dataSchemaVersion: 1, isolation: 'in-process', trusted: true, entry, capabilities: caps, timeoutMs: 1000, ...extra });
  return { root, host, write, manifest };
}

test('unrelated plugin lifecycle preserves data and Core-owned state; reinstall resumes', async t => {
  const { root, host, write, manifest } = await fixture(t);
  const coreMarker = path.join(root, 'identity-marker'); await fs.writeFile(coreMarker, 'do not own this');
  const entry = await write('echo', `import fs from 'node:fs/promises'; import path from 'node:path'; export function createPlugin(c){return {async start(){await fs.writeFile(path.join(c.dataRoot,'retained.txt'),'retained')},invoke(n,i){return {echo:i,device:c.deviceId}}}}`);
  const m = manifest('reference', entry); await host.install(m, { enabled: true }); await host.start();
  assert.deepEqual(await host.invoke('text.echo', { text: 'hello' }), { echo: { text: 'hello' }, device: 'fixture-device' });
  await host.disable('reference'); assert.deepEqual(host.capabilities(), []);
  assert.equal(await fs.readFile(path.join(host.dataRoot('reference'), 'retained.txt'), 'utf8'), 'retained');
  await host.enable('reference'); assert.deepEqual(host.capabilities(), ['text.echo']);
  await host.uninstall('reference'); assert.equal(await fs.readFile(coreMarker, 'utf8'), 'do not own this');
  assert.equal(await fs.readFile(path.join(host.dataRoot('reference'), 'retained.txt'), 'utf8'), 'retained');
  await host.install(m, { enabled: true }); assert.deepEqual(await host.invoke('text.echo', 42), { echo: 42, device: 'fixture-device' });
  await host.disable('reference'); await host.purge('reference'); await assert.rejects(fs.stat(host.dataRoot('reference')), { code: 'ENOENT' });
});

test('capability dependencies, priority, augmentation and graceful loss are generic', async t => {
  const { host, write, manifest } = await fixture(t);
  const base = await write('base', `export const createPlugin=()=>({invoke:(n,i)=>({value:i})});`);
  const augment = await write('augment', `export const createPlugin=()=>({invoke:(n,{result})=>({...result,augmented:true})});`);
  await host.install(manifest('base', base, [{ name: 'text.echo', version: '1.0.0', priority: 10 }]), { enabled: true });
  await host.install(manifest('augment', augment, [{ name: 'text.echo', version: '1.0.0', role: 'augmenter' }], { requires: [{ name: 'text.echo', version: '^1.0.0' }] }), { enabled: true });
  await host.start(); assert.deepEqual(await host.invoke('text.echo', 'x'), { value: 'x', augmented: true });
  await host.disable('base'); assert.deepEqual(host.capabilities(), []); assert.equal(host.list().find(p => p.manifest.id === 'augment').state, 'faulted');
});

test('missing dependencies and cycles fail without bringing down the host', async t => {
  const { host, write, manifest } = await fixture(t);
  const entry = await write('plugin', `export const createPlugin=()=>({invoke:()=>true});`);
  await host.install(manifest('one', entry, [{ name: 'cap.one', version: '1.0.0' }], { requires: [{ name: 'cap.two', version: '1.x' }] }), { enabled: true });
  await host.install(manifest('two', entry, [{ name: 'cap.two', version: '1.0.0' }], { requires: [{ name: 'cap.one', version: '1.x' }] }), { enabled: true });
  await host.start(); assert.deepEqual(host.capabilities(), []); assert.ok(host.list().every(p => p.state === 'faulted'));
  await host.install(manifest('independent', entry, [{ name: 'cap.independent', version: '1.0.0' }]), { enabled: true });
  assert.equal(await host.invoke('cap.independent', {}), true);
});

test('process crash and synchronous hang are isolated and bounded', { timeout: 15000 }, async t => {
  const { host, write, manifest } = await fixture(t);
  const entry = await write('fatal', `export const createPlugin=()=>({invoke(n,i){if(i==='crash')process.exit(19);while(true){}}});`);
  await host.install(manifest('fatal', entry, [{ name: 'fault.run', version: '1.0.0' }], { isolation: 'process', trusted: false, timeoutMs: 1000 }), { enabled: true });
  await host.start(); await assert.rejects(host.invoke('fault.run', 'crash'));
  await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 100)); assert.deepEqual(host.capabilities(), []);
  await host.enable('fatal'); const start = Date.now(); await assert.rejects(host.invoke('fault.run', 'hang'), error => error.code === 'PLUGIN_TIMEOUT');
  assert.ok(Date.now() - start < 5000); await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 100));
  assert.equal(host.list()[0].state, 'faulted'); assert.equal(2 + 2, 4);
});

test('cooperative cancellation acknowledges the original request and keeps the process provider healthy', { timeout: 10000 }, async t => {
  const { host, write, manifest } = await fixture(t);
  const entry = await write('cooperative-cancel', `export const createPlugin=()=>({invoke(n,i,c){if(i!=='wait')return 'ok';return new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{const timer=setTimeout(()=>resolve('late'),5000);c.signal.addEventListener('abort',()=>{clearTimeout(timer);reject(Object.assign(new Error('cancelled'),{code:'CANCELLED'}))},{once:true})})}});`);
  await host.install(manifest('cooperative-cancel', entry, [{ name: 'cancel.run', version: '1.0.0' }], { isolation: 'process', trusted: false, timeoutMs: 3000 }), { enabled: true });
  await host.start();
  const controller = new AbortController();
  const pending = host.invoke('cancel.run', 'wait', { signal: controller.signal });
  setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 50);
  await assert.rejects(pending, { code: 'CANCELLED' });
  await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 150));
  assert.equal(host.list()[0].state, 'enabled');
  assert.deepEqual(host.capabilities(), ['cancel.run']);
  assert.equal(await host.invoke('cancel.run', 'next'), 'ok');
});

test('ordinary domain errors preserve a cooperative provider; thrown exceptions fault only that plugin', async t => {
  const { host, write, manifest } = await fixture(t);
  const entry = await write('errors', `export const createPlugin=()=>({invoke(n,i){if(i==='invalid')throw Object.assign(new Error('input'),{code:'INVALID_ARGUMENT'});if(i==='throw')throw new Error('bug');return 'ok'}});`);
  await host.install(manifest('errors', entry), { enabled: true }); await host.start();
  await assert.rejects(host.invoke('text.echo', 'invalid'), { code: 'INVALID_ARGUMENT' }); assert.equal(await host.invoke('text.echo', 'valid'), 'ok');
  await assert.rejects(host.invoke('text.echo', 'throw')); await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 50)); assert.equal(host.list()[0].state, 'faulted');
});

test('explicit upgrade migrates private data and failed migration rolls back', async t => {
  const { host, write, manifest } = await fixture(t);
  const v1 = await write('v1', `import fs from 'node:fs/promises';import path from 'node:path';export const createPlugin=c=>({async start(){try{await fs.access(path.join(c.dataRoot,'value'))}catch{await fs.writeFile(path.join(c.dataRoot,'value'),'one')}},invoke:()=>1});`);
  await host.install(manifest('versioned', v1), { enabled: true }); await host.start();
  const v2 = await write('v2', `import fs from 'node:fs/promises';import path from 'node:path';export async function migrate(o){await fs.writeFile(path.join(o.dataRoot,'value'),'two')}export const createPlugin=()=>({invoke:()=>2});`);
  await host.upgrade(manifest('versioned', v2, undefined, { version: '2.0.0', dataSchemaVersion: 2 }));
  assert.equal(await host.invoke('text.echo', {}), 2); assert.equal(await fs.readFile(path.join(host.dataRoot('versioned'), 'value'), 'utf8'), 'two');
  const bad = await write('bad', `import fs from 'node:fs/promises';import path from 'node:path';export async function migrate(o){await fs.writeFile(path.join(o.dataRoot,'value'),'corrupted');throw new Error('migration fail')}export const createPlugin=()=>({invoke:()=>3});`);
  await assert.rejects(host.upgrade(manifest('versioned', bad, undefined, { version: '3.0.0', dataSchemaVersion: 3 })));
  assert.equal(await fs.readFile(path.join(host.dataRoot('versioned'), 'value'), 'utf8'), 'two'); assert.equal(await host.invoke('text.echo', {}), 2);
});

test('upgrade intent recovery restores or commits complete generations after process loss', async t => {
  async function scenario(phase) {
    const root = await fs.mkdtemp(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'mcp-v12-upgrade-recovery-'));
    const entry1 = path.join(root, 'v1.mjs'), entry2 = path.join(root, 'v2.mjs');
    await fs.writeFile(entry1, `export const createPlugin=()=>({invoke:()=>1});`);
    await fs.writeFile(entry2, `export const createPlugin=()=>({invoke:()=>2});`);
    const manifest = (entry, version) => ({ id: 'recoverable', version, dataSchemaVersion: 1, isolation: 'in-process', trusted: true, entry, capabilities: [{ name: 'recover.value', version: '1.0.0' }], timeoutMs: 1000 });
    const first = new PluginHost({ stateRoot: root, deviceId: 'fixture' });
    await first.install(manifest(entry1, '1.0.0'), { enabled: true });
    await fs.mkdir(first.dataRoot('recoverable'), { recursive: true });
    await fs.writeFile(path.join(first.dataRoot('recoverable'), 'value'), 'old');
    await first.close();
    const registryPath = path.join(root, 'plugins', 'registry.json');
    const registry = JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(registryPath, 'utf8'));
    const previous = structuredClone(registry.plugins.recoverable), next = structuredClone(previous);
    next.manifest = manifest(entry2, '2.0.0'); next.state = 'enabled';
    const token = randomUUID(), pluginRoot = path.join(root, 'plugins', 'recoverable');
    const candidateName = 'upgrade-candidate-' + token, backupName = 'upgrade-backup-' + token, failedName = 'upgrade-failed-' + token;
    const data = path.join(pluginRoot, 'data'), candidate = path.join(pluginRoot, candidateName), backup = path.join(pluginRoot, backupName);
    await fs.cp(data, candidate, { recursive: true }); await fs.writeFile(path.join(candidate, 'value'), 'new');
    if (phase !== 'pre-swap') {
      await fs.rename(data, backup); if (phase !== 'swapping') await fs.rename(candidate, data);
    }
    if (phase === 'swapped' || phase === 'committed') {
      registry.revision += 1; registry.plugins.recoverable = next; await fs.writeFile(registryPath, JSON.stringify(registry, null, 2) + '\n');
    }
    const intent = { version: 1, id: 'recoverable', token, pid: 2147483646, startedAt: Date.now(),
      phase: phase === 'pre-swap' ? 'candidate-ready' : phase, candidate: candidateName, backup: backupName, failed: failedName, previous, next };
    await fs.writeFile(path.join(pluginRoot, 'upgrade-intent.json'), JSON.stringify(intent, null, 2) + '\n');
    const recovered = new PluginHost({ stateRoot: root, deviceId: 'fixture' }); await recovered.load();
    const expected = phase === 'committed' ? 'new' : 'old';
    assert.equal(await fs.readFile(path.join(recovered.dataRoot('recoverable'), 'value'), 'utf8'), expected);
    assert.equal(recovered.list()[0].manifest.version, phase === 'committed' ? '2.0.0' : '1.0.0');
    for (const name of ['upgrade-intent.json', candidateName, backupName, failedName]) await assert.rejects(fs.lstat(path.join(pluginRoot, name)), { code: 'ENOENT' });
    await recovered.close(); await fs.rm(root, { recursive: true, force: true, maxRetries: 5, retryDelay: 50 });
  }
  await scenario('pre-swap'); await scenario('swapping'); await scenario('swapped'); await scenario('committed');
});

test('uninstall tombstone survives restart; bundle does not silently reinstall', async t => {
  const { root, host, write, manifest } = await fixture(t);
  const entry = await write('bundle', `export const createPlugin=()=>({invoke:()=>true});`), m = manifest('bundle', entry);

  const restarted = new PluginHost({ stateRoot: root, deviceId: 'fixture-device' }); t.after(() => restarted.close());
  await restarted.ensureBundled([m]); await restarted.start(); assert.deepEqual(restarted.capabilities(), []); assert.equal(restarted.list()[0].state, 'uninstalled');
});

test('external lifecycle changes remove dependent capability advertisements without restart', { timeout: 10000 }, async t => {
  const { root, host, write, manifest } = await fixture(t);
  const entry = await write('external', `export const createPlugin=()=>({invoke:()=>true});`);
  await host.install(manifest('provider', entry, [{ name: 'base.value', version: '1.0.0' }]), { enabled: true });
  await host.install(manifest('dependent', entry, [{ name: 'derived.value', version: '1.0.0' }], { requires: [{ name: 'base.value', version: '1.x' }] }), { enabled: true });
  await host.start(); let updates = 0; host.onCapabilitiesChanged(() => updates++);
  const management = new PluginHost({ stateRoot: root, deviceId: 'management' });
  await management.disable('provider'); await management.close();
  const deadline = Date.now() + 5000;
  while (host.capabilities().length && Date.now() < deadline) await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 100));
  assert.deepEqual(host.capabilities(), []); assert.ok(updates > 0);
  assert.equal(host.list().find(p => p.manifest.id === 'dependent').state, 'faulted');
});

test('capability version ranges do not silently cross incompatible zero-major versions', () => {
  assert.equal(compatible('1.9.0', '^1.2.0'), true);
  assert.equal(compatible('2.0.0', '^1.2.0'), false);
  assert.equal(compatible('0.3.0', '^0.2.1'), false);
  assert.equal(compatible('0.2.9', '^0.2.1'), true);
  assert.equal(compatible('0.0.2', '^0.0.1'), false);
  assert.equal(compatible('1.5.0', '1.x'), true);
});

test('unproven process termination blocks host closure and replacement until resource release succeeds', { timeout: 15000 }, async t => {
  const { host, write, manifest } = await fixture(t);
  const entry = await write('retained-process', "export const createPlugin=()=>({invoke:()=>true});");
  await host.install(manifest('retained-process', entry, undefined, { isolation: 'process', trusted: false }), { enabled: true });
  await host.start();
  // Simulate an OS refusing termination after the active advertisement is removed.
  // Public close/enable must still retain ownership of that exact live driver.
  const driver = host.drivers.get('retained-process'), originalStop = driver.stop.bind(driver);
  let attempts = 0;
  driver.stop = async () => { attempts++; throw Object.assign(new Error('fixture OS termination refusal'), { code: 'PLUGIN_TERMINATION_FAILED' }); };
  try {
    await assert.rejects(host.disable('retained-process'), { code: 'PLUGIN_TERMINATION_FAILED' });
    assert.deepEqual(host.capabilities(), []);
    await assert.rejects(host.enable('retained-process'), { code: 'PLUGIN_TERMINATION_FAILED' });
    await assert.rejects(host.close(), { code: 'PLUGIN_TERMINATION_FAILED' });
    assert.ok(attempts >= 3, 'Retired process ownership was forgotten');
  } finally {
    driver.stop = originalStop;
    await host.close();
  }
});

test('a cooperative cleanup rejection retains its instance and is retried before closure succeeds', async t => {
  const { host, write, manifest } = await fixture(t);
  const entry = await write('cooperative-cleanup', "import fs from 'node:fs/promises';import path from 'node:path';export const createPlugin=c=>{let attempts=0;return {invoke:()=>true,async stop(){if(++attempts===1)throw new Error('fixture cleanup refused');await fs.writeFile(path.join(c.dataRoot,'cleanup-complete'),String(attempts))}}};");
  await host.install(manifest('cooperative-cleanup', entry), { enabled: true }); await host.start();
  const concurrent = await Promise.allSettled([host.close(), host.close()]);
  for (const result of concurrent) { assert.equal(result.status, 'rejected'); assert.equal(result.reason.code, 'PLUGIN_TERMINATION_FAILED'); }
  assert.deepEqual(host.capabilities(), []);
  await assert.rejects(fs.stat(path.join(host.dataRoot('cooperative-cleanup'), 'cleanup-complete')), { code: 'ENOENT' });
  await host.close();
  assert.equal(await fs.readFile(path.join(host.dataRoot('cooperative-cleanup'), 'cleanup-complete'), 'utf8'), '2');
});
