===== FILE test/v12/plugin-services.test.js sha256=7719c947fb25013596189d71b408df4bcc8b3c7fe3245abfca2e29975d8dda9e =====
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import fs from 'node:fs/promises';
import os from 'node:os';
import path from 'node:path';
import { spawnSync } from 'node:child_process';
import { PluginHost } from '../../dist/platform/plugin-host.js';
import { createPluginServices } from '../../dist/platform/plugin-services.js';

async function fixture(t) {
  const root = await fs.mkdtemp(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'mcp-v12-services-'));
  const host = new PluginHost({ stateRoot: root, deviceId: 'service-fixture' });
  t.after(async () => { await host.close(); await fs.rm(root, { recursive: true, force: true, maxRetries: 5, retryDelay: 50 }); });
  const write = async (name, text, extension = 'mjs') => {
    const entry = path.join(root, name + '.' + extension); await fs.writeFile(entry, text); return entry;
  };
  const manifest = (id, entry, capability, extra = {}) => ({
    id, entry, version: '1.0.0', dataSchemaVersion: 1, isolation: 'in-process', trusted: true,
    capabilities: [{ name: capability, version: '1.2.0' }], timeoutMs: 2000, ...extra
  });
  return { root, host, write, manifest };
}
async function until(check, message) {
  const deadline = Date.now() + 4000;
  while (Date.now() < deadline) { if (await check()) return; await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 20)); }
  assert.fail(message);
}

test('service binding restricts declarations and versions, carries cancellation, and closes with its parent scope', async () => {
  const controller = new AbortController(), calls = [];
  const requirements = [{ name: 'math.scale', version: '^1.2.0' }];
  const services = createPluginServices(requirements, async (name, input, options) => {
    calls.push({ name, input, options }); return Number(input) * 3;
  }, controller.signal);
  requirements[0].version = '*';
  assert.equal(await services.invoke('math.scale', 7), 21);
  assert.equal(calls[0].options.version, '^1.2.0');
  assert.equal(calls[0].options.compose, false);
  assert.equal(calls[0].options.signal, controller.signal);
  await assert.rejects(services.invoke('private.read', {}), { code: 'CAPABILITY_DEPENDENCY_UNDECLARED' });
  assert.equal(calls.length, 1);
  controller.abort();
  await assert.rejects(services.invoke('math.scale', 9), { code: 'CANCELLED' });
  assert.equal(calls.length, 1);
});

for (const isolation of ['in-process', 'process']) {
  test(isolation + ' dependent really invokes its version-compatible provider during init and invocation', { timeout: 15000 }, async t => {
    const { host, write, manifest } = await fixture(t);
    const old = await write('old', "export const createPlugin=()=>({invoke:()=>({wrongVersion:true})});");
    const provider = await write('provider', "export const createPlugin=()=>{let count=0;return {invoke(n,i){return {scaled:Number(i)*3,count:++count}}}};");
    const dependent = await write('dependent', "export async function createPlugin(c){const initial=await c.services.invoke('math.scale',2);return {async invoke(n,i,ctx){if(i==='expired')return c.services.invoke('math.scale',9);return {initial,live:await ctx.services.invoke('math.scale',i)}}}}");
    await host.install(manifest('old', old, 'math.scale', { capabilities: [{ name: 'math.scale', version: '1.0.0', priority: 100 }] }), { enabled: true });
    await host.install(manifest('provider', provider, 'math.scale'), { enabled: true });
    await host.install(manifest('dependent', dependent, 'math.derived', {
      isolation, trusted: isolation === 'in-process', requires: [{ name: 'math.scale', version: '^1.2.0' }]
    }), { enabled: true });
    await host.start();
    assert.deepEqual(await host.invoke('math.derived', 7), { initial: { scaled: 6, count: 1 }, live: { scaled: 21, count: 2 } });
    await assert.rejects(host.invoke('math.derived', 'expired'), { code: 'CANCELLED' });
    assert.deepEqual(await host.invoke('math.derived', 8), { initial: { scaled: 6, count: 1 }, live: { scaled: 24, count: 3 } });
  });
}

test('an augmenter can call its declared provider without composing itself recursively', async t => {
  const { host, write, manifest } = await fixture(t);
  const base = await write('base', "export const createPlugin=()=>({invoke:(n,i)=>({value:i})});");
  const augment = await write('augment', "export const createPlugin=()=>({async invoke(n,{input,result},ctx){return {...result,derived:await ctx.services.invoke('text.base',input+'!')}}});");
  await host.install(manifest('base', base, 'text.base'), { enabled: true });
  await host.install(manifest('augment', augment, 'text.base', {
    capabilities: [{ name: 'text.base', version: '1.2.0', role: 'augmenter' }], requires: [{ name: 'text.base', version: '^1.0.0' }]
  }), { enabled: true });
  await host.start();
  assert.deepEqual(await host.invoke('text.base', 'hello'), { value: 'hello', derived: { value: 'hello!' } });
});

test('undeclared services are denied before a real provider receives a call', async t => {
  const { host, write, manifest } = await fixture(t);
  const provider = await write('secret', "export const createPlugin=()=>{let count=0;return {invoke(){return ++count}}};");
  const dependent = await write('consumer', "export const createPlugin=()=>({invoke:(n,i,c)=>c.services.invoke('private.value',i)});");
  await host.install(manifest('secret', provider, 'private.value'), { enabled: true });
  await host.install(manifest('consumer', dependent, 'derived.value', { isolation: 'process', trusted: false }), { enabled: true });
  await host.start();
  await assert.rejects(host.invoke('derived.value', 1), { code: 'CAPABILITY_DEPENDENCY_UNDECLARED' });
  assert.equal(await host.invoke('private.value', {}), 1);
  assert.ok(host.capabilities().includes('derived.value'));
});

test('a non-Node JSON-lines sidecar invokes dependencies and callback ids cannot settle ordinary requests', { timeout: 15000 }, async t => {
  let executable=process.env.DEVICE_PARITY_PYTHON;
  if(!executable&&process.platform==='win32'){
    const resolved=spawnSync('py',['-3','-c','import sys;print(sys.executable)'],{encoding:'utf8',windowsHide:true});
    if(resolved.status===0)executable=resolved.stdout.trim();
  }
  executable??=process.platform==='win32'?'python':'python3';
  if (spawnSync(executable, ['--version'], { windowsHide: true }).status !== 0) { t.skip('Python executable unavailable'); return; }
  const { host, write, manifest } = await fixture(t);
  const provider = await write('scale', "export const createPlugin=()=>({invoke:(n,i)=>({scaled:i*5})});");
  const python = await write('external', [
    'import sys,json',
    'pending={}',
    'def send(value):',
    ' print(json.dumps(value),flush=True)',
    'for line in sys.stdin:',
    ' m=json.loads(line)',
    ' if m.get("type")=="host_result":',
    '  parent=pending.pop(m["id"],None)',
    '  if parent:',
    '   if "error" in m: send({"id":parent,"error":m["error"]})',
    '   else: send({"id":parent,"result":{"transport":"python","provider":m.get("result")}})',
    ' elif m.get("method")=="init": send({"id":m["id"],"result":{"ready":True}})',
    ' elif m.get("method")=="invoke":',
    '  pending[m["id"]]=m["id"]',
    '  value=m["params"]["input"]',
    '  send({"type":"host_call","id":m["id"],"parent_id":"expired" if value=="expired" else m["id"],"method":"capability.invoke","params":{"name":"math.external","input":value}})',
    ' elif m.get("method")=="stop":',
    '  send({"id":m["id"],"result":{"stopped":True}})',
    '  break',
    ''
  ].join('\n'), 'py');
  await host.install(manifest('scale', provider, 'math.external'), { enabled: true });
  await host.install(manifest('external', python, 'derived.external', {
    isolation: 'process', trusted: false, executable, args: [python], requires: [{ name: 'math.external', version: '1.x' }]
  }), { enabled: true });
  await host.start();
  assert.deepEqual(await host.invoke('derived.external', 6), { transport: 'python', provider: { scaled: 30 } });
  await assert.rejects(host.invoke('derived.external', 'expired'), { code: 'CAPABILITY_PARENT_EXPIRED' });
  assert.deepEqual(await host.invoke('derived.external', 9), { transport: 'python', provider: { scaled: 45 } });
});

for (const trigger of ['cancel', 'deadline', 'disable']) {
  test('process dependency work is cancelled on parent ' + trigger, { timeout: 15000 }, async t => {
    const { host, write, manifest } = await fixture(t);
    const provider = await write('waiting', "export const createPlugin=()=>{let started=0,aborted=0;return {invoke(n,i,c){if(i==='status')return {started,aborted};started++;return new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{const timer=setTimeout(()=>resolve('late'),8000);c.signal.addEventListener('abort',()=>{aborted++;clearTimeout(timer);reject(Object.assign(new Error('cancelled'),{code:'CANCELLED'}))},{once:true})})}}};");
    const dependent = await write('waiter', "export const createPlugin=()=>({invoke:(n,i,c)=>c.services.invoke('wait.base',i)});");
    await host.install(manifest('waiting', provider, 'wait.base', { timeoutMs: 10000 }), { enabled: true });
    await host.install(manifest('waiter', dependent, 'wait.derived', {
      isolation: 'process', trusted: false, timeoutMs: trigger === 'deadline' ? 1200 : 4000,
      requires: [{ name: 'wait.base', version: '^1.0.0' }]
    }), { enabled: true });
    await host.start();
    const controller = new AbortController();
    const count = trigger === 'disable' ? 4 : 1;
    const outcomes = Array.from({ length: count }, () => host.invoke('wait.derived', 'wait', { signal: controller.signal })
      .then(value => ({ value }), error => ({ error })));
    await until(async () => (await host.invoke('wait.base', 'status')).started === count, 'Provider never started all requests');
    if (trigger === 'cancel') controller.abort();
    if (trigger === 'disable') await host.disable('waiter');
    for (const completed of await Promise.all(outcomes)) {
      assert.ok(completed.error, 'Cancelled dependency unexpectedly succeeded');
      assert.equal(completed.error.code, trigger === 'deadline' ? 'PLUGIN_TIMEOUT' : trigger === 'disable' ? 'PLUGIN_STOPPED' : 'CANCELLED');
    }
    await until(async () => (await host.invoke('wait.base', 'status')).aborted === count, 'Provider did not receive all aborts');
    assert.ok(host.capabilities().includes('wait.base'));
  });
}

test('loss of a pinned provider cannot turn a formerly acyclic dependency graph into fallback recursion', async t => {
  const { host, write, manifest } = await fixture(t);
  const entry = await write('graph', "export const createPlugin=()=>({invoke:()=>true});");
  await host.install(manifest('root-provider', entry, 'graph.x', { capabilities: [{ name: 'graph.x', version: '1.2.0', priority: 100 }] }), { enabled: true });
  await host.install(manifest('middle', entry, 'graph.y', { requires: [{ name: 'graph.x', version: '1.x' }] }), { enabled: true });
  await host.install(manifest('leaf', entry, 'graph.x', { requires: [{ name: 'graph.y', version: '1.x' }] }), { enabled: true });
  await host.start();
  assert.deepEqual(host.capabilities(), ['graph.x', 'graph.y']);
  await host.disable('root-provider');
  assert.deepEqual(host.capabilities(), []);
  assert.equal(host.list().find(item => item.manifest.id === 'middle').state, 'faulted');
  assert.equal(host.list().find(item => item.manifest.id === 'leaf').state, 'faulted');
  await assert.rejects(host.enable('middle'), { code: 'CAPABILITY_CYCLE' });
  assert.deepEqual(host.capabilities(), []);
});

===== FILE test/v12/server-package-cold.test.mjs sha256=d9358e60e1c1210e8d6c69c084612890c786ae0a069eba4b48fd3cf2e6baf2f9 =====
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import fs from 'node:fs/promises';
import os from 'node:os';
import path from 'node:path';
import { fork, execFile } from 'node:child_process';
import { promisify } from 'node:util';
import { createHash, randomBytes } from 'node:crypto';
import { pathToFileURL } from 'node:url';
const exec = promisify(execFile);
const installed = process.env.MCP_V12_COLD_PACKAGE_ROOT;
const base = 'http://127.0.0.1:8104';
let rpcId = 0;
async function http(route, { cookie, token, form, json, ...options } = {}) {
  const headers = { ...options.headers };
  if (cookie) headers.cookie = cookie;
  if (token) headers.authorization = 'Bearer ' + token;
  if (form) headers['content-type'] = 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded';
  if (json) headers['content-type'] = 'application/json';
  return fetch(base + route, { ...options, redirect: 'manual', signal: AbortSignal.timeout(10000), headers, ...(form ? { method: 'POST', body: new URLSearchParams(form) } : {}), ...(json ? { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify(json) } : {}) });
}
function hidden(html, name) { const value = html.match(new RegExp('name="' + name + '" value="([^"]+)"'))?.[1]; assert.ok(value); return value; }
async function rpc(token, method, params = {}) {
  const id = ++rpcId;
  const response = await http('/mcp', { token, headers: { accept: 'application/json, text/event-stream', 'mcp-protocol-version': '2025-11-25' }, json: { jsonrpc: '2.0', id, method, params } });
  assert.equal(response.status, 200);
  const text = await response.text();
  return response.headers.get('content-type')?.includes('text/event-stream') ? text.split(/\r?\n/).filter(line => line.startsWith('data:')).map(line => JSON.parse(line.slice(5))).find(message => message.id === id) : JSON.parse(text);
}
async function authorize(cookie) {
  const callback = base + '/package-fixture-callback', verifier = randomBytes(32).toString('base64url');
  const registration = await http('/register', { json: { client_name: 'cold-package-acceptance', redirect_uris: [callback], token_endpoint_auth_method: 'none', grant_types: ['authorization_code', 'refresh_token'], response_types: ['code'] } });
  assert.equal(registration.status, 201); const client = await registration.json();
  const params = new URLSearchParams({ response_type: 'code', client_id: client.client_id, redirect_uri: callback, code_challenge: createHash('sha256').update(verifier).digest('base64url'), code_challenge_method: 'S256', scope: 'mcp:tools offline_access', state: 'cold-package', resource: base + '/mcp' });
  const gate = await http('/authorize?' + params, { cookie }); assert.equal(gate.status, 200); const html = await gate.text();
  const approval = await http('/oauth/continue', { cookie, form: { pending: hidden(html, 'pending'), csrf: hidden(html, 'csrf') } }); assert.equal(approval.status, 302);
  const code = new URL(approval.headers.get('location')).searchParams.get('code'); assert.ok(code);
  const exchange = await http('/token', { form: { grant_type: 'authorization_code', client_id: client.client_id, code, code_verifier: verifier, redirect_uri: callback } });
  assert.equal(exchange.status, 200); return { ...await exchange.json(), client_id: client.client_id };
}

test('cold installed package runs CLI server, persists pairing/OAuth and restores complete stopped state', { timeout: 120000, skip: !installed ? 'Set MCP_V12_COLD_PACKAGE_ROOT to the isolated npm tarball installation' : false }, async t => {
  const packageRoot = path.resolve(installed), entry = path.join(packageRoot, 'dist', 'mcp-device.js');
  const packageInfo = JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(path.join(packageRoot, 'package.json'), 'utf8'));
  assert.equal(packageInfo.name, '@hcu-lab.me/mcp-device'); assert.equal(packageInfo.version, '1.0.12');
  const palaceEntry = path.join(packageRoot,'dist','plugins','palace','cli.js');
  const bundledSkill = await fs.readFile(path.join(packageRoot,'skills','palace-cli','SKILL.md'),'utf8');
  assert.match(bundledSkill,/name: palace-cli/);

  const fixture = await fs.mkdtemp(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'v12-cold-')), home = path.join(fixture, 'home');

  await fs.writeFile(path.join(home, '.pi', 'agent', 'sessions', 'cold.jsonl'), JSON.stringify({ type: 'message', message: { role: 'toolResult', toolName: 'test', content: [{ type: 'text', text: 'Decision: cold package fixture records durable isolated acceptance evidence.' }] } }) + '\n');
  const preload = path.join(fixture, 'isolate-home.mjs');
  await fs.writeFile(preload, `import os from 'node:os';import{syncBuiltinESMExports}from'node:module';import{Server}from'node:net';os.homedir=()=>${JSON.stringify(home)};syncBuiltinESMExports();const listen=Server.prototype.listen;Server.prototype.listen=function(...a){if(a[0]!==8104||a[1]!=='127.0.0.1')throw new Error('TEST_LISTENER_OUTSIDE_8104');return listen.apply(this,a)};`);

  const password = randomBytes(24).toString('hex'), email = 'cold-admin@example.test';

  async function start(root) {
    const child = fork(entry, ['server', '--runtime-dir', root, '--host', '127.0.0.1', '--port', '8104', '--legacy-knowledge-mcp'], { execPath: process.execPath, execArgv: ['--import', pathToFileURL(preload).href], cwd: home, env: { ...env, MCP_DEVICE_CONFIG_DIR: root }, stdio: ['ignore', 'pipe', 'pipe', 'ipc'], windowsHide: true });
    let buffer = '', stderr = '', pairing;
    const ready = new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      const timer = setTimeout(() => reject(new Error('CLI startup timed out: ' + stderr)), 20000);
      child.stderr.on('data', chunk => { stderr = (stderr + chunk).slice(-2000); });
      child.stdout.on('data', chunk => { buffer += chunk; let end; while ((end = buffer.indexOf('\n')) !== -1) { const line = buffer.slice(0, end); buffer = buffer.slice(end + 1); try { const event = JSON.parse(line); if (event.event === 'device_pairing_required') pairing = event.pairing; if (event.event === 'server_ready') { clearTimeout(timer); resolve(event); } } catch {} } });
      child.once('error', error => { clearTimeout(timer); reject(error); });
      child.once('exit', code => { clearTimeout(timer); reject(new Error('CLI exited ' + code + ': ' + stderr)); });
    });
    const closed = new Promise(resolve => child.once('exit', code => resolve(code)));
    running = { child, closed };
    const event = await ready;
    assert.equal(event.version, '1.0.12'); assert.equal(event.local_runtime, 'shared-device-core');
    return { ...event, pairing };
  }
  async function stop() {
    if (!running) return;
    const active = running; running = undefined;
    if (active.child.exitCode !== null) return;
    active.child.send({ type: 'mcp-device:shutdown' });
    let timer;
    try { assert.equal(await Promise.race([active.closed, new Promise((_, reject) => { timer = setTimeout(() => { active.child.kill(); reject(new Error('CLI did not stop gracefully')); }, 15000); })]), 0); }
    finally { clearTimeout(timer); }
  }
  t.after(async () => { await stop(); await fs.rm(fixture, { recursive: true, force: true, maxRetries: 4, retryDelay: 100 }); });
  const admin = JSON.parse((await cli(['admin', '--runtime-dir', runtimeRoot, 'account', 'create-admin', email], { HCU_ADMIN_PASSWORD: password })).stdout);
  assert.equal(admin.ok, true);
  const invited = JSON.parse((await cli(['admin', '--runtime-dir', runtimeRoot, 'invite', 'create', '1'])).stdout);
  assert.equal(invited.ok, true); assert.equal(invited.codes.length, 1);
  const initial = await start(runtimeRoot);
  const livePalace = JSON.parse((await exec(process.execPath,[palaceEntry,'--runtime-dir',runtimeRoot,'status'],{cwd:home,env,timeout:20000})).stdout);
  assert.equal(livePalace.ok,true); assert.equal(livePalace.mode,'read-only');
  assert.ok(livePalace.totals.cards >= 0);
  assert.ok(initial.pairing?.user_code); assert.equal('device_code' in initial.pairing, false);
  const anonymous = await http('/mcp', { headers: { accept: 'application/json, text/event-stream' }, json: { jsonrpc: '2.0', id: 1, method: 'tools/list', params: {} } }); assert.equal(anonymous.status, 401);
  const login = await http('/login', { form: { identity: email, password } }); assert.equal(login.status, 302);

  const cookie = signup.headers.get('set-cookie').split(';')[0];
  assert.equal((await http('/operator', { cookie })).status, 403);
  const verify = await http('/device/verify?user_code=' + encodeURIComponent(initial.pairing.user_code), { cookie });
  assert.equal(verify.status, 200);
  assert.match(await verify.text(), /localhost/);
  const claimed = await http('/device/verify', { cookie, form: { user_code: initial.pairing.user_code } }); assert.equal(claimed.status, 200);
  const tokens = await authorize(cookie);
  const initialized = await rpc(tokens.access_token, 'initialize', { protocolVersion: '2025-11-25', capabilities: {}, clientInfo: { name: 'cold-package-acceptance', version: '1.0.12' } });
  assert.ok(initialized.result?.serverInfo);
  let capabilities;
  for (let tries = 0; tries < 60; tries++) {
    capabilities = await rpc(tokens.access_token, 'tools/call', { name: 'capability.list', arguments: { device_id: initial.device_id } });
    if (capabilities.result?.structuredContent?.capabilities?.includes('knowledge.search')) break;

  }
  assert.ok(capabilities.result.structuredContent.capabilities.includes('knowledge.search'));

  const filename = path.join(home, 'cold-file.txt');

===== FILE scripts/v12/palace-package-closure.mjs sha256=78431132f63c7a50f2a15001a8f950e79020f395cac3d4e5e229b1e67abce4d5 =====
// Build-time verification only. Reuse TypeScript's parser/resolver; no runtime dependency is added.
import fs from 'node:fs/promises';
import path from 'node:path';
import { isBuiltin } from 'node:module';
import { fileURLToPath, pathToFileURL } from 'node:url';
import ts from 'typescript';

const compilerOptions={module:ts.ModuleKind.NodeNext,moduleResolution:ts.ModuleResolutionKind.NodeNext};
const builtins={has:isBuiltin}; // Includes prefix-only built-ins such as node:sqlite.
const within=(root,file)=>{const relative=path.relative(root,file);return relative!==''&&!relative.startsWith('..'+path.sep)&&relative!=='..'&&!path.isAbsolute(relative);};
async function filesUnder(dir){
  const result=[];
  for(const entry of await fs.readdir(dir,{withFileTypes:true})){
    const file=path.join(dir,entry.name);
    if(entry.isSymbolicLink())throw new Error('PALACE_PACKAGE_SYMLINK: '+file);
    if(entry.isDirectory())result.push(...await filesUnder(file));else if(entry.isFile())result.push(file);
  }
  return result;
}
function importsOf(filename,text){
  const source=ts.createSourceFile(filename,text,ts.ScriptTarget.Latest,true,filename.endsWith('.cjs')||filename.endsWith('.js')?ts.ScriptKind.JS:ts.ScriptKind.TS);
  const imports=[],dynamic=[];
  const add=node=>{if(node&&ts.isStringLiteralLike(node))imports.push(node.text);};
  const visit=node=>{
    if(ts.isImportDeclaration(node)||ts.isExportDeclaration(node))add(node.moduleSpecifier);
    if(ts.isImportEqualsDeclaration(node)&&ts.isExternalModuleReference(node.moduleReference))add(node.moduleReference.expression);
    if(ts.isImportTypeNode(node)&&ts.isLiteralTypeNode(node.argument))add(node.argument.literal);
    if(ts.isCallExpression(node)&&(node.expression.kind===ts.SyntaxKind.ImportKeyword||(ts.isIdentifier(node.expression)&&node.expression.text==='require'))){
      if(node.arguments.length===1&&ts.isStringLiteralLike(node.arguments[0]))add(node.arguments[0]);
      else dynamic.push({line:source.getLineAndCharacterOfPosition(node.getStart(source)).line+1,expression:node.getText(source)});
    }
    ts.forEachChild(node,visit);
  };
  visit(source);return {imports:[...new Set(imports)],dynamic};
}

export async function completeDeclarationClosure(canonicalRoot,packageRoot){
  canonicalRoot=path.resolve(canonicalRoot);packageRoot=path.resolve(packageRoot);
  const pending=(await filesUnder(path.join(packageRoot,'dist'))).filter(file=>file.endsWith('.d.ts'));
  const seen=new Set(),added=[];
  while(pending.length){
    const packaged=pending.pop();if(seen.has(packaged))continue;seen.add(packaged);
    const canonical=path.join(canonicalRoot,path.relative(packageRoot,packaged));
    for(const specifier of importsOf(packaged,await fs.readFile(packaged,'utf8')).imports){
      if(builtins.has(specifier))continue;
      if(!specifier.startsWith('.'))throw new Error('PALACE_DECLARATION_EXTERNAL_DEPENDENCY: '+specifier);
      const resolved=ts.resolveModuleName(specifier,canonical,compilerOptions,ts.sys).resolvedModule;
      if(!resolved||!resolved.resolvedFileName.endsWith('.d.ts')||!within(path.join(canonicalRoot,'dist'),resolved.resolvedFileName))
        throw new Error('PALACE_DECLARATION_CLOSURE_FAILED: '+canonical+' -> '+specifier);
      const destination=path.join(packageRoot,path.relative(canonicalRoot,resolved.resolvedFileName));
      if(!await fs.access(destination).then(()=>true).catch(()=>false)){
        await fs.mkdir(path.dirname(destination),{recursive:true});await fs.copyFile(resolved.resolvedFileName,destination);
        added.push(path.relative(packageRoot,destination).split(path.sep).join('/'));
      }
      pending.push(destination);
    }
  }
  return {checked:seen.size,added};
}

export async function verifyPackageImports(packageRoot){
  packageRoot=path.resolve(packageRoot);const unresolved=[],dynamicImports=[];
  const files=(await filesUnder(packageRoot)).filter(file=>/\.(?:c?js|mjs|d\.ts)$/.test(file));
  for(const file of files){
    const relative=path.relative(packageRoot,file).split(path.sep).join('/');
    const parsed=importsOf(file,await fs.readFile(file,'utf8'));
    for(const specifier of parsed.imports){
      if(builtins.has(specifier))continue;
      if(!specifier.startsWith('.')){unresolved.push({file:relative,specifier,reason:'undeclared-external-import'});continue;}
      const resolved=file.endsWith('.d.ts')
        ?ts.resolveModuleName(specifier,file,compilerOptions,ts.sys).resolvedModule?.resolvedFileName
        :fileURLToPath(new URL(specifier,pathToFileURL(file)));
      if(!resolved||!within(packageRoot,resolved))unresolved.push({file:relative,specifier,reason:'import-escapes-package'});
      else if(!await fs.stat(resolved).then(stat=>stat.isFile()).catch(()=>false))unresolved.push({file:relative,specifier,reason:'missing-relative-import'});
    }
    for(const item of parsed.dynamic){
      // These are reviewed, intentional configuration boundaries, not hidden module-graph dependencies.
      const allowed=(relative==='dist/plugins/palace/plugin.js'&&/import\(pathToFileURL\(settings\.validationModule\)\.href\)/.test(item.expression))
        ||(relative==='scripts/install-palace-skill.cjs'&&/import\(pathToFileURL\(manager\)\.href\)/.test(item.expression));
      dynamicImports.push({file:relative,...item,contract:allowed?'reviewed-configured-path':'unreviewed'});
      if(!allowed)unresolved.push({file:relative,specifier:item.expression,reason:'unreviewed-dynamic-import'});
    }
  }
  return {ok:unresolved.length===0,filesChecked:files.length,unresolved,dynamicImports};
}

===== FILE src/platform/coordination-lock.ts sha256=c2da0700e8488bf31fdaa9545aebfa6761a731df5dd14b6bb1f884ef5414805b =====
import fs, {type FileHandle} from 'node:fs/promises';
import path from 'node:path';
import {randomUUID} from 'node:crypto';
import {processBirth,processNamespace,sameProcessNamespace,type ProcessNamespace} from './process-identity.js';

export interface CoordinationLockBody {pid:number;token:string;createdAt?:number;processBirth?:string;namespace?:ProcessNamespace}
export interface CoordinationObservation {body?:CoordinationLockBody;ageMs:number;mtimeMs:number;size:number}
export interface CoordinationCodes {busy:string;active:string;recoveryRequired:string}
export interface CoordinationOptions {
  nonBlocking?:boolean;breakLock?:boolean;attempts?:number;staleMs?:number;codes:CoordinationCodes;
  messagePrefix?:string;retainAbandoned?:boolean;
}
const sleep=(ms:number)=>new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,ms));
const failure=(code:string,message:string,details:Record<string,unknown>={})=>Object.assign(new Error(message),{code,...details});

export async function coordinationObservation(filename:string):Promise<CoordinationObservation|null>{
  let stat;try{stat=await fs.lstat(filename);}catch(error){if((error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code==='ENOENT')return null;throw error;}
  if(!stat.isFile()||stat.isSymbolicLink())throw failure('COORDINATION_PATH_UNSAFE','Coordination path is not an ordinary file.',{path:filename});
  let body:CoordinationLockBody|undefined;
  if(stat.size<=4096){
    try{
      const parsed=JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(filename,'utf8'));
      if(Number.isSafeInteger(parsed.pid)&&parsed.pid>0&&typeof parsed.token==='string'&&parsed.token){
        body={pid:parsed.pid,token:parsed.token,...(Number.isFinite(parsed.createdAt)?{createdAt:parsed.createdAt}:{}),
          ...(typeof parsed.processBirth==='string'&&parsed.processBirth.length<=256?{processBirth:parsed.processBirth}:{}),
          ...(parsed.namespace&&typeof parsed.namespace.platform==='string'&&typeof parsed.namespace.hostname==='string'?{namespace:parsed.namespace as ProcessNamespace}:{})};
      }
    }catch{}
  }
  return {body,ageMs:Math.max(0,Date.now()-stat.mtimeMs),mtimeMs:stat.mtimeMs,size:stat.size};
}
export function processAppearsDead(pid:number):boolean{
  try{process.kill(pid,0);return false;}catch(error){return (error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code==='ESRCH';}
}
export async function coordinationOwnerAbandoned(body:CoordinationLockBody):Promise<boolean>{
  if(!await sameProcessNamespace(body.namespace))return false;
  if(processAppearsDead(body.pid))return true;
  if(!body.processBirth)return false;
  const actual=await processBirth(body.pid);
  return actual!==null&&actual!==body.processBirth;
}
export async function createCoordinationLock(filename:string,token=randomUUID()):Promise<{handle:FileHandle;token:string}>{
  await fs.mkdir(path.dirname(filename),{recursive:true});
  const [birth,namespace]=await Promise.all([processBirth(process.pid),processNamespace()]);
  const body=JSON.stringify({pid:process.pid,token,createdAt:Date.now(),...(birth?{processBirth:birth}:{}),...(namespace?{namespace}:{})})+'\n';
  const claim=filename+'.claim-'+token;
  let handle:FileHandle|undefined,linked=false,direct=false;
  try{
    handle=await fs.open(claim,'wx',0o600);
    await handle.writeFile(body);await handle.sync();
    try{await fs.link(claim,filename);linked=true;await fs.unlink(claim);return {handle,token};}
    catch(error){
      const code=(error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code;
      if(!['EPERM','EXDEV','ENOTSUP','EOPNOTSUPP','EINVAL'].includes(String(code)))throw error;
      await handle.close();handle=undefined;await fs.rm(claim,{force:true});
      handle=await fs.open(filename,'wx',0o600);direct=true;
      try{await handle.writeFile(body);await handle.sync();return {handle,token};}
      catch(writeError){await handle.close();handle=undefined;await fs.unlink(filename).catch(()=>{});throw writeError;}
    }
  }catch(error){
    if(handle)await handle.close().catch(()=>{});
    if(linked){const current=await coordinationObservation(filename).catch(()=>null);if(current?.body?.token===token)await fs.unlink(filename).catch(()=>{});}
    if(direct)await fs.unlink(filename).catch(()=>{});
    await fs.rm(claim,{force:true}).catch(()=>{});
    throw error;
  }
}
export async function releaseCoordinationLock(lock:{handle:FileHandle;token:string},filename:string):Promise<void>{
  await lock.handle.close();
  try{
    const current=await coordinationObservation(filename);
    if(current?.body?.token===lock.token)await fs.unlink(filename);
  }catch(error){if((error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code!=='ENOENT')throw error;}
}
async function recoverObserved(lockPath:string,recoveryPath:string,observed:CoordinationObservation,options:CoordinationOptions,allowIncomplete:boolean):Promise<boolean>{
  const recoveryToken=randomUUID();let recovery:{handle:FileHandle;token:string}|undefined;
  try{recovery=await createCoordinationLock(recoveryPath,recoveryToken);}
  catch(error){if((error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code==='EEXIST')return false;throw error;}
  try{
    const current=await coordinationObservation(lockPath);if(!current)return true;
    const same=current.mtimeMs===observed.mtimeMs&&current.size===observed.size
      &&current.body?.token===observed.body?.token&&current.body?.pid===observed.body?.pid&&current.body?.processBirth===observed.body?.processBirth;
    if(!same)return false;
    if(current.body){
      if(!await coordinationOwnerAbandoned(current.body))return false;
    }else if(!allowIncomplete||current.ageMs<(options.staleMs??5000))return false;
    const abandoned=lockPath+'.abandoned-'+randomUUID();
    if(options.retainAbandoned!==false)await fs.rename(lockPath,abandoned);else await fs.unlink(lockPath);
    return true;
  }finally{await releaseCoordinationLock(recovery,recoveryPath);}
}
export async function repairCoordinationLock(lockPath:string,options:CoordinationOptions):Promise<{recovered:boolean;reason?:string}>{
  const observed=await coordinationObservation(lockPath);if(!observed)return {recovered:false,reason:'absent'};
  if(observed.body&&!await coordinationOwnerAbandoned(observed.body))throw failure(options.codes.active,(options.messagePrefix??'Coordination')+' owner is live or cannot be proved abandoned.',{path:lockPath,pid:observed.body.pid});
  if(!observed.body&&observed.ageMs<(options.staleMs??5000))throw failure(options.codes.active,(options.messagePrefix??'Coordination')+' lock may still be initializing.',{path:lockPath});
  const ok=await recoverObserved(lockPath,lockPath+'.recovery',observed,options,true);
  if(!ok)throw failure(options.codes.recoveryRequired,(options.messagePrefix??'Coordination')+' ownership changed during recovery.',{path:lockPath});
  return {recovered:true};
}
export async function withCoordinationLock<T>(lockPath:string,work:()=>Promise<T>,options:CoordinationOptions):Promise<T>{
  const recoveryPath=lockPath+'.recovery';
  if(options.breakLock)await repairCoordinationLock(lockPath,options).catch(error=>{if((error as any).reason==='absent')return;throw error;});
  let lock:{handle:FileHandle;token:string}|undefined;
  const attempts=options.nonBlocking?1:(options.attempts??200);
  for(let attempt=0;attempt<attempts;attempt++){
    try{lock=await createCoordinationLock(lockPath);break;}
    catch(error){
      if((error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code!=='EEXIST')throw error;
      const prior=await coordinationObservation(lockPath);if(!prior)continue;
      if(prior.body&&await coordinationOwnerAbandoned(prior.body)){
        if(await recoverObserved(lockPath,recoveryPath,prior,options,false))continue;
      }
      if(!prior.body&&prior.ageMs>=(options.staleMs??5000))
        throw failure(options.codes.recoveryRequired,(options.messagePrefix??'Coordination')+' has an incomplete old lock; explicit recovery is required.',{path:lockPath});
      if(options.nonBlocking)throw failure(options.codes.busy,(options.messagePrefix??'Coordination')+' is busy.',{path:lockPath});
      await sleep(10);
    }
  }
  if(!lock)throw failure(options.codes.busy,(options.messagePrefix??'Coordination')+' is busy or uninspectable.',{path:lockPath});
  try{return await work();}finally{await releaseCoordinationLock(lock,lockPath);}
}

===== FILE src/platform/process-identity.ts sha256=06602c6742b13b979ea06526de5d1a95cdb7d09de3d34d1b7284de433177ecf2 =====
import fs from 'node:fs/promises';
import path from 'node:path';
import os from 'node:os';
import {execFile} from 'node:child_process';
import {promisify} from 'node:util';

const exec=promisify(execFile);
let ownBirth:Promise<string|null>|undefined;
let ownNamespace:Promise<ProcessNamespace|null>|undefined;

export interface ProcessNamespace { platform:string;hostname:string;bootId?:string;pidNamespace?:string }

export async function processNamespace():Promise<ProcessNamespace|null>{
  ownNamespace??=(async()=>{
    try{
      const common={platform:process.platform,hostname:os.hostname()};
      if(process.platform==='linux'){
        const [boot,pidNamespace]=await Promise.all([
          fs.readFile('/proc/sys/kernel/random/boot_id','utf8'),
          fs.readlink('/proc/self/ns/pid')
        ]);
        if(!/^[a-f0-9-]{36}$/i.test(boot.trim())||!/^pid:\[\d+\]$/.test(pidNamespace))return null;
        return {...common,bootId:boot.trim(),pidNamespace};
      }
      return common;
    }catch{return null;}
  })();
  return ownNamespace;
}

export async function sameProcessNamespace(recorded:ProcessNamespace|undefined):Promise<boolean>{
  const current=await processNamespace();
  return !!current&&!!recorded&&current.platform===recorded.platform&&current.hostname===recorded.hostname
    &&current.bootId===recorded.bootId&&current.pidNamespace===recorded.pidNamespace;
}

export async function processBirth(pid:number):Promise<string|null>{
  if(!Number.isSafeInteger(pid)||pid<1)return null;
  if(pid===process.pid){ownBirth??=observe(pid);return ownBirth;}
  return observe(pid);
}

async function observe(pid:number):Promise<string|null>{
  try{
    if(process.platform==='linux'){
      const [stat,boot]=await Promise.all([
        fs.readFile(`/proc/${pid}/stat`,'utf8'),
        fs.readFile('/proc/sys/kernel/random/boot_id','utf8')
      ]);
      const end=stat.lastIndexOf(')'),fields=stat.slice(end+2).trim().split(/\s+/),start=fields[19];
      if(end<0||!/^\d+$/.test(start??'')||!/^[a-f0-9-]{36}$/i.test(boot.trim()))return null;
      return 'linux:'+boot.trim()+':'+start;
    }
    if(process.platform==='win32'){
      const powershell=path.join(process.env.SystemRoot??'C:\\Windows','System32','WindowsPowerShell','v1.0','powershell.exe');
      const command=`[Console]::Write((Get-Process -Id ${pid} -ErrorAction Stop).StartTime.ToFileTimeUtc().ToString([Globalization.CultureInfo]::InvariantCulture))`;
      const {stdout}=await exec(powershell,['-NoLogo','-NoProfile','-NonInteractive','-Command',command],{
        encoding:'utf8',windowsHide:true,timeout:3000,maxBuffer:4096
      });
      const ticks=stdout.trim();
      return /^\d{10,}$/.test(ticks)?'win32:'+ticks:null;
    }
  }catch{ /* unknown identity never authorizes takeover */ }
  return null;
}

interface OwnerRecord { packageName:string;version:string;root:string;source:string;files:Record<string,string>;updatedAt:number;missingSince?:number;missingObservations?:number }
interface TargetRecord { selectedOwner:string;version:string;files:Record<string,string>;linkTarget?:string }
interface PendingIntent { id:string;target:string;stage:string;aside:string;selectedOwner:string;version:string;files:Record<string,string>;hadPrior?:boolean;priorFiles?:Record<string,string>;adopted?:boolean }
interface Ledger { version:1;revision:number;owners:Record<string,OwnerRecord>;targets:Record<string,TargetRecord>;pending?:PendingIntent;retained?:Record<string,string>;retainedFiles?:Record<string,Record<string,string>>;removedOwners?:Record<string,true>;offers?:Record<string,string>;optOutPackages?:Record<string,true>;publishFailures?:Record<string,{code:string;message:string;at:number}> }
export interface SkillManagerOptions { home?:string;force?:boolean;removeCurrentOwner?:boolean;removeAll?:boolean;install?:boolean;repair?:boolean;packageRoot?:string;nonBlocking?:boolean;breakLock?:boolean;abandonPending?:boolean;adoptTarget?:'agents'|'pi';forgetOwner?:string;prune?:boolean }

const failure=(code:string,message=code,details:Record<string,unknown>={})=>Object.assign(new Error(message),{code,...details});

const sleep=(ms:number)=>new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,ms));
const hash=(value:Buffer|string)=>createHash('sha256').update(value).digest('hex');
const slash=(value:string)=>value.split(path.sep).join('/');
const exists=async(filename:string)=>{try{await fs.lstat(filename);return true;}catch(error){if((error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code==='ENOENT')return false;throw error;}};
function compareVersions(a:string,b:string):number {
  // Follow npm/node-semver compareMain/comparePre precedence, not locale ordering.
  // In particular, release > prerelease, numeric identifiers compare numerically,
  // and build metadata never affects precedence.
  const parse=(value:string)=>{
    const match=value.length<=256&&value.match(/^(0|[1-9]\d*)\.(0|[1-9]\d*)\.(0|[1-9]\d*)(?:-([0-9A-Za-z-]+(?:\.[0-9A-Za-z-]+)*))?(?:\+[0-9A-Za-z-]+(?:\.[0-9A-Za-z-]+)*)?$/);
    if(!match)throw failure('PALACE_SKILL_VERSION_INVALID');
    const pre=match[4]?.split('.')??[];
    if(pre.some(id=>/^\d+$/.test(id)&&id.length>1&&id.startsWith('0')))throw failure('PALACE_SKILL_VERSION_INVALID');
    return {main:match.slice(1,4).map(BigInt),pre};
  };
  const av=parse(a),bv=parse(b);
  for(let i=0;i<3;i++){if(av.main[i]!==bv.main[i])return av.main[i]>bv.main[i]?1:-1;}
  if(!av.pre.length||!bv.pre.length)return av.pre.length?-1:bv.pre.length?1:0;
  for(let i=0;i<Math.max(av.pre.length,bv.pre.length);i++){
    const x=av.pre[i],y=bv.pre[i];if(x===y)continue;
    if(x===undefined)return -1;if(y===undefined)return 1;
    const xn=/^\d+$/.test(x),yn=/^\d+$/.test(y);
    if(xn&&yn)return BigInt(x)>BigInt(y)?1:-1;
    if(xn!==yn)return xn?-1:1;
    return x>y?1:-1;
  }
  return 0;
}
async function fileMap(root:string,options:{ignoreMarker?:boolean}={}):Promise<Record<string,string>> {
  const rootStat=await fs.lstat(root);
  if(!rootStat.isDirectory()||rootStat.isSymbolicLink())throw failure('PALACE_SKILL_UNSAFE_TREE');
  const result:Record<string,string>=Object.create(null);let entries=0,bytes=0;
  async function walk(dir:string):Promise<void>{
    if(path.relative(root,dir).split(path.sep).length>32)throw failure('PALACE_SKILL_SCAN_LIMIT');
    const names=(await fs.readdir(dir)).sort();
    // Empty user directories are user content too; the old file-only map discarded them.
    if(dir!==root&&!names.length)result[slash(path.relative(root,dir))+'/']='directory';
    for(const name of names){
      if(options.ignoreMarker&&dir===root&&name==='.mcp-device-palace-owned')continue;
      if(++entries>4096)throw failure('PALACE_SKILL_SCAN_LIMIT');
      const filename=path.join(dir,name),stat=await fs.lstat(filename);
      if(stat.isSymbolicLink())throw Object.assign(new Error('SKILL_SYMLINK_UNSAFE: '+filename),{code:'SKILL_SYMLINK_UNSAFE'});
      if(stat.isDirectory())await walk(filename);
      else if(stat.isFile()){bytes+=stat.size;if(bytes>32*1024*1024)throw failure('PALACE_SKILL_SCAN_LIMIT');result[slash(path.relative(root,filename))]=hash(await fs.readFile(filename));}
      else throw Object.assign(new Error('SKILL_ENTRY_UNSUPPORTED: '+filename),{code:'SKILL_ENTRY_UNSUPPORTED'});
    }
  }
  await walk(root);return result;
}
const sameMap=(a:Record<string,string>,b:Record<string,string>)=>{
  const ak=Object.keys(a).sort(),bk=Object.keys(b).sort();return ak.length===bk.length&&ak.every((key,index)=>key===bk[index]&&a[key]===b[key]);
};
async function readJson<T>(filename:string,fallback:T):Promise<T>{
  try{
    const stat=await fs.stat(filename);if(!stat.isFile()||stat.size>65536)throw failure('PALACE_SKILL_METADATA_LIMIT','Package metadata exceeds the bounded inspection limit.',{path:filename});
    return JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(filename,'utf8')) as T;
  }catch(error){if((error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code==='ENOENT')return fallback;throw error;}
}
async function renameManaged(source:string,target:string):Promise<void>{
  for(let attempt=0;;attempt++){
    try{await fs.rename(source,target);return;}
    catch(error){
      if(process.platform!=='win32'||attempt>=7||!['EPERM','EBUSY','EACCES'].includes(String((error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code)))throw error;
      await sleep(Math.min(200,20*2**attempt));
    }
  }
}
async function writeJsonAtomic(filename:string,value:unknown):Promise<void>{
  const text=JSON.stringify(value,null,2)+'\n';
  if(Buffer.byteLength(text)>2*1024*1024)throw failure('PALACE_SKILL_LEDGER_LIMIT','Ownership history reached its bounded limit. Inspect retained content and use palace skill prune for verified pristine backups before another update.');
  await fs.mkdir(path.dirname(filename),{recursive:true});const temporary=filename+'.'+randomUUID()+'.tmp';
  try{
    const handle=await fs.open(temporary,'wx',0o600);
    try{await handle.writeFile(text);await handle.sync();}finally{await handle.close();}
    await renameManaged(temporary,filename);
    if(process.platform!=='win32'){
      const directory=await fs.open(path.dirname(filename),'r');
      try{await directory.sync();}finally{await directory.close();}
    }
  }finally{await fs.rm(temporary,{force:true});}
}
